### [1] [Superconductivity in the Metallic Wigner Crystals of Rhombohedral Graphene: Possible Realization of an Electronic Supersolid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10117)
*Félix Desrochers,Mark R. Hirsbrunner,Yong Baek Kim,Ashvin Vishwanath*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究菱面体多层石墨烯中金属维格纳晶体(MWC)中集体激发介导的超导性,提出并验证了"超导维格纳晶体"(电子超固态)这一电子类比场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验在菱面体多层石墨烯中观测到金属维格纳晶体(MWC),其中自发破缺的平移对称性与可动载流子共存;其与时间反演对称性破缺超导的邻近性,暗示电子晶体的集体激发可能介导残余巡游载流子间的配对,形成超导维格纳晶体。作者旨在通过理论检验这一可能性。

Method: 作者采用具有自旋和谷极化的电子掺杂菱面体多层石墨烯的现实模型(其低对称性消除了常规的Cooper不稳定性),依次使用自洽及含时Hartree-Fock理论追踪自掺杂份额的演化并刻画MWC的集体模谱,随后进行多带线性化Eliashberg配对分析。

Result: 随密度增加,MWC出现不稳定性,由一条弱色散的未占据能带逼近化学势所触发。在稳定的MWC深处配对仍很弱;但在接近该不稳定性时,识别出一个由带间电子-空穴激发组成的发生软化的集体模。该模在实验相关温度下驱动配对。主导的配对不稳定性位于有限动量,并将费米面态与起始(incipient)能带中的态结合起来,使费米面或单带配对分析不再适用。

Conclusion: 结果确立了金属维格纳晶体中集体模介导的超导性是菱面体石墨烯的一种可行机制,并激励实验在其超导相中寻找共存的电子电荷序。

Abstract: Recent experiments on rhombohedral multilayer graphene have reported metallic Wigner crystals (MWCs), in which spontaneously broken translational symmetry coexists with mobile charge carriers. Their proximity to time-reversal-symmetry-breaking superconductivity raises the intriguing possibility that collective excitations of the electron crystal mediate pairing among the residual itinerant carriers, giving rise to a superconducting Wigner crystal---an electronic analog of a supersolid. Here we investigate this possibility using a realistic model of electron-doped rhombohedral multilayer graphene with spin and valley polarization, whose low symmetry removes the usual Cooper instability. We use self-consistent and time-dependent Hartree-Fock theory to map the evolution of the self-doping fraction and characterize the collective-mode spectrum of the MWCs, followed by a multiband linearized Eliashberg analysis of pairing. Upon increasing density, we find an instability of the MWC, triggered by a weakly dispersing unoccupied band approaching the chemical potential. Pairing remains weak deep in the stable MWC. However, on approaching the instability, we identify a collective mode composed of interband electron-hole excitations that softens. We show that this mode drives pairing at experimentally relevant temperatures. The leading pairing instability is at finite momentum and combines Fermi surface states with states in the incipient band, making a Fermi surface or single-band pairing analysis inadequate. Our results establish collective-mode-mediated superconductivity in MWCs as a viable scenario for rhombohedral graphene and motivate experimental searches for coexisting electronic charge order in its superconducting phases.

</details>


### [2] [Magnetic behavior of the $5d^1$ Re-based double perovskite Sr$_2$ZnReO$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.04071)
*Muhammad Maikudi Isah,Biswajit Dalal,Xun Kang,Dario Fiore Mosca,Ifeanyi John Onuorah,Valerio Scagnoli,Pietro Bonfà,Roberto De Renzi,Alexei A. Belik,Cesare Franchini,Kazunari Yamaura,Samuele Sanna*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过同步辐射XRD、磁化率、μSR测量与DFT计算，确定Sr₂ZnReO₆（5d¹有序双钙钛矿）在低温下形成单斜P2₁/n结构，呈现倾斜反铁磁基态，具有极小的有序磁矩（≲0.2 μB）与异常高的矫顽场（2 K时23 kOe），揭示了自旋-轨道耦合与轨道序在5d¹双钙钛矿磁性中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属离子中自旋-轨道耦合、交换作用与阳离子有序之间的微妙相互作用可产生奇异磁态，而Re基5d¹双钙钛矿的基态磁序此前尚不明确，需要结合实验与计算加以澄清，并阐明极小磁矩与巨大矫顽力共存的机理。

Method: 综合采用同步辐射X射线衍射（XRD）确定低温晶体结构；磁化率与M-H回线测量磁性转变与磁滞行为；零场μSR探测内部场并分析静态矩；并结合DFT以及force-theorem下的Hubbard-I计算确定磁基态与自旋-轨道耦合效应。

Result: XRD表明低温为单斜P2₁/n结构；磁化率显示~13 K以下存在磁性转变，M-H回线呈铁磁样磁滞且2 K矫顽场高达23 kOe；零场μSR在T_M≈12 K以下检测到静态且空间无序的内场，与倾斜反铁磁基态一致；高温有效磁矩仅约0.76 μB，静态矩极小（≲0.2 μB），表明自旋-轨道耦合起决定性作用。

Conclusion: 研究明确确定了Sr₂ZnReO₆的倾斜反铁磁序，证明极小有序磁矩与异常大矫顽力可以共存，凸显自旋-轨道耦合与轨道序的重要性，为理解5d¹双钙钛矿磁性提供了新见解。

Abstract: The subtle interplay between spin-orbit coupling, exchange interactions, and cation ordering can lead to exotic magnetic states in transition-metal ions. We report a comprehensive study of the Re-based (5$d^1$) ordered double perovskite oxide Sr$_2$ZnReO$_6$ combining synchrotron x-ray diffraction (XRD), magnetic susceptibility, muon spin relaxation ($μ$SR) measurements, and density functional theory (DFT) calculations. XRD reveals that Sr$_2$ZnReO$_6$ crystallizes in the monoclinic structure (space group $P2_1/n$) at low temperature. Magnetic susceptibility data indicate a transition below $\sim$13 K, with $M$--$H$ loops showing ferromagnetic-like hysteresis and an unusually high coercive field of 23 kOe at 2 K. Zero-field $μ$SR measurements detect static and spatially disordered internal fields below $T_M \simeq $ 12 K, consistent with a canted antiferromagnetic ground state determined by detailed DFT and force-theorem in Hubbard-I calculations. The reduced high-temperature effective moment ($\sim0.76~μ_B$) and very small static moment ($\lesssim 0.2~μ_B$) derived from $μ$SR analysis and local-field simulations indicate a decisive role of spin-orbit coupling. Through a combined experimental and computational approach we unambiguously determine the canted antiferromagnetic order in Sr$_2$ZnReO$_6$, showing that a very small ordered moment coexists with an exceptionally large coercivity. These results underscore the crucial role of spin-orbit coupling and orbital ordering, providing new insights into magnetism in 5$d^1$ double perovskites.

</details>


### [3] [Energy-resolved current response and the crossover between two approximately linear-in-$T$ resistivity regimes in the infinite-$U$ Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10124)
*Shuo Liu,Aman Kumar,Yuhao Ma,Philip W. Phillips,Hitesh J. Changlani,B. Andrei Bernevig*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过精确对角化研究二维无限U哈伯德模型（单空穴掺杂，16与18格点方形团簇）的电荷输运，证明恒定的dc f_x函数是严格线性电阻率ρ_x(T)=αT的充要条件，并发现数值外推的dc f_x在谱内部近似平坦、在谱边缘剧烈变化，对应电阻率在高温与低温近似线性区间之间的渐进转变，并伴随一个宽比热峰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维无限U哈伯德模型中的电荷输运机制，特别是厘清线性电阻率ρ_x(T)=αT出现的严格条件，以及电阻率在降温过程中偏离高温线性的行为来源。

Method: 利用全精确对角化（ED）处理N=16和18格点的二维方形团簇（单掺杂空穴），对能级简并进行显式处理，证明恒定dc f_x函数（定义为连接给定能量本征态与近邻能量本征态的电流矩阵元平方和、对简并初态取平均）是严格线性电阻率的充要条件；随后对dc f_x进行数值外推，并计算电阻率与比热的温度依赖。

Result: 数值外推的dc f_x并非严格恒定，但在多体谱内部较宽区域内近似平坦，并在谱边缘尺度ε_{f_x}内剧烈变化。电阻率在T/t≥0.05范围内呈现高温与低温近似线性区间之间的渐进转变，N=16和18的转变温度T_cr/t分别约为0.414和0.467；对应宽比热峰分别出现在T/t≈0.70和0.68。

Conclusion: 恒定的dc f_x函数是严格线性电阻率的充要条件；其近似平坦（而非严格恒定）解释了电阻率在高温与低温近似线性区之间的渐进转变，且该转变与宽比热峰相关联。

Abstract: We study charge transport in the two-dimensional infinite-$U$ Hubbard model with hopping amplitude $t$ using full exact diagonalization (ED) on $N=16$- and $18$-site square clusters with a single doped hole. We first provide a proof, treating degeneracies explicitly, that a constant dc $f_x$ function, defined as the sum of the squared current matrix elements connecting eigenstates at a given energy to eigenstates of nearly the same energy, averaged over degenerate initial states, is necessary and sufficient for strictly linear-in-$T$ resistivity: $ρ_x(T)=αT$ for all $T>0$ with a constant $α$. The numerically extrapolated dc $f_x$ is not exactly constant, but it is approximately flat over a broad region in the interior of the many-body spectrum and varies strongly within a scale $\varepsilon_{f_x}$ of the spectral edges. This scale $\varepsilon_{f_x}$ is related to a characteristic temperature $T_{\mathrm{cr}}$, which quantifies the leading departure of the resistivity from high-temperature linearity upon cooling. Within the studied temperature range $T/t \geq 0.05$, our ED resistivity results reveal a gradual crossover between high- and low-temperature approximately linear-in-$T$ regimes, with characteristic crossover temperatures $T_{\mathrm{cr}}/t\approx0.414$ and $0.467$ for $N=16$ and $18$, respectively. We also find a broad specific-heat peak associated with this gradual crossover, at $T/t \approx 0.70$ and $0.68$ for $N=16$ and $18$, respectively.

</details>


### [4] [Quartet ferromagnetism in the small-exchange limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10512)
*Samuel Ogundeji,Tianxiong Han,Tyler J. Slade,Olivia Medina,Joanna M. Blawat,Filip Ronning,John Singleton,Sean M. Thomas,Alimamy F. Bangura,Wei Tian,Eleanor M. Clements,Bing Li,Adam A. Aczel,Paul C. Canfield,Benjamin G. Ueland,Rebecca Flint,Robert J. McQueeney,Aashish Sapkota*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在 RV6Sn6 稀土kagome金属中发现，当交换作用小于晶体场能标时，低能级晶体场多重态的简并决定涌现的量子相：低能准四重态允许轴向与横向偶极分量独立有序，产生倾斜铁磁相；而准三重态(自旋-1)若无强四极(向列)相互作用则无法独立偶极有序，说明四重态是偶极驱动独立有序所需的最小简并度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土磁体通常因其大角动量 J 被视为经典体系，但在交换作用小于晶体场(CEF)能标的新机制下，这一经典图像失效，低能晶体场多重态的简并度成为决定涌现量子相的关键。作者旨在揭示并利用这一机制，阐明倾斜磁基态所需的极小微观条件，并探索 CEF 调控作为实现非常规量子相的普适途径。

Method: 作者综合运用热力学测量与中子衍射，解析 DyV6Sn6 中连续的轴向与倾斜铁磁相变及其轴向 Mz、横向 Mx 偶极分量的独立有序行为；并将 HoV6Sn6(在 0.03 K 以下仍保持轴向)与 DyV6Sn6 对比，追溯差异到 Ho3+ 的非Kramers性质(CEF 解除四重态简并留下准三重态)。同时结合自旋-1 有效模型分析和 CEF 参数空间映射，判断不同简并度下偶极有序、四极有序及非磁单重态等竞争相。

Result: DyV6Sn6 展现由轴向 Mz 与横向 Mx 偶极分量独立有序所导致的连续轴向与倾斜铁磁相，其根源是罕见的准四重基态；HoV6Sn6 虽有类似倾斜磁各向异性却因非Kramers Ho3+ 的准三重基态而始终轴向。有效自旋-1 三重态在缺乏破缺旋转对称的强四极(向列)相互作用时不允许独立的偶极有序，因此四重晶体场多重态是偶极驱动独立有序所需的最小简并度。CEF 参数空间映射表明在多重简并基态驱动下，磁相、非磁单重态及可能的四极相相互竞争。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了四重晶体场多重态是偶极驱动独立有序(从而形成倾斜磁基态)所需的最小简并度，揭示出多极(四极)相互作用在低简并体系中的本质前提作用，并表明 CEF 调控可作为在更广 RT6X6 家族中实现非常规量子相的通用路径。

Abstract: Rare-earth magnets are often regarded as classical owing to their large angular momentum J, but this picture breaks down when exchange is smaller than the crystalline electric-field (CEF) energy scale, allowing the degeneracy of low-lying CEF manifolds to dictate the emergent quantum phases. Here, we uncover such a regime in the RV6Sn6 (R = rare-earth) kagome metals with strong magnetic anisotropy. Thermodynamic and neutron-diffraction measurements reveal successive axial and tilted ferromagnetic (FM) phases in DyV6Sn6, arising from independent ordering of the axial Mz and transverse Mx dipolar components enabled by a rare quasi-quartet ground state. On the other hand, HoV6Sn6 has a similar tilted magnetic anisotropy, but remains axial down to 0.03 K. This discrepancy can be traced back to the non-Kramers nature of Ho3+ where the CEF lifts the quartet degeneracy, leaving a quasi-triplet ground state. Unlike quartets or higher multiplets, we find that the effective spin-1 triplet does not allow independent dipolar ordering without strong quadrupolar (nematic) interactions that act to break rotational symmetry, revealing an essential multipolar prerequisite for tilted magnetic ground states. These results establish a quartet CEF manifold as the minimal degeneracy required for dipolar-driven independent ordering. Mapping the surrounding CEF parameter space further reveals competing magnetic, non-magnetic singlet, and potential quadrupolar phases, driven by multifold ground state degeneracies. These results establish CEF tuning as a versatile route to unconventional quantum phases across the broader RT6X6 (T = transition metal; X = Sn, Ge) family.

</details>


### [5] [On the nature of low-energy electronic states in twisted bilayer graphene: a first-principles study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10253)
*Albert Zhu,Daniel Bennett,Daniel T. Larson,Mohammed M. Al Ezzi,Efstratios Manousakis,Efthimios Kaxiras*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过无进一步近似的密度泛函理论，系统计算了魔角附近扭曲双层石墨烯（TBG）的低能电子态，构造了摩尔尺度局域 Wannier 态，建立了每个 K 谷的十带有效模型，并计算了局域态的库仑与交换积分，阐明了平带轨道间主导跃迁随扭转角的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TBG 在魔角附近的低能电子态性质在文献中多基于定性讨论，而这些态对构建少带有效模型、进而开展多体理论至关重要；因此需要精确、系统的第一性原理计算来定量确定这些低能态及相应的相互作用参数。

Method: 采用不做进一步近似的密度泛函理论（DFT），对魔角范围内的 TBG 电子态进行大规模系统计算；构造摩尔尺度的局域 Wannier 态，针对每个 K 谷建立十带有效模型；计算局域态的库仑积分与交换积分，并与已有文献采用或假设的数值进行比较；同时对十带哈密顿量和基轨道进行对称化处理。

Result: 得到的 Wannier 轨道同时包含原子尺度和摩尔尺度细节，且中心位于摩尔元胞的高对称堆叠附近；获得了平带局域态的库仑与交换积分，这些是多体模型的基本输入量；通过与既往工作的比较，给出了这些积分值的定量差异；揭示出平带轨道间主导跃迁随扭转角的演化规律。

Conclusion: 通过第一性原理计算构造的十带 Wannier 模型，为魔角 TBG 的低能电子态及多体理论提供了定量可靠的微观基础，明确了库仑/交换积分数值并解释了平带跃迁随角度的变化趋势。

Abstract: The nature of low-energy electronic states in twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) near the magic angle has been extensively discussed in the literature on qualitative grounds, since these states play a key role in the construction of effective few-band models toward a many-body theory. Here, we report extensive and systematic first-principles calculations of TBG electronic states in the magic-angle range, using density functional theory with no further approximations. We construct moiré-scale localized Wannier states for the low-energy bands and develop an effective ten-band model for each $K$ valley. The Wannier orbitals contain both atomic and moiré-scale details and are centered near the high-symmetry stackings in the moiré cell. We calculate Coulomb and exchange integrals of these localized states, quantities that are the basic ingredients in many-body models. We also compare the values of these integrals to those obtained or assumed in previous works. By symmetrizing the ten-band Hamiltonians and basis orbitals, we provide insight into how the dominant hoppings between the flat-band orbitals evolve as a function of twist angle.

</details>


### [6] [Discovery of Kondo-Kramers Nodal Line Topological State in strongly correlated material CeAuAl$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10368)
*Hari Bhandari,Kevin Allen,Kuan-Sen Li,Yuan Fang,Arushi,Karthik Rao,Mounica Mahankali,Sanu Mishra,Jian-Xin Zhu,Christopher Lane,Qimiao Si,Emilia Morosan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在非中心对称、非手性的近藤晶格材料 CeAuAl₃ 中，首次实验与理论结合证实了强关联体系中的 Kramers 结点线（KNL）拓扑态，即"近藤-Kramer 结点线"，并观测到由对称性破缺导致的内禀反常霍尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kramers 结点线理论上存在于所有非手性、非中心对称晶体结构中，但此前的实验实现仅局限于弱关联体系，其在强关联（近藤）体系中的实现仍属空白。作者希望探索近藤关联对 KNL 拓扑的影响，并寻找强关联拓扑材料。

Method: 结合磁输运测量、热力学测量、密度泛函理论（DFT）以及紧束缚模型计算，系统研究 CeAuAl₃ 的能带拓扑、KNL 位置及对称性保护机制，并通过对称性破缺（含自旋极化破坏时间反演对称）分析 Berry 曲率与反常霍尔效应的起源。

Result: 发现 CeAuAl₃ 在 Γ–Z 和 X–P 方向存在由时间反演对称和镜面对称保护的 KNL；破坏晶体对称性及自旋极化会解除 KNL 简并，产生 Berry 曲率并导致内禀反常霍尔效应（AHE）。更重要的是，大 AHE 出现在磁有序温度 T_N 以下，且更关键地出现在近藤温度以下，表明近藤关联使 KNL 处于费米面处而非偶然能量位置。CeAuAl₃ 成为首个被明确鉴定为"近藤-Kramers 结点线"这一新关联拓扑态的化合物。

Conclusion: 本工作鉴定了一种新的拓扑态——近藤-Kramers 结点线，CeAuAl₃ 是首个承载该关联拓扑态并表现出相应 AHE 的化合物。该研究为在所有兼具非手性、非中心对称结构与近藤关联的化合物中寻找近藤-KNL 体系指明了路径。

Abstract: Kramers nodal lines (KNLs) are predicted in all achiral, non-centrosymmetric crystal structures, and yet there are very few experimental realizations of KNLs, only in weakly correlated systems. Especially, their realization in strongly correlated systems remains unexplored. Here we investigate KNL topology in the non-centrosymmetric, achiral Kondo lattice CeAuAl$_3$, using magnetotransport, thermodynamic measurements, density functional theory (DFT), and tight-binding toy model calculations. We find that CeAuAl$_3$ hosts KNLs in the $\mathitΓ\text{-}Z$ and $X\text{-}P$ directions, protected by time-reversal and mirror symmetries. Breaking of these crystalline symmetries, along with time-reversal symmetry via spin polarization, lifts the KNL degeneracy, which, in turn, generates Berry curvature resulting in an intrinsic anomalous Hall effect (AHE). More importantly, CeAuAl$_3$ shows large AHE not only below the magnetic ordering temperature $T_N$ but rather below the Kondo temperature. This underscores the role of the Kondo correlations in bringing the KNLs at the Fermi surface rather than at accidental energies, as would be expected without correlations. Our work identifies a new topological state, Kondo-Kramers Nodal Line, with CeAuAl$_3$ being the first compound explicitly identified to host this new correlated topological state, and demonstrating the expected AHE associated with it. More generally, our work outlines the pathway for finding Kondo-KNL systems in all compounds that combine achiral, non-centrosymmetric structure with Kondo correlations.

</details>

Applied magnetic fields suppress these anomalies and reduce the recovered magnetic entropy, suggesting further enhancement of frustration under field. A systematic magnetodielectric response closely follows the field dependence of the magnetization, indicating coupling between magnetic and dielectric degrees of freedom. The low lattice symmetry enables multiple inequivalent exchange pathways and anisotropic interactions while relaxing symmetry constraints on coupling among spin, lattice, and dielectric degrees of freedom. These results establish rose-phase Na$_2$CoP$_2$O$_7$ as a platform for exploring frustrated quantum magnetism and coupled functionalities in effective $S=1/2$ magnets.

Abstract: Quantum spin ice attracts considerable attention due to its remarkable low-energy properties, described by the effective gauge theory, which mimics the quantum electrodynamics (QED). The ``effective" QED in quantum spin ice is beyond the ``real" QED in our universe in many respects. Above all, the effective QED hosts two kinds of gapped particles, the electric monopole, in addition to the magnetic monopole. Among the two monopoles, the electric monopole is of purely quantum origin, and its character has not been well resolved. In this work, we focus on the Rokhsar Kivelson point of the quantum dimer model, as the low-energy description of the quantum XXZ model on a pyrochlore lattice, and address the excitation spectrum of the electric monopoles, by combining the quantum Monte Carlo simulation with the stochastic analytical continuation. Most strikingly, the electric monopole spectrum exhibits clear double-peak structure, corresponding to the bound states and scattering states of electric monopoles. The lower-energy peak is attributed to the exciton-like bound state of an electric monopole pair, a signal of positronium in quantum spin ice. We found that the flippable loop operator serves as a local physical observable to excite a pair of electric monopoles, implying that monopoles can be detected through lattice or optical response.

Here we study the momentum dependence of the energy and damping of collective excitations in optimally doped cuprates in the normal state. In particular, we analyze existing Electron Energy Loss Spectroscopy (EELS) data of Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8 [Phys. Rev. B 44, 7155 (1991)] and new analogous optical plasmon data of Bi2Sr2Ca2Cu3O10. Furthermore, we reanalyze similar Resonant Inelastic Xray Scattering (RIXS) acoustic plasmon data [Phys. Rev. R 6, 043184 (2024)] of La1.84Sr0.16CuO4 and La1.84Ce0.16CuO4. We contrast these two-particle excitation results with one-particle excitations measured by Angle-Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES). Most of the plasmon behavior can be described well by combined self-energies of a hole and an electron. The dispersion of optical plasmons at high energy is only weakly determined by correlation effects, indicating resilient quasiparticles. The damping of the optical plasmons at low energy, however, is strongly influenced by the reduced scattering rates described in the marginal Fermi liquid model for "strange-metal" behavior. In addition, the same model describes the linear-in-energy damping of acoustic plasmons. Contrary to some theoretical predictions, we see no overdamped optical plasmons below the classical Lindhard critical wave vector.

Abstract: Observers are expected to be essential to describe closed universes, but their description within a conventional holographic theory remains challenging. We study two double-scaled Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev models coupled by a four-parameter interaction, which includes a generalized Maldacena-Qi coupling, in a state that contains an observer, represented as a particle. With a heavy particle inside, the wormhole connecting the two systems can only grow. Rare products of Hamiltonian strings cancel the string of the particle: a closed baby universe then carries the observer, and the wormhole, now empty, recovers discrete states of finite length. The energy gained by the empty wormhole pays for the entropy of the observer, in a first-order transition whose latent heat is the transition temperature times this entropy. Krylov complexity, which measures the wormhole length, drops at the transition and thus detects from the boundary the emergence of the baby universe, whose boundary description is actively debated. The boundary also loses the Holevo information about the microstates of the observer, which the baby universe carries.

Each term represents a resummation of infinitely many Feynman diagrams. Near the critical gauge coupling where conformality is lost, we resum the leading singular behaviour to all orders in $q$. Additionally, we compute the leading contributions to the Bremsstrahlung function, the coefficient of the leading irrelevant operator in the fusion limit, and argue that the Casimir energy receives no corrections semiclassically. Abstract: Stanley Deser was Ancell Professor Emeritus at Brandeis University and Visiting Professor at the California Institute of Technology. He was a world-renowned theoretical physicist whose accomplishments include the Arnowitt-Deser-Misner (ADM) formulation of general relativity, supergravity with Zumino, the dynamics of flat space with Jackiw and 't Hooft and many other contributions to the quantum theory of gravity. In the 1980s he was instrumental in securing National Science Foundation approval of the LIGO gravitational wave experiment against much opposition. He was awarded the Einstein Medal in 2015 for important contributions to general relativity, in particular the development of the ADM formalism. Other awards include: The Dannie Heineman Prize 1994; Fellow of the National Academy of Sciences 1994; Honorary Member of the Torino Academy of Sciences 2001; Member of the American Academy of Arts and Sciences 1979, Fellow of the American Institute of Physics.

The Lifshitz region shrinks until its scaling solution disappears at $λ^*$, leaving a no-AdS region, beyond whose edge the flows end before reaching any asymptotic region, and above $λ_c$ the dome consists entirely of asymptotically AdS hairy black holes. There this value lies at the deepest symmetry-breaking minimum that the horizon effective potential, of Landau-Ginzburg form, can have. Unlike a conventional Landau-Ginzburg description, this interpretation rests on the scalar field at the horizon rather than on the boundary order parameter. Abstract: We revisit the thermodynamic implications of the Gauss-Bonnet topological term in four spacetime dimensions. It has been previously claimed in the literature that this term, although it does not modify the equations of motion, shifts the black hole entropy by a contribution proportional to the Euler characteristic of the horizon. We argue that this conclusion is sensitive to the choice of the boundary action on the horizon. We then identify a choice for which the Wald entropy expression naturally reproduces the Jacobson-Myers entropy, even for non-stationary black holes. We also show that there are no further contributions to conserved quantities from smooth outer boundaries, regardless of the choice of boundary conditions there and of the asymptotic behaviour of the spacetime. Moreover, we demonstrate that the topological Gauss-Bonnet term changes the critical temperature of the Hawking-Page phase transition, opening, in principle, a way for detecting its presence in the gravitational action. In fact, for a positive Gauss-Bonnet coupling constant, we observe a zeroth-order Hawking phase transition at high temperatures and pressures. While our discussion focuses on the case of the Gauss-Bonnet term in 4D, the results are more general and qualitatively apply to other Lovelock Lagrangians in their respective critical dimensions.

Together, these terms determine an integral relative characteristic number; the additive response is one third of that number, modulo integers. When the base's third and fourth rational cohomology groups vanish, the response equals eight times the Crowley-Goette invariant, without assuming that the base is simply connected. A calibration using the actual quaternionic line bundle gives additive value one third on $\mathrm{Sp}(2)$, recovering the known third-root phase. Distinct integral String data on one fixed ten-manifold give all three phase values, showing which global background information the response can detect. The physical identifications retain their prescribed fermion data, while the separate application to integral topological modular forms and vertex operator superalgebras requires compatible integral lifts. The response calculation constructs neither a microscopic realization nor an equivalence of complete theories.

Abstract: In this Letter, we develop a graph-geometric framework and prove that the pentagonal bipyramid (PBP) is the unique global minimiser of both the seven-particle Lennard-Jones energy in $\mathbb{R}^3$ and the seven-charge Thomson energy on $S^2$, up to rigid symmetries and particle relabelling. For the Thomson problem, this strengthens a recent deterministic global-optimality certificate to numerical tolerance into an exact global-optimality and uniqueness theorem. A weak-interaction graph, defined by thresholding pair geometry, organises configuration space without imposing symmetry or discarding any pair interaction. For Lennard-Jones, pair-energy counting, graph classification, subset bounds, and force balance reduce all possible minimisers to 29 bounded domains; exact geometric certificates exclude 28, localise the survivor near the PBP, and a uniform restoring-energy estimate proves uniqueness. The Thomson proof follows the same progression: graph reduction, geometric localisation, and rigidity, but finalises through whole-domain coercivity and a global localisation theorem. The same fivefold order therefore emerges in two very different physical settings: an unconstrained cluster with a preferred bond length and purely repelling charges confined to a sphere, showing a common geometric organising principle for seven particles that is not tied to either interaction.

Abstract: A Markov operator on a matrix algebra, that is, a unital completely positive map, is called positivity improving if it maps every nonzero positive element to an invertible one. This is the noncommutative counterpart of a stochastic matrix with strictly positive entries. We show that positivity improvement is self-dual and equivalent to a faithful quantum Markov--Dobrushin--Doeblin minorization. We also compare the scalar trace minorization with the optimal common-lower-bound construction of the quantum Markov--Dobrushin coefficient. We then characterize positivity improvement through the Kraus space of the map: a Markov operator is positivity improving if and only if the Hilbert--Schmidt orthogonal complement of its Kraus space contains no operator of rank one. As a consequence the Kraus rank of a positivity improving Markov operator on the algebra of $d\times d$ matrices is at least $2d-1$, and an explicit Hankel-type channel shows that this bound is attained in every dimension. Although deciding positivity improvement is NP-hard in general, we obtain complete and effectively checkable classifications for qubits and qutrits, together with families of examples (Pauli, Weyl and Werner--Holevo channels) showing that all our estimates are optimal. Finally we show that, in infinite dimensions, positivity improvement no longer implies any contraction of the Markov operator.

Abstract: The central difficulty in quantum many-body physics is that the Hilbert space dimension grows exponentially with the total degrees of freedom. A key feature to circumvent this problem is locality, which manifests dynamically through an approximate causal structure in the form of a Lieb-Robinson bound (LRB). LRBs have been proved for quantum spin systems and certain lattice boson systems, but so far never for realistic continuum systems. We present the first LRB that applies to a standard system of one-dimensional continuum fermions. It applies to initial states with controlled moments of local particle number and energy and is thus thermodynamically stable. Our result breaks the main technical barrier that has existed for using the vast locality toolbox on continuum systems.

Abstract: We compare the fixed-charge planar Gross--Witten--Wadia (GWW) model with source-free one-dimensional QCD at large $N$ and $N_f/N$ fixed. In both models the resolvent and one-cut condition give an algebraic equation for a derivative of the free energy: a quartic for GWW and a sextic for QCD. Finite-$N$ Toeplitz determinants independently check the selected QCD branch. Signed permutations of homogeneous parameter triples realize $S_4$; the resulting action on the algebraic branches and operator labels gives a common projective description of the two models. For QCD, the sextic yields a differential recurrence linking small-charge rows two apart. The same second-order PDE gives a finite-width column recursion that reconstructs the coefficient pyramid from its GWW boundary. A distinct boundary, obtained from the first resummed coefficient, is the Narayana triangle. A formally collapsed slice counts decorated bipartite plane trees and admits an exact symmetric product formula. The many-flavor scaling reduces the sextic to the quartic and retains transverse projective data: the QCD divided-difference triple coalesces at this boundary, whereas the limiting quartic satisfies the full standard GWW third-order system. We distinguish algebraic degree, mixed differential order, and fixed-parameter ordinary differential order throughout.

Abstract: In non-archimedean numerical analysis, a verified $p$-adic approximation can determine an absolute value, and hence downstream probabilistic rankings and classification decisions, with mathematical exactness long before the underlying number is fully resolved. We turn this observation into rigorous finite-precision certificates for norm-based readouts of amplitude families. For a common error radius, a nonempty resolved set already fixes every exact maximizer, ties included, at the sharp integer absolute precision $v_*+1$, and conditioning on that set yields an exact total-variation identity separating unresolved $p$-adic mass from real rounding error. For aggregates of $p$-adic exponentials, we give a centered-moment certificate with an explicit remainder bound: on rational data it runs in exact rational arithmetic without individual exponential evaluations, attains its minimal bound at an active phase, and resolves every nonzero aggregate at high order; we also obtain an aggregate perturbation bound free of summand-count factors. For multiclass $p$-adic classifiers that decode by minimizing the norm of a class discriminant, we prove a necessary and sufficient certification condition and show that the sharp common precision threshold is $v^{(2)}+1$, governed by the second-largest discriminant valuation, so the winning discriminant need not itself be resolved. We apply these certificates to discrete $p$-adic path-integral actions on uniform affine chains, deriving explicit action centers and proving exact output-independence at zero effective quadratic coupling. Pseudocode and a reproducible reference implementation accompany the theory.

Abstract: We study the asymmetric simple exclusion process on the half-line with particle insertion and no particle removal at the boundary. Abstract: Which complex classical paths contribute to a real-time Feynman path integral? We show that resurgence provides a practical alternative. We compute the asymptotic series of the real and complex classical paths to high order, with Feynman diagrams and with a transport recursion for the cumulants, and read off the adjacency of the classical paths from the divergent tails of the series with Borel-Padé methods. The South-East rule then turns adjacency into relevance. For a particle meeting a Rosen-Morse barrier, we identify the relevant complex classical paths in front of and behind the barrier, including the complex path that carries quantum tunnelling, follow ordinary and higher-order Stokes phenomena as the final point moves, and verify the predictions with the exact propagator. The resummed series reproduce the propagator with exponential accuracy. Finally, we outline how the expansion extends to relativistic path integrals governed by a Hamiltonian constraint.

Abstract: We describe the projective orbits of complex spinors from the point of view of the momentum map. Abstract: We study eigenstate fluctuations of the particle number in the nearest-neighbour free-fermion model on the periodic cubic lattice $(\mathbb{Z}/L\mathbb{Z})^d$. For every fixed dimension, every particle number, every subvolume, and every normalized energy eigenstate, we prove a bound of order $NL^{d-1}$ for the particle-number variance. The corresponding eigenstate-thermalization estimate is of order $L^{d-1}/(Nη^2)$ for fluctuations of relative size $η$. In particular, at positive particle density the bound decays as the inverse linear size. The proof decomposes the centered particle-number operator according to one-particle energy transfer and treats the zero- and nonzero-transfer components separately. The nonzero-transfer part is controlled by a sharp resonance bound, proved from Mann's theorem on relations among roots of unity, while the zero-transfer part is estimated by a Hilbert-Schmidt argument. These bounds are optimal in every dimension: the maximal nonzero-gap multiplicity has order $L^{d-1}$, and suitable many-body eigenstates realize matching variance lower bounds and show that the concentration bound cannot be improved in estimates uniform in the tolerance parameter.

Abstract: We study a dense $N$-soliton condensate for the infinite Toda lattice in the limit $N \to \infty$. In contrast to previous mathematical studies of soliton gases where the norming constants are assumed to be deterministic or infinitesimal, we consider initial scattering data where both the discrete eigenvalues and the norming constants are independent random variables. The norming constants $γ_j$ are allowed to be arbitrarily large under a mild moment condition $\mathbb{E}[γ_j^ν] < \infty$ ($ν> 1$). Our main result is divided into two parts. First, we develop a probabilistic Riemann--Hilbert framework to show that the system self-averages: despite large, independent fluctuations in the initial data, the random Toda lattice variables converge deterministically to an effective continuum model at a polynomial rate $\mathcal{O}(N^{-δ})$ for $0 < δ< 1/2$. Second, by performing the Deift--Zhou nonlinear steepest descent analysis of the continuum problem, we obtain the complete leading-order macroscopic description of the lattice across the entire space-time plane. The solution decomposes into five distinct finite-gap regimes, characterized explicitly by modulated elliptic and genus-2 waves with an error of order $\mathcal{O}(1/\ln N)$. In contrast to the straight characteristic rays typical of standard soliton gas limits, the interfaces separating these five macroscopic regimes in the space-time plane are genuinely non-linear curves; in particular, the boundary of the modulated genus-2 region is completely non-linear. Finally, under our assumptions, we establish the Nonlinear Dispersion Relations for the Toda soliton condensate and prove that the macroscopic ensemble averages are determined by the soliton gas density of states.

Result: 占据场由自洽洛伦兹轮廓支配，有效共振尺度K_N(E)≍N^{2-γ}；广义逆参与比和在q=1/2处发生相变；最大分量服从Gumbel涨落，最小分量在N^{-1-γ}尺度上有指数极限；对任意固定q>0，最大分量对逆参与比和的贡献渐近为零，故固定q统计量不冻结。所有结论为淬灭意义下成立，且在对角无序的高概率正则性事件上一致，故在退火设置下亦成立。

Conclusion: GUE Rosenzweig-Porter模型（1<γ<2）的特征向量占据场呈洛伦兹型共振结构，逆参与比统计在q=1/2处有相变，极值分量分别呈现Gumbel与指数极限律，且固定q统计量不冻结，结果在淬灭与退火意义下均成立。

Abstract: We study the occupation probabilities $p_i=|u_i|^2$ of a normalized bulk eigenvector in the GUE Rosenzweig-Porter model $A_N=B_N+τ_NG_N$, with $τ_N^2=N^{1-γ}$ and $1<γ<2$. Conditioning on an eigenvalue at a regular bulk energy $E$ through the normalized one-eigenpair Kac-Rice law, we derive an exact finite-$N$ projective density and reduce its asymptotic analysis to a Hubbard-Stratonovich integral over $2\times2$ Hermitian matrices, which we evaluate by the saddle-point method. The resulting occupation field is governed by a self-consistent Lorentzian profile with effective resonant size $K_N(E)\asymp N^{2-γ}$ and local exponential fluctuations. We prove the asymptotics of all generalized inverse participation sums and identify a transition at $q=1/2$. We also obtain a two-branch thickened amplitude-counting spectrum. At the two ends of the occupation field we prove marked Poisson point-process limits: the largest components have Gumbel fluctuations, whereas the smallest component has an exponential limit on the scale $N^{-1-γ}$. Finally, for every fixed $q>0$ the largest component contributes an asymptotically vanishing fraction of the inverse participation sum, showing that these fixed-$q$ statistics do not freeze. The statements are quenched, uniformly on a high-probability regularity event for the diagonal disorder, and hence also hold in the annealed setting.

Abstract: The investigation on the spin conductivity has now been blooming in burgeoning altermagnetic materials. Here, an alternative and elementary derivation for the spin conductivity expression is presented. This is done within the context of perturbation theory. It indicates that our derived expression for the $\mathcal{T}$-even ($\mathcal{T}$ for time reversal) spin conductivity agrees exactly with those derived from the Kubo linear response theory and quantum kinetic theory. In addition, the expression for the $\mathcal{T}$-odd spin conductivity in the clean limit is exactly consistent with those presented previously. We show that the $\mathcal{T}$-odd ($\mathcal{T}$-even) spin conductivity arises from the contribution of zeroth-order (first-order) wave function correction. Our work provides some physical insight into the spin conductivity from the perturbation theory and shines more light on the charge-to-spin conversion mechanism in magnetic materials.

Method: 本文采用路线图（Roadmap）形式，通过欧洲COST Action CA21144（SuperQuMap）网络内的密集讨论与协作，组织并综述了以下研究方向：（1）探索量子材料，重点研究拓扑与电子关联如何催生技术跃迁；（2）操控超导相位，利用磁学、3D制造技术与可调电子关联；（3）运用先进显微技术（如角分辨光电子能谱ARPES和扫描探针）可视化量子行为；（4）详述网络内开发的量子器件及其对现代量子计算的影响。

Conclusion: 在量子力学百年之际，超导作为可直接观测和操控的宏观量子现象，是量子技术的关键平台。通过SuperQuMap网络的协同，该路线图为基于超导的量子技术确立了清晰的发展方向，有望借助拓扑与关联电子、相位操控、先进表征和器件工程实现量子计算领域的量子跃迁。

Abstract: In 2025, the Year of Quantum Science and Technology, we celebrate a century of quantum mechanics, witnessing a surge in activities that illuminate its inherent strangeness and drive technological innovation. Superconductivity, discovered 114 years ago, stands as a prime example, offering direct and compelling evidence of macroscopic quantum phenomena. Beyond its ability to conduct immense currents without loss, superconductivity reveals the quantum realm operating on a scale we can directly observe and manipulate. The macroscopic quantum coherence, where an ensemble of particles is described by a single wave function, leads to remarkable consequences: dissipation-less current and flux quantization - the basic properties exploited in superconducting quantum circuit fabrication. This Roadmap has been inspired by intensive discussions and collaborations emerging from the European COST Action CA21144 (SuperQuMap - Superconducting Nanodevices and Quantum Materials for Coherent Manipulation). The roadmap outlines the network's concrete activities, driving advancements in superconductor-based quantum technologies and charting future directions. Spanning fundamental research to practical applications, the roadmap incorporates insights from industry partners developing quantum computation. It begins by exploring quantum materials, highlighting how topology and electronic correlations could catalyze a quantum leap in technology. We then delve into manipulating the superconducting phase, leveraging advancements in magnetism, 3D fabrication, and tunable correlations. Further, we showcase the advanced microscopy techniques - such as angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy and scanning probes - used to visualize quantum behavior. Finally, and crucially, we detail the quantum devices developed within the network, and their transformative impact on modern quantum computing approaches.

</details>

Conclusion: 压片压力是调控Pr1111在常压合成中结构、微观结构与超导性能的重要加工参数,存在最佳压力(约870 MPa),压力过高会导致性能劣化。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of the pelletization pressure on the structural, microstructural, and superconducting properties of fluorine-doped PrFeAsO0.7F0.3 (Pr1111). Precursor powders are pressed into pellets at pressures ranging from 174 to 1131 MPa, after which the applied pressure is released and all samples undergo an identical two-step conventional synthesis process at ambient pressure (CSP-AP). All samples predominantly exhibit the tetragonal P4/nmm structure, and the lattice parameter a and unit-cell volume V decrease with increasing pelletization pressure up to an intermediate-pressure range (~900 MPa) before increasing at higher pressures. The actual fluorine-content derived from energy-dispersive X-ray spectroscopy (EDX) also increase with pelletization pressure, while Raman spectra show only modest variations in the characteristic vibrational modes associated with Fe and As. The superconducting response exhibits a non-monotonic dependence on the pelletization pressure. Among the investigated samples, the sample pelletized at 870 MPa exhibits the most favorable overall superconducting properties, with a superconducting onset temperature of 44.2 K, ~5 K higher than that of the lowest-pressure sample. It also displays a narrower superconducting transition, the highest relative sample density, a favorable effective magnetic critical current density at high fields, and a relatively large effective thermally activated flux-flow activation energy. At higher pelletization pressures, the superconducting response deteriorates, coinciding with increases in the lattice parameters, increased secondary-phase contributions, and reduced relative density. These findings establish the pelletization pressure as a relevant processing parameter for modifying the structural, microstructural and superconducting properties of Pr1111 during CSP-AP.

Abstract: The pseudogap state, an anomalous momentum dependent partial suppression of low energy electronic excitations persisting well above the superconducting critical temperature in underdoped cuprates, remains one of the most profound enigmas in condensed physics today. ARPES reveals a Fermi surface truncated into disconnected segments and scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) visualizes a spatially heterogeneous energy gap around the Fermi level. These observations are contextualized alongside transport anomalies, including resistivity and bulk magnetic susceptibility, to construct a phenomenological portrait of a phase that violates conventional Fermi liquid theory. By mapping the electronic phase diagram, we highlight the abrupt transition at above optimal doping. We conclude with open questions still to be answered. This review underscores the role of the pseudogap as a key to unlocking the unconventional pairing mechanism of hole doped cuprates and the broader physics of strongly correlated electron systems.

The low-energy edge response is suppressed when the magnetization aligns with the edge-state spin axis. Abstract: Nonreciprocal superconducting responses provide a sensitive probe of superconducting states in systems where inversion and time-reversal symmetries are broken. These observations exhibit phenomenology similar to that reported in layered superconductors, where an orbital-FFLO state with finite-momentum Cooper pairing has been reported. Our results establish a protocol for assessing out-of-plane vortex contributions to the superconducting diode effect and demonstrate that nonreciprocal responses provide complementary probes of unconventional superconducting states.

Abstract: This paper presents a finite-element framework to study how device geometry affects the switching current of multilayer heater nano cryotrons (hTrons). The device comprises a 50 nm Au/5 nm Ti heater, a 100 nm SiO2 spacer, a 23.6 nm NbN channel, and a Si substrate. Two-dimensional electrothermal simulations examine nine heater and channel width combinations using widths of 100, 500, and 1000 nm. Three-dimensional simulations show how heat flows through the layers and spreads across the channel near the heater-channel overlap. Heater electrical losses and heat transport are coupled, whereas the channel switching threshold is obtained separately from a temperature-dependent constitutive relation. Published global temperature measurements support the switching-law calibration, and heater-current measurements assess the resulting device response. The model measurement comparisons show a systematic reduction of switching-current density with heating, but also geometry-dependent discrepancies in the low-drive plateau and transition shape. A common extraction protocol is formulated for heater-current and heater-power requirements at a specified suppression level, while separating fitted device amplitudes from controlled geometry effects. The resulting comparisons connect thermal fields to design metrics while distinguishing calibration from validation and excluding transient-performance claims from the stationary analysis

Abstract: The integration of polar and superconducting materials in the atomically thin limit typically results in a competition that suppresses the superconducting order. Contrary to the expectation that dense insulating barriers would quench superconductivity, we find that the polar interface can coexist with a superconducting state. We observe that the transition temperature (Tc) of TiN is enhanced by nearly 2 K over its bulk value, while δ-NbN maintains a relatively high Tc exceeding 20 K. We demonstrate that this resilience arises from the formation of a polar metal, where the strong internal electric fields of the AlN layers are partially screened by a charge redistribution within the nitride layers. Abstract: Superconducting pairing symmetry provides crucial insight into the microscopic mechanisms of Cooper pairing. However, probing the local manifestations of sign-changing superconducting order parameters remains experimentally challenging. A distinct Josephson tunneling signal is detected above O sites in the underlying CuO2 plane, whereas the signal nearly vanishes above the Cu-plaquette diagonals, including Cu sites and plaquette centers. This unique intra-unit-cell spatial pattern agrees with the theoretical prediction for the symmetry-dependent cancellation of Josephson tunneling between an s-wave superconducting tip and a d-wave superconducting sample. Our findings provide atomic-scale, real-space evidence for d-wave pairing symmetry in OPT high-Tc Bi-2223 and establish SJTM as a powerful approach for investigating the pairing symmetry of unconventional superconductors.

Abstract: Understanding interactions between different low-energy excitations is a central theme in condensed-matter physics. Our results provide direct experimental evidence of a supercurrent-controlled interaction between magnons and quasiparticles in the superconductor. The approach used in this study relies on supercurrents, enabling magnon modulation without Joule heating. These findings establish a new route to control magnons via superconductivity and pave the way for energy-efficient magnonic devices.

Conclusion: 共振扭转磁测量能够区分 Fulde-Ferrell 有限动量态，但判别依据应是 FF 相内的宽带软化和上临界场处的有限台阶，而非一阶相变线处的尖锐下陷。

Abstract: Resonant torsion magnetometry measures the angular curvature of the free energy and is sensitive to magnetic anisotropy. Field rotation drives a first-order transition from the uniform condensate into the finite-momentum phase and a continuous transition into the normal state. The first-order line produces a sharp softening whose integrated weight is fixed by the magnetization jump, so the dip alone does not identify finite momentum. The distinguishing response is a broad softening inside the Fulde-Ferrell phase, caused by relaxation of the gap and pairing momentum, followed by a finite step at the upper critical field.

</details>

Abstract: The recently synthesized intergrowth compound Th$_2$Mo$_2$Ir$_2$Si$_4$C is an experimentally established bulk superconductor whose topological electronic structure remains unexplored. Here, we identify it as a candidate for connate topological superconductivity and reveal two microscopic routes to vortex Majorana zero modes. Vortex Bogoliubov--de Gennes calculations further establish a relatively robust surface-state-associated Kitaev phase and a weakly gapped, Th-orbital-derived Kitaev phase. For the latter, shifting the vortex core closes and reopens the vortex-line gap and reverses its one-dimensional topological invariant, consistent with an effective Kitaev-chain mass inversion. Finite-length-vortex calculations reveal distinct spatial and spin-resolved signatures of their Majorana end modes. Abstract: The quantum metric plays an essential role in flat-band superconductivity, governing the superfluid weight and coherence length. While previous studies have primarily focused on mean-field superconducting properties, the role of quantum geometry in fluctuation phenomena remains largely unexplored, despite the importance of fluctuations in flat-band systems. To address this issue, we employ a multiorbital T-matrix approximation that retains the orbital structure of the Bloch wave functions. By analyzing models in which the quantum metric can be tuned independently of the band dispersion, we show that an enhanced quantum metric suppresses the fluctuation-induced pseudogap in both flat-band and dispersive-band systems. In dispersive-band systems, this suppression is accompanied by a crossover from non-Fermi-liquid to Fermi-liquid behavior. This spectral evolution suggests a quantum-metric-driven crossover from a BEC-like strong-coupling regime to a BCS-like weak-coupling regime. More broadly, our results establish the quantum metric as a control parameter of interaction-driven fluctuation phenomena beyond mean-field theory.

Abstract: Normal-state topology can obstruct superconducting pairing and give rise to gapless nodes in the quasiparticle spectrum. However, existing pairing-obstruction mechanisms largely rely on Fermi-surface topology or global Bloch-band topology. Here, we establish a new and general mechanism: one-dimensional (1D) real topology, defined on antiunitary-symmetry-invariant loops, enforces pairing zeros without requiring Fermi-surface or global Bloch-band topology. We derive sufficient conditions and lower bounds for pairing zeros across diverse pairing channels, for both zero and finite Cooper-pair momenta. The theorems apply to systems of arbitrary spatial dimensions without requiring the pairing bands to be globally isolated. We further show that such topologically enforced pairing zeros, if isolated, necessarily carry odd vorticity and can give rise to Bogoliubov-de Gennes nodes and zero-energy boundary modes, as demonstrated in a minimal two-orbital model. Our results reshape the understanding of how normal-state topology can obstruct the superconducting order, highlight the significant role of 1D real topology beyond single-particle band phenomena, and provide a general framework for identifying candidate materials hosting nodal superconductivity.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导二极管通常需要外加磁场或复杂的材料工程来实现非互易输运。作者旨在探索一种无需外加磁场的超导二极管方案，利用相邻铁磁层（FePt）的剩余磁化及其杂散场来实现场自由、可控的超导非互易性，同时采用室温溅射工艺以保证器件制备的简便性与可扩展性。

Method: 作者采用完全在室温下溅射沉积的方法制备W-N/FePt双层膜，其中无定形W-N为超导层，FePt层厚48 nm且具有剩余条带畴磁化。利用光刻图形化微桥器件，对比W-N/FePt双层膜与单层W-N参考膜，并在三种名义W-N厚度（18、24、28 nm）下进行测试。通过测量零外加磁场下不同剩余磁化取向（相对输运电流垂直或平行）时的电流-电压（I-V）特性，表征二极管行为；并用涡旋非对称进入与运动机制解释杂散场诱导的非互易性。

Result: 双层膜的超导转变温度约为4.1-4.3 K。零场下I-V特性呈现显著非互易性：当FePt剩余磁化垂直于电流时二极管响应最大，磁化反向则极性反转，磁化平行于电流时响应被强烈抑制。在3 K、剩余磁化垂直于电流的构型下，W-N厚度从18 nm增至28 nm，使正反极性耗散阈值之间的电流区间由约0.3 mA拓宽至1.55 mA。三种双层膜在此构型下的二极管效率均约为75-80%。

Conclusion: 通过在室温溅射的W-N超导层上集成具有剩余条带畴磁化的FePt层，可以实现高效率、无场且极性可调的超导二极管效应。其非互易性源于桥边缘附近磁杂散场驱动的涡旋非对称进入与运动，且可通过改变W-N厚度调控电流非互易区间，为可扩展的超导二极管器件提供了简便的制备途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate high-efficiency, field-free superconducting diode behavior in W-N/FePt bilayers fabricated entirely by sputtering at room temperature. The devices combine amorphous W-N superconducting thin films with a 48-nm-thick FePt layer exhibiting remanent stripe-domain magnetization. We used lithographically patterned microbridges to compare W-N/FePt bilayers with single-layer W-N reference films at three nominal W-N thicknesses (18, 24, and 28 nm). The bilayers exhibit superconducting transition temperatures of approximately 4.1-4.3 K. At zero applied magnetic field, the current-voltage (I-V) characteristics exhibit pronounced nonreciprocity, controlled by the orientation of the in-plane remanent FePt magnetization relative to the transport current. The diode response is maximized for perpendicular alignment, reverses polarity upon magnetization reversal, and is strongly suppressed when the magnetization is aligned with the current. This behavior is consistent with asymmetric vortex entry and motion governed by the magnetic stray fields generated near the bridge edges. At 3 K with the remanent magnetization perpendicular to the current, increasing the W-N thickness from 18 to 28 nm widens the current interval between the dissipation thresholds for opposite polarities from about 0.3 to 1.55 mA. All three bilayers exhibit diode efficiencies of approximately 75-80% in this configuration.

</details>


### [99] [Direct imaging of vortex lattices across fractional fillings in two-dimensional Josephson junction arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12548)
*Cequn Li,Austin R. Kaczmarek,Omri Lesser,Maciej W. Olszewski,Katja C. Nowack*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用扫描SQUID显微镜首次在二维Au-Nb约瑟夫森结阵列中直接成像填充因子f=1/8到1/2的层级化涡旋晶格，揭示了多种无输运特征的状态、f=1/4处两种结构的共存，以及缺陷如何破坏涡旋有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约瑟夫森结阵列被预测存在丰富的公度涡旋晶格序列，但此前缺乏直接成像手段，输运测量也无法分辨底层涡旋排列及其有序性的破缺方式。

Method: 采用扫描超导量子干涉器件（SQUID）显微镜对二维Au-Nb约瑟夫森结阵列进行局部磁成像，直接分辨单个涡旋，系统表征不同公度填充因子下的涡旋构型及偏离公度填充时的缺陷结构。

Result: 在填充因子f=1/8至1/2范围内直接成像出层级化涡旋晶格，其中包括若干无明确输运信号的状态；在f=1/4处发现4×4与2×4两种结构不同的涡旋有序共存，与受挫XY模型中的能量简并一致；偏离公度填充时，f=1/2棋盘格在孤立多余涡旋与空位周围保持有序，而f=1/3阶梯状结构则碎裂成由畴壁分隔的畴。

Conclusion: 受挫作用、竞争构型与缺陷共同塑造了工程化超导晶格中公度涡旋有序的空间相干性，直接成像揭示了输运测量无法捕捉的涡旋排列细节与有序破缺机制。

Abstract: Josephson junction arrays are predicted to host a rich sequence of commensurate vortex lattices. However, direct imaging of these configurations remains limited, while transport measurements cannot resolve the underlying vortex arrangements or how order breaks down. Using scanning superconducting quantum interference device (SQUID) microscopy, we resolve individual vortices and directly image this long-predicted hierarchy spanning filling factors $f$ = 1/8 to 1/2 in two-dimensional Au-Nb Josephson junction arrays, including several states with no discernible transport signatures. At $f$ = 1/4, two structurally distinct vortex orders with 4$\times$4 and 2$\times$4 unit cells coexist, consistent with their energetic degeneracy in the frustrated XY model. Away from commensurate filling, we resolve defect structures tied to the underlying vortex-lattice order: the $f$ = 1/2 checkerboard retains its order around isolated excess vortices and vacancies, whereas the $f$ = 1/3 staircase fragments into domains separated by domain walls. These results reveal how frustration, competing configurations, and defects shape the spatial coherence of commensurate vortex order in engineered superconducting lattices.

</details>


### [100] [Ambient-pressure superconductivity in bulk single crystals of La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{10}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12788)
*R. Zhang,P. Reiss,A. Krajewska,M. Isobe,H. Takagi,P. Puphal*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了四层Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{13}$块体单晶的高压高温生长，并通过拓扑还原获得平方平面相La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{10}$，该相在常压下呈现体超导（起跳温度约18 K，屏蔽分数>60%）。研究发现超导与密度波/金属-绝缘体转变共存，上临界场各向异性较小（γ≈2.4），表明三维超导和四层块体间强耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐的超导仅局限于高压下的多层化合物或受外延应变稳定的平方平面无限层薄膜，缺乏块体、常压超导的平台，且难以系统研究层数对超导的调控。

Method: 采用高压高温（HPHT）技术生长四层RP镍酸盐La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{13}$（n=4）块体单晶，并复现五层La$_6$Ni$_5$O$_{16}$（n=5）的合成；随后用CaH$_2$对La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{13}$单晶进行拓扑还原，得到平方平面相La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{10}$。通过磁化率（屏蔽分数）、比热（跳变）及上临界场各向异性等测量表征超导性质。

Result: La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{10}$为块体、常压超导体，超导起跳温度约18 K，屏蔽分数超过60%，比热跳变证实体配对转变。所有晶体中均发现超导与密度波或金属-绝缘体转变共存。上临界场各向异性较小（γ≈2.4），且在T→Tc时保持有限，表明三维超导及四层块体间存在实质性耦合。

Conclusion: 平方平面镍酸盐R$_{n+1}$Ni$_n$O$_{2n+2}$可作为块体研究平台，仅通过改变层数即可调控镍价态进入超导区间，无需化学掺杂、压力或外延约束。

Abstract: Superconductivity in the Ruddlesden-Popper (RP) nickelates has so far been confined either to multilayer compounds under high pressure or to square-planar infinite-layer-type films stabilized by epitaxial strain. Here we report the high-pressure high-temperature (HPHT) growth of bulk single crystals of the quadruple-layer RP nickelate La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{13}$ ($n=4$) and reproduce the quintuple-layer La$_6$Ni$_5$O$_{16}$ ($n=5$) synthesis, two members of the series that had previously been accessible only as thin films. Topotactic reduction of La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{13}$ single crystals with CaH$_2$ yields the square-planar phase La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{10}$, which we show to be a bulk, ambient-pressure superconductor with $T_\mathrm{c}^\mathrm{onset} \approx 18\,\mathrm{K}$, a shielding fraction above 60\%, and a specific-heat jump that confirms a bulk pairing transition. All crystals show besides superconductivity a coexistence with a density-wave or metal-insulator transition. The upper critical field shows a small anisotropy, $γ\approx 2.4$, which remains finite as $T \to T_c$, indicating three-dimensional superconductivity with substantial coupling between the four-layer blocks. These results identify the square-planar nickelates $R_{n+1}$Ni$_n$O$_{2n+2}$ as a bulk platform in which the layer number alone tunes the nickel valence into the superconducting regime, without chemical substitution, pressure, or epitaxial constraint.

</details>

### [101] [Interpretable machine learning of formation energies and band gaps in hybrid perovskites through SHAP analysis and SISSO modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09944)
*Christopher Xia,Hao Zhang,Qingyuan Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合XGBoost机器学习筛选、SHAP可解释性分析与SISSO符号回归，在4,456种杂化有机-无机钙钛矿上建立了形成能和带隙的高精度预测模型，并推广至18万余种候选材料，实现了高效候选筛选与可解释的结构-性质关系分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂化有机-无机钙钛矿的性质预测需要兼顾准确性与可解释性，以支撑高效的材料探索与筛选；现有模型（如UPET）在该体系上的迁移性存疑，缺乏将机器学习预测与物理机理理解相结合的统一框架。

Method: 以4,456种化合物为数据集，训练并优化XGBoost模型预测形成能与带隙；利用SHAP分析及基于相关性的特征剪枝，为每个性质保留8个主要特征；再用SISSO从这些特征中推导出含4个非线性描述符的数学表达式；并将模型应用于超过180,000个候选材料，同时与CGCNN和UPET在约17,000个共有化合物上进行比较。

Result: 优化后的XGBoost模型测试集R²达0.99（形成能）和0.93（带隙）。在约17,000个共有化合物上，XGBoost与CGCNN的预测分布广泛重叠，而UPET预测值明显偏低，提示其在杂化有机-无机钙钛矿上的迁移性可能受限。SHAP与相关性剪枝为每个性质量保留8个主要特征，SISSO据此推导出含4个非线性描述符的表达式：形成能表达式耦合分子性质与无机性质，带隙表达式强调相对原子尺寸和B位特征。

Conclusion: 该框架将机器学习筛选与SHAP分析和SISSO描述符发现相结合，实现了高精度、可解释的杂化有机-无机钙钛矿性质预测，支持高效候选排序，并揭示了模型学习到的结构-性质关系（如形成能受分子与无机性质共同作用，带隙受原子尺寸与B位特征主导），同时指出UPET在该体系上的迁移性局限。

Abstract: Accurate and interpretable property prediction is essential for exploring hybrid organic-inorganic perovskites. Here, we combine machine-learning screening with SHAP analysis and SISSO descriptor discovery. Using 4,456 compounds, optimized XGBoost models achieved test-set $R^2$ values of 0.99 for formation energy and 0.93 for band gap and were applied to over 180,000 candidates. Comparison across approximately 17,000 common compounds revealed broadly overlapping distributions from XGBoost and CGCNN but substantially lower predictions from UPET, suggesting potential limitations in its transferability to hybrid organic-inorganic perovskites. SHAP analysis and correlation-based pruning retained eight primary features for each property, from which SISSO derived expressions comprising four nonlinear descriptors. These expressions couple molecular and inorganic properties for formation energy and emphasize relative atomic sizes and B-site characteristics for band gap. This framework supports efficient candidate prioritization and interpretable analysis of learned structures-property relationships in hybrid perovskites.

</details>


### [102] [Controlled Oxidation of Tellurium Nanoparticles for Telecom Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10135)
*Biman Jana*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了溶液法制备的碲（Te）纳米颗粒集合体在自然环境逐步暴露氧化下的光电性质，发现适度氧化可兼顾颗粒间电荷传输与暗电流抑制，从而制备出在405、532和1550 nm均有响应的光电探测器，1550 nm处达到最快17.7 s上升/40.2 s下降时间及1.3×10^6 Jones的比探测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面氧化对半导体纳米颗粒集合体的电荷传输有重要影响，但过度氧化与氧化不足之间的平衡难以把握。作者希望探索一种可控的、温和的表面氧化策略，以在促进颗粒间电荷传输的同时抑制暗电流，从而提升溶液法制备碲基光电探测器的性能，尤其是面向短波红外应用。

Method: 采用逐步自然空气暴露的方式氧化溶液法制备的碲纳米颗粒。通过结构表征与光谱分析确认颗粒为互联的元素碲纳米颗粒，表面共存α-TeO2和含羟基物种，且氧化态碲的相对占比低于此前强制空气氧化的样品。基于该材料构建光电探测器，并在405、532和1550 nm波长下测试其光响应、响应时间及噪声特性。

Result: 所得光电探测器在405、532和1550 nm均表现光响应，最大响应度分别为10.1、14.4和8.5 mA/W。1550 nm处上升时间17.7 s、下降时间40.2 s，估算的散粒噪声限比探测率为1.3×10^6 Jones。该自然氧化体系较强制空气氧化体系具有更低的氧化态碲相对贡献。

Conclusion: 控制表面氧化程度对于平衡颗粒间电荷传输与暗电流抑制至关重要。通过温和的自然空气暴露实现适度氧化，可制备出具备短波红外探测能力的溶液法碲基光电探测器，为相关器件的开发提供了有价值的思路。

Abstract: Surface oxidation plays an important role in governing charge transport in semiconductor nanoparticle assemblies. Here, we investigate the optoelectronic properties of solution-processed tellurium (Te) nanoparticle ensembles prepared through gradual ambient-air exposure. Structural and spectroscopic analyses reveal interconnected elemental Te nanoparticles with coexisting alpha-TeO2 and hydroxyl-containing surface species, exhibiting a lower relative contribution of oxidized Te than previously investigated forced-air-oxidized ensembles. The resulting photodetectors demonstrate photoresponse at 405, 532, and 1550 nm, with maximum responsivities of 10.1, 14.4, and 8.5 mA/W, respectively. At 1550 nm, the device exhibits rise and fall times of 17.7 and 40.2 s, respectively, and an estimated shot-noise-limited specific detectivity of 1.3 * 10^6 Jones. These findings highlight the importance of controlling surface oxidation to balance interparticle charge transport and dark-current suppression, offering insights for the development of solution-processed Te-based photodetectors for short-wave infrared applications.

</details>


### [103] [Hidden memory above the Neel temperature programs antiferromagnetic order in Cr2O3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10173)
*Josiah Keagy,Sumukh Mahesh,Wei-Cheng Liao,Jing Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究发现，在Cr₂O₃中，可以在高于奈尔温度(T_N)的顺磁态通过纯电场调控来编程反铁磁序，无需传统的同时施加电、磁场冷却。这种电场条件化产生一个可逆、可擦除、可弛豫的持久内部电偏置，进而在后续仅磁冷却时选择相反的奈尔矢量取向，揭示了电场编程记忆与反铁磁畴形成之间的意外耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁序的调控是磁电与反铁磁自旋电子器件的核心。在原型磁电反铁磁体Cr₂O₃中，畴选择传统上依赖冷却穿过T_N或在T_N以下同时施加电场与磁场，方法受限。作者旨在探索是否能在顺磁态(T_N以上)仅通过电场条件化来编程反铁磁序，从而揭示新的畴控制机制。

Method: 作者在不同热历史和电场历史下测量块体Cr₂O₃晶体的磁电系数α_zz。具体做法是：在T_N以上的顺磁态施加足够大的电场进行"条件化"，然后撤去电场；随后在无外加电场的情况下进行磁冷却，检验所得到的单畴状态及其奈尔矢量取向，并与传统磁电场冷却结果对比。同时验证该电场诱导状态的可逆写入、擦除及高温弛豫行为。

Result: 在T_N以上施加足够大的电场可建立一种电场撤除后仍持久的可逆状态。随后无外加电场的磁冷却产生了单畴状态，其奈尔矢量取向与传统磁电场冷却相反。该电诱导状态可在高温下可逆写入、擦除并弛豫，表现为长寿命的内部电偏置来主导畴选择。该行为与一种可产生内部偏置的亚稳态表面关联极态相一致。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了Cr₂O₃中电场编程记忆与反铁磁畴形成之间此前未知的耦合：仅通过在顺磁态的电场条件化即可产生长寿命内部电偏置并决定反铁磁畴取向。该发现为反铁磁畴控制提供了新途径，并提示亚稳态表面关联极态可能是内部偏置的来源。

Abstract: Control of antiferromagnetic order is central to magnetoelectric and antiferromagnetic spintronic devices. In the prototypical magnetoelectric antiferromagnet Cr2O3, domains are conventionally selected by simultaneous application of electric and magnetic fields during cooling through the Neel temperature (T_N) or at a fixed temperature below T_N. Here, we show that antiferromagnetic order can instead be programmed by electric-field conditioning entirely in the paramagnetic state above T_N. By measuring the magnetoelectric coefficient (alpha_zz) of bulk Cr2O3 crystals under different thermal and field histories, we find that a sufficiently large electric field above T_N establishes a reversible state that persists after the field is removed. Subsequent magnetic-field cooling with no applied electric field produces a single-domain state with the opposite Neel-vector orientation from conventional magnetoelectric field cooling. The electrically induced state can be reversibly written, erased, and allowed to relax at high temperatures, demonstrating a long-lived internal electric bias that governs domain selection. The observed behavior is consistent with a metastable surface-associated polar state that may generate this internal bias. These results reveal an unexpected coupling between electrically programmed memory and antiferromagnetic domain formation in Cr2O3.

</details>


### [104] [Crystal and magnetic structures of MnSi in zero magnetic field: Neutron diffraction and symmetry analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11353)
*Vladimir Pomjakushin,Igor Plokhikh,Douglas H. Fabini,Ekaterina Pomjakushina*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用高分辨率广角中子粉末衍射结合磁超空间对称分析，首次系统精修了立方 P2₁3 MnSi 零场下跨多个磁卫星族的磁结构，并比较了单臂菱方 3+1D 与立方四臂 3+4D 磁超空间群描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MnSi 的零场磁结构此前缺乏跨多个磁卫星族的系统精修；需要厘清单臂 3+1D 与四臂 3+4D 磁超空间群两种描述的差异与等价性，并确定磁结构的理想极限模型。

Method: 将高分辨率广角中子粉末衍射与磁超空间对称分析相结合；沿 〈111〉 方向采用传播矢量，分别构建单臂菱方 3+1D 与立方四臂 3+4D 磁超空间群描述；利用 {100} 附近两个卫星群的同时消光以及 18 个独立变化的卫星群强度进行精修，确定横向圆螺旋模型；单臂理想模型采用 MSSG R3.1'(0,0,g)ts。

Result: {100} 附近两卫星群同时消光，指示横向圆螺旋模型，且仅需一个可调磁振幅即可描述；理想单臂模型中每 Mn 磁矩恒定为 0.535(3) μB；四臂 3+4D 构造（所示相位选择）磁矩范围从 0 到 0.874(5) μB/Mn，但均方根磁矩与 3+1D 结构相同；两种模型粉末谱完全相同，现有测量无法区分。平均晶体结构在实验分辨率内保持立方。平均晶格参数相对精度 σₐ/a ≲ 10⁻⁵，在 Néel 温度以下呈非单调温度依赖。

Conclusion: MnSi 零场磁结构可用横向圆螺旋理想极限模型（单臂 R3.1'(0,0,g)ts）与单个磁振幅良好描述；单臂与四臂超空间群描述在中子粉末谱上不可区分，需要更高辨别力的测量方能确定真实模型。

Abstract: Combining high-resolution wide-angle neutron powder diffraction with magnetic-superspace symmetry analysis, we refine the crystal structure and present, to our knowledge, the first systematic refinement of cubic $P2_13$ MnSi's zero-field magnetic structure across multiple magnetic satellite families. We compare single-arm rhombohedral 3+1D and cubic four-arm 3+4D magnetic superspace-group (MSSG) descriptions with propagation vectors along $\langle111\rangle$. Simultaneous extinction of the two satellite groups around $\{100\}$ dictates a transverse circular-helix model. This suppression and refinements of 18 separately varied satellite-group intensities support this ideal limit with only one adjustable magnetic amplitude. In the ideal single-arm model with MSSG $R3.1'(0,0,g)ts$, the moment has constant magnitude 0.535(3) $μ_B$ per Mn. An explicit four-arm 3+4D cubic superspace construction presented here combines four interpenetrating helices and, for the illustrated phase choice, has moments ranging from zero to 0.874(5) $μ_B$ per Mn, with the same root-mean-square moment as the 3+1D structure. Their identical powder profiles prevent the present measurements from distinguishing them. The average crystal structure remains cubic within experimental resolution, providing structural motivation for the cubic magnetic model. The mean lattice parameter, determined with formal relative precision $σ_a/a \lesssim 10^{-5}$, exhibits nonmonotonic temperature dependence below the Néel temperature.

</details>


### [105] [Temperature-Driven Evolution of Magnetization Dynamics in Cobalt-Doped Fe$_5$GeTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10225)
*Sourabh Singh,Soumik Aon,Andrew F. May,Joost Ridderbos,Ramanathan Mahendiran,Wilfred G. van der Wiel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用宽带铁磁共振(FMR)波谱研究了钴掺杂Fe₅GeTe₂(CFGT)单晶的温度相关磁化动力学，发现约200 K处出现由单模向双模响应的动力学转变，揭示了体磁测量无法察觉的动态交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解金属范德瓦尔斯铁磁体的磁化动力学，对于揭示其磁相互作用并评估其在高频自旋电子学中的潜力至关重要；而化学掺杂体系(如钴掺杂Fe₅GeTe₂)的动态磁学行为尚缺乏系统表征。

Method: 采用宽带铁磁共振(FMR)光谱对钴掺杂Fe₅GeTe₂单晶进行温度依赖(25 K至室温)测量，通过拟合共振场与线宽随频率的变化，提取有效g因子、有效Gilbert阻尼参数及有效磁各向异性等动态参数。

Result: 高温下FMR响应由单一宽共振主导，降温时共振峰向低场移动；室温附近有效g因子约2.45、有效Gilbert阻尼参数约0.15。有效磁各向异性随温度强烈演化，冷却至200 K附近面内(易平面)贡献逐渐增强。低于约200 K时出现额外共振，形成双模响应并持续至最低测量温度25 K。两支共振的共振场与线宽呈现不同的温度依赖关系，且额外模式的出现并未伴随静态磁化强度的明显异常。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了CFGT中存在一种体磁测量难以察觉的动态交叉(dynamical crossover)，并凸显了FMR对化学取代范德瓦尔斯铁磁体磁学行为的高灵敏探测能力，为其高频自旋电子学应用提供了依据。

Abstract: Understanding magnetization dynamics in metallic van der Waals ferromagnets is essential to uncover their magnetic interactions and evaluate their potential for high-frequency spintronics applications. In this study, we investigate the temperature-dependent magnetization dynamics of a single crystal of cobalt-doped $Fe_{5}GeTe_{2}$ (CFGT) using broadband ferromagnetic resonance (FMR) spectroscopy. At high temperatures, the FMR response is dominated by a single broad resonance that shifts toward lower magnetic fields upon cooling. Fits to the frequency dependence of the resonance field and linewidth yield an effective $g$ factor of approximately 2.45 and an effective Gilbert damping parameter of approximately 0.15 near room temperature. The effective magnetic anisotropy evolves strongly with temperature, with an increasingly pronounced easy-plane contribution upon cooling toward 200 K. Below approximately 200 K, an additional resonance emerges and evolves into a distinct two-mode response that persists to the lowest measured temperature of 25 K. The two branches exhibit different temperature dependences of their resonance fields and linewidths, and the emergence of the additional mode is not accompanied by a comparably pronounced anomaly in the static magnetization. These results reveal a dynamical crossover in CFGT that is not readily apparent from bulk magnetometry and highlight the sensitivity of FMR to the magnetic behavior of chemically substituted van der Waals ferromagnets.

</details>


### [106] [Schottky barrier height modulation and memristive behavior in a mechanically exfoliated $MoS_2$ flake with multiple lateral electrodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12484)
*Gabriel Caballero,Ramón Bernardo-Gavito,Mariela Menghini,Daniel Granados*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于机械剥离少层 MoS₂ 薄片的两端横向忆阻器，重点考察肖特基势垒高度(SBH)随接触面积的变化规律，及其对忆阻行为和电阻开关特性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华材料（尤其是过渡金属二硫属化物 TMDs 等低维材料）表现出忆阻行为，有望用于阻变存储器和神经形态计算，但肖特基势垒高度这一接触工程参数对 MoS₂ 忆阻特性的影响尚不明确，需要系统研究。

Method: 制备基于机械剥离少层 MoS₂ 薄片的两端横向忆阻器；在同一 MoS₂ 薄片上测量不同接触面积下的肖特基势垒高度；通过 J-V_DS 特性曲线分析 SBH 对忆阻行为及非易失/易失性电阻开关的影响。

Result: 在同一 MoS₂ 薄片上观测到从 60 meV 到 208 meV 不等的肖特基势垒高度；SBH 显著影响 J-V_DS 曲线的忆阻特性和观测到的非易失/易失性电阻开关行为。

Conclusion: 接触工程（通过调控肖特基势垒高度）对获得 MoS₂ 基器件中不同的忆阻特性至关重要，为优化阻变存储和神经形态计算器件提供了指导。

Abstract: Van de Waals materials possess great potential as core active materials for novel electronics, optoelectronics, and sensing applications. In particular, transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) and other low-dimensional materials, such as hexagonal boron nitride and binary oxides exhibit memristive behavior, making them attractive candidates as building blocks for resistive memories and neuromorphic computing devices. In this work, we investigate two-terminal lateral memristors based on mechanically exfoliated few layer $MoS_2$ flakes. We study the influence of the Schottky barrier height (SBH) as a function of the contact area. We report different SBHs from 60 meV up to 208 meV in the same $MoS_2$ flake and analyze the effect of the SBH in the memristive-like $J-V_{DS}$ curves and the observed non-volatile/volatile resistance switching. Our results highlight the importance of contact engineering to obtain different memristive properties in $MoS_2$-based devices.

</details>


### [107] [A Transport-Constrained Gradient-Flow Description of Continuum Dislocation Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10232)
*Paria Behnoud,Anthony Rollett,Carlos Mora-Corral,Kaushik Dayal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了连续位错动力学的梯度流框架，引入输运约束的可容许速率，并通过约束昂萨格原理变分选取传输速度，同时具备变分梯度流结构与几何位错守恒律。模型给出的位错驱动力包含弹性应力（Peach-Koehler力）、核心能和非凸晶格能贡献，并用简化模型研究了异号位错对的能量与动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的位错演化模型通常仅具备变分梯度流结构或几何位错守恒律二者之一，缺乏同时满足两者的统一框架。作者旨在为连续位错动力学建立一个既有变分梯度流结构、又与几何位错守恒律一致的完整模型。

Method: 以位错密度和相容滑移场为主要状态变量，其可容许速率受几何守恒律限制，再通过约束昂萨格原理（constrained Onsager principle）变分选取相应的传输速度，从而构建输运约束下的梯度流框架。该热力学结构给出包含弹性应力、核心能和非凸晶格能贡献的位错驱动力。

Result: 新模型同时具备变分梯度流结构和几何位错守恒一致性。简化模型中，晶格能使位错核心保持紧凑；外加剪切应力在低于阈值时促进异号位错对湮灭，高于阈值时则使位错对分离。

Conclusion: 该梯度流框架成功统一了变分梯度流结构与几何位错守恒律，为连续位错动力学提供了热力学一致的基础，并揭示了核心能、非凸晶格能与弹性应力在位错驱动中的共同作用。

Abstract: Gradient flow frameworks are fundamental to numerous models of defect and microstructure evolution. This work develops a gradient flow formulation for continuum dislocation dynamics, with the key distinction that the admissible rates are transport-constrained. Using the dislocation density and compatible slip fields as the primary state variables, their admissible rates are restricted by geometric conservation laws. The corresponding transport velocities are then selected variationally through a constrained Onsager principle. The resulting model has both a variational gradient flow structure and consistency with the geometric dislocation conservation statement; existing models of dislocation evolution typically have either one or the other feature.
  This thermodynamic structure provides a dislocation driving force that includes elastic stress contributions -- corresponding to the classical Peach--Koehler force -- as well as contributions from the core and nonconvex lattice energies. A simplified version of the model is used to study the energetics and dynamics of an opposite-sign dislocation pair. The lattice energy keeps the cores compact, and an external applied shear either assists annihilation or, above a threshold, separates the pair.

</details>


### [108] [Finite temperature and pressure structural relaxations including zero-point motion and anharmonicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12736)
*Henricus R. A. ten Eikelder,Florian Knoop,Florian Trybel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 WARP 方法，一种基于随机温度相关有效势、受 Wyckoff 约束的变胞弛豫技术，可在压力和温度下最小化吉布斯自由能，并保持（或提升）晶体对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限温度和压力下，晶体的平衡结构应最小化吉布斯自由能而非静态 Born-Oppenheimer 能量；现有弛豫方法难以在引入非谐性和零点运动的同时保持晶体对称性约束。

Method: 提出 WARP（Wyckoff-constrained Anharmonic Relaxations at Pressure and temperature）方法：基于随机温度相关有效势（stochastic temperature-dependent effective potential, sTDEP）方法，进行受对称性约束的变胞弛豫。每次位置更新投影到 Wyckoff 位置，每次应变投影到对称应变，因此弛豫只能提升对称性而不会降低对称性；同时包含非谐性和零点运动。

Result: 铝的热膨胀在低温下与实验一致，包括 50 K 以下的量子平台；兆巴压力下镧氢化物的状态方程与基于声子和路径积分计算吻合，LaH10 从其菱方静态极小值弛豫到实验观测的立方相（使用在 150 个超胞上微调的机器学习势也能实现）；水镁石 Mg(OD)2 的晶格参数、键长和热膨胀与中子衍射一致，并捕捉到 O-D 同位素效应。

Conclusion: WARP 为有限温度和压力下的晶体结构弛豫提供了稳健、对称性保持的方案，在金属、高压氢化物和氢键体系上均验证了其准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: At finite temperature and pressure, the equilibrium structure of a crystal minimizes the Gibbs free energy rather than the static Born-Oppenheimer energy. We present WARP (Wyckoff-constrained Anharmonic Relaxations at Pressure and temperature), a symmetry-constrained, variable-cell relaxation based on the stochastic temperature-dependent effective potential method, including anharmonicity and zero-point motion. Every position update is projected onto Wyckoff positions and every strain onto symmetric strains, so a relaxation can raise the symmetry but never lower it. For aluminum, the thermal expansion follows measurements at cryogenic temperatures, including the quantum plateau below 50 K. For lanthanum hydrides at megabar pressures, equations of state agree with phonon-based and path-integral calculations, and LaH$_{10}$ relaxes from its rhombohedral static minimum to the observed cubic phase, also with a machine-learned potential fine-tuned on 150 supercells. For brucite, Mg(OD)$_2$, lattice parameters, bond distances and thermal expansion agree with neutron diffraction, and the O-D isotope effect is captured.

</details>


### [109] [Atomically abrupt sp-sp$^2$ all-carbon heterojunction preserving the electronic identity of constituent allotropes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10272)
*Alice Cartoceti,Masoumeh Alihosseini,Gianni Conti,Eliecer Pelaez-Sifonte,Alessio Orbelli Biroli,Francesco Sedona,Paolo D'Agosta,Francesco Tumino,Andrea Li Bassi,Jorge Lobo-Checa,Carlo S. Casari,Simona Achilli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在Au(111)表面通过Fe介导的分级表面合成，首次实现了sp²扶手椅型石墨烯纳米带（GNR）与sp-sp²氢化石墨双炔（hGDY）之间的全碳横向共价异质结，并揭示了其原子级突变的非对称type-I界面及电子结构变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将不同碳同素异形体以原子精度共价集成目前仍难以实现，但联合互补功能同时保持各自电子特性的优势明显，因此亟需探索全碳横向异质结的可控构筑方法。

Method: 在Au(111)衬底上，通过Fe介导的分级表面合成（hierarchical on-surface synthesis）实现sp²扶手椅型GNR与sp-sp²氢化石墨双炔的横向共价连接；结合低温扫描隧道显微镜（LT-STM）、扫描隧道谱（STS）与密度泛函理论（DFT）对异质结界面进行表征与计算。

Result: 获得了原子级突变（界面过渡区<0.5 nm）的非对称type-I界面。边缘局域化的GNR态会离域到第一个hGDY单元，表现出局域电子连续性，而两者的特征电子结构在界面之外立即重新出现。计算还表明，异质结的形成削弱了两种组分与Au(111)衬底的相互作用，从而拓宽了其带隙并降低了吸附引起的界面极化。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了横向共价集成作为在全碳纳米结构中构筑原子级精确定义的电子界面并调控衬底耦合的有效途径。

Abstract: Covalently integrating distinct carbon allotropes with atomic precision remains, to date, elusive, despite the clear advantage of combining complementary functionalities while preserving their electronic identities. Here, we realize an all-carbon lateral heterojunction combining ($sp^2$) armchair graphene nanoribbons (GNRs) and $sp$-$sp^2$ hydrogenated graphdiyne (hGDY) on Au(111) through Fe-mediated hierarchical on-surface synthesis. Low-temperature scanning tunnelling microscopy and spectroscopy combined with density-functional theory reveal an atomically abrupt (<0.5 nm), asymmetric type-I interface. Redistribution of an edge-localized GNR state onto the first hGDY unit demonstrates local electronic continuity, whereas their characteristic electronic structures re-emerge immediately beyond the interface. Calculations further indicate that heterojunction formation weakens the interaction of both constituents with the underlying Au(111) substrate, widening their gaps and reducing adsorption-induced interfacial polarization. These results establish lateral covalent integration as a route to engineer atomically defined electronic interfaces and substrate coupling in all-carbon nanostructures.

</details>


### [110] [Fabrication and Characterization of Flexible In2O3 Thin-Film Transistors on Mica Substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10354)
*Hyeonjun Kong,Ping-Hsein Wu,Xian-Ye Jiang,Yasutaka Matsuo,Ying-Hao Chu,Hiromichi Ohta*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文证明以云母（mica）为柔性衬底，可在无需高温塑料衬底的情况下制造高迁移率（约75 cm²/Vs）的多晶In2O3基柔性薄膜晶体管；器件耐受向外弯曲（flex-out）和湿热环境，但向内弯曲（flex-in）会导致早期失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代柔性显示器需要高场效应迁移率（uFE > 70 cm²/Vs）的柔性氧化物半导体TFT。多晶In2O3基TFT很有前景，但其制造通常需要约350°C高温，而塑料等常规柔性衬底不耐此温度。

Method: 采用云母作为柔性衬底来制造多晶In2O3基TFT（受此前在云母上生长结晶氧化物的工作启发）。对器件进行5 mm半径的向外弯曲（flex-out）与向内弯曲（flex-in）循环测试（10³次），以及85°C、40%相对湿度下一天的湿热测试。

Result: 所制TFT的场效应迁移率uFE约75 cm²/Vs。器件在5 mm半径下经过10³次向外弯曲循环后仍保持栅极调制工作；而向内弯曲循环导致早期电气失效，可能源于In2O3薄膜退化。器件在85°C、40%相对湿度下暴露一天后仍正常工作。

Conclusion: 云母衬底可实现高迁移率且兼容向外弯曲的In2O3基TFT，为下一代柔性显示器提供了可行的衬底方案。

Abstract: Flexible oxide-semiconductor thin-film transistors (TFTs) with high field-effect mobility (uFE > 70 cm^2/Vs) are required for next-generation flexible displays. Polycrystalline In2O3-based TFTs are promising candidates; however, conventional flexible substrates such as plastics are not suitable because their fabrication typically requires relatively high temperatures (~350 deg.C). In this study, we used mica as a flexible substrate to fabricate polycrystalline In2O3-based TFTs, motivated by previous demonstrations of crystalline oxide growth on mica. The resulting TFTs exhibited a uFE of approximately 75 cm^2/Vs. The devices maintained gate-modulated operation after 10^3 flex-out bending cycles at a 5 mm radius, whereas flex-in cycling caused early electrical failure, likely due to In2O3 film degradation. The TFTs also operated normally after exposure to 85 deg.C and 40% relative humidity for one day.
