These results demonstrate that mica substrates enable high-mobility, flex-out-compatible In2O3-based TFTs.

</details>


### [111] [Multifractality in sputtered crystalline and amorphous transition metal nitride thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10427)
*K. Anantha Padmanabhan,Kuraganti Vasu,P. Manimaran,M. Ghanashyam Krishna*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同衬底上氮化钛(TiN)和氮化铌(NbN)薄膜中的多重分形生长，发现枝晶和雪花状等多重分形结构，并利用2D MFDFA分析证实其多重分形特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究氮化物薄膜中自相似多重分形结构的形成机制，以及衬底类型和工艺参数如何调控分形维数，以深入理解薄膜生长过程中的形态不稳定性与扩散限制聚集(DLA)的竞争关系。

Method: 在不同衬底（晶体衬底与非晶衬底的多种组合）上制备TiN和NbN薄膜，通过扫描电子显微镜观察枝晶和雪花状结构，并采用二维多重分形去趋势波动分析(2D MFDFA)对SEM图像进行分析，统计矩取值在-5到+5之间，计算Hausdorff维数。

Result: 实验证明分形生长既出现在晶体/非晶衬底上的晶体薄膜中，也出现在晶体衬底上的非晶薄膜中。TiN薄膜的Hausdorff维数在1.65至1.79之间，NbN薄膜在1.64至1.74之间，2D MFDFA分析明确揭示了这些氮化物薄膜的多重分形特性。

Conclusion: 氮化物薄膜中的多重分形生长是由形态不稳定性和扩散限制聚集(DLA)机制之间的竞争所导致的，分形维数可通过工艺参数与膜-衬底界面条件的组合加以调控。

Abstract: In this work, we present evidence for multifractal growth in Titanium and Niobium nitride thin films on different substrates. Self-similar multifractal structures such as dendrites and multi-branched snowflake-like structures are reported. The fractal dimensions are controlled by a combination of processing parameters and conditions at the film-substrate interface. Significantly, fractal growth is observed not only in crystalline films on crystalline and amorphous substrates but also on amorphous films on crystalline substrates. The 2D Multifractal Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (2D MFDFA) carried out on the scanning electron micrographs, clearly reveals the multifractal nature of the nitride thin films. The statistical moments were varied between -5 to +5. The Hausdorff dimensions for the TiN films are in the range of 1.65 to 1.79 while it is 1.64 to 1.74 for the NbN thin films. It is proposed that the observed multifractality is due to a competition between morphological instabilities and Diffusion-Limited Aggregation

</details>


### [112] [Dopant Cluster Engineering for Tunable Room-Temperature Ferromagnetism in 2D Transition Metal Dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10480)
*Shivangee,Sachin Grewal,Akash Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用DFT+U系统研究了过渡金属掺杂的2D-MoSe₂和2D-WSe₂的热力学稳定性与磁性，发现成对掺杂比孤立掺杂更稳定，并实现了大磁各向异性能与高居里温度，为2D-TMDs中的高温铁磁性工程化提供了掺杂团簇的新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维过渡金属硫族化合物（2D-TMDs）是实现稀磁半导体的理想平台，但同时实现大磁各向异性能（MAE）和高居里温度（Tc）仍是重大挑战，亟需探究有效的掺杂策略以同时提升这两项关键磁性指标。

Method: 采用DFT+U方法，系统研究过渡金属原子掺杂的2D-MoSe₂和2D-WSe₂的热力学稳定性与磁性质；重点考察成对掺杂构型的交换耦合、自旋轨道耦合、轨道杂化对磁各向异性能的影响，并利用蒙特卡罗模拟估算居里温度Tc。

Result: 成对掺杂构型比此前认为的孤立掺杂更稳定；2D-MoSe₂中所有成对掺杂均表现出主要由载流子介导交换作用驱动的长程铁磁耦合（FMC），而2D-WSe₂则表现为弱交换耦合；其中2Mn₂W由短程稳定性较差的铁磁→反铁磁耦合（AFMC）转变为长程高度稳定的FMC。自旋轨道耦合、交换作用与轨道杂化的协同效应产生大MAE，最大面内值为2V₂Mo的−3.5 meV和2Mn₂W的−3.2 meV；蒙特卡罗模拟估算2Fe₂Mo的Tc≈440 K，确保其在2D-MoSe₂中的高温稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究确立了掺杂团簇作为在2D-TMDs中工程化高温铁磁性的有效途径，并凸显了其在纳米自旋电子学应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Two-dimensional transition metal dichalcogenides (2D-TMDs) offer a promising platform for realizing dilute magnetic semiconductors. However, simultaneously achieving large magnetic anisotropy energy (MAE) and high Curie temperature (T$_\text{c}$) remains a major challenge. In this work, we employ DFT+U to systematically investigate the thermodynamic stability and magnetic properties of 2D-MoSe$_2$ and 2D-WSe$_2$ doped with transition metal atoms. We found that the paired dopant configurations are more stable than previously attributed isolated dopants. Despite their nearly identical crystal structures, 2D-MoSe$_2$ and 2D-WSe$_2$ show contrasting properties upon doping. All paired dopants in 2D-MoSe$_2$ exhibit long-range ferromagnetic coupling (FMC) primarily due to carrier-mediated exchange interactions, whereas those in 2D-WSe$_2$ show weak exchange coupling. Interestingly, 2Mn$_\text{2W}$ shows a transition from less stable antiferromagnetic coupling (AFMC) in the short range to highly stable FMC in the long-range. The combined effect of spin-orbit coupling, exchange interactions, and orbital hybridization gives rise to a large MAE with maximum in-plane values of $-$3.5 meV for 2V$_\text{2Mo}$ and $-$3.2 meV for 2Mn$_\text{2W}$. Monte Carlo simulations estimate T$_\text{c}$ $\simeq$ 440 K for 2Fe$_\text{2Mo}$ and ensure its high-temperature stability in 2D-MoSe$_2$. Our results establish dopant clustering as an effective route to engineer high-temperature ferromagnetism in 2D-TMDs and highlight their potential for nano-spintronic applications.

</details>


### [113] [Scalable MoS2/Si Photodiode Arrays from Roll-to-Roll Mechanical Exfoliation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10696)
*Yigit Sozen,Thomas Pucher,Andres Castellanos-Gomez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全干法、可规模化制备 MoS2/Si 光电二极管的路线，利用高通量卷对卷机械剥离获得大横向尺寸 MoS2 纳米片致密薄膜，并将其转移至局部刻蚀的 Si 上形成工作在光伏模式下的异质结，实现了自供电的快速宽谱光探测器及成像阵列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溶液法制备的薄膜在致密性、尺寸均匀性与规模化等方面存在局限，难以较好服务于低成本、自供电的光探测与成像应用；因此需要一种可扩展、全干法的薄膜制备与器件集成路线，以克服这些限制。

Method: 采用高通量卷对卷机械剥离技术制备大横向尺寸 MoS2 纳米片的致密薄膜，并将其转移到局部刻蚀的 Si 区域上形成 MoS2/Si 异质结；器件在光伏（自供电）状态下工作。进一步制作了线阵像素阵列用于位置感知，以及 4×4 阵列作为成像传感器的原理验证。

Result: 所制器件表现出稳健、快速的光响应和宽波段灵敏度；线阵实现了位置感知能力，4×4 阵列验证了成像功能，表明该卷对卷机械剥离路线能够克服溶液法薄膜的常见限制。

Conclusion: 卷对卷机械剥离是一种可扩展的全干法方案，能够实现低成本、自供电的光电子探测器，在传感与成像应用中具有良好前景。

Abstract: We present a scalable, all-dry route to MoS2/Si photodiodes based on high-throughput roll-to-roll mechanical exfoliation. The method yields dense films of large-lateral-size MoS2 nanosheets and enables the formation of heterojunctions that operate in the photovoltaic regime when transferred onto locally etched Si regions. Individual devices show robust, fast photoresponses and broadband wavelength sensitivity. We further implemented a linear pixel array demonstrating position sensing capability and a 4x4 array as a proof-of-concept imaging sensor. These results indicate that roll-to-roll mechanical exfoliation can overcome common limitations of solution-processed films to enable low-cost, self-powered optoelectronic detectors for sensing and imaging applications.

</details>


### [114] [A Geometry-Guided Feature-Space Framework for Interpretable Unsupervised 4D-STEM Analysis of Complex Multiphase Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10853)
*Arda Genc,Daniel A. Bolden,Albert Nguyen,Daniel S. Gianola,Ravit Silverstein*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合物理信息几何描述符与加权高维嵌入的机器学习框架，用于对非均质材料的4D-STEM数据集进行快速、高保真聚类分析，并在Si/SiGe外延层和多相Ti-Nb合金上验证了其优于vanilla自编码器和无模型模式向量化的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D-STEM数据集因共存相、取向变体、局域应变梯度和TEM薄膜效应而产生复杂重叠的衍射信号，使得非均质材料的快速高保真分析极具挑战，需要一种在最小先验知识下自动识别的可靠方法。

Method: 提出一种机器学习框架：先构建正则化的物理信息几何描述符空间，再进行加权高维嵌入和基于密度的聚类，以识别潜在结构模式。系统比较了空间加权和旋转加权对聚类稳定性、噪声占比及密度分离度（DBCV）的影响。

Result: 空间加权将中位聚类持续性从0.04提升至0.55，并将噪声占比从约22%降至0%；旋转加权将DBCV从0.80提升至0.92，但噪声占比从0.2%升至1.0%，体现出密度分离改善与噪声剔除之间的权衡。在Si/SiGe外延层和Ti-Nb合金上与预训练vanilla自编码器及无模型模式向量化对比，物理信息描述符具有更高的聚类保真度、更强的可解释性和显著更低的维度。

Conclusion: 物理信息几何描述符结合加权高维嵌入和密度聚类，能在低先验知识下稳定识别4D-STEM数据中的潜在结构模式，兼具高聚类保真度、可解释性和低维优势，为复杂非均质材料的自动化分析提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Rapid, high-fidelity analysis of four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D-STEM) datasets from heterogeneous materials remains challenging because coexisting phases, orientation variants, local strain gradients, and intrinsic TEM thin-foil effects produce complex, overlapping diffraction signatures. Here, we present a machine-learning (ML) framework that combines physics-informed geometric descriptors with weighted high-dimensional embeddings to identify latent structural patterns with minimal prior knowledge. By constructing a regularized descriptor space before embedding and density-based clustering, the method improves cluster stability, suppresses noise-driven fragmentation, and reduces sensitivity to embedding hyperparameters. Spatial weighting produced the largest stability gain, increasing median cluster persistence from 0.04 to 0.55 while reducing the noise fraction from approximately 22% to 0%. Rotation weighting increased DBCV from 0.80 to 0.92, although the noise fraction rose from 0.2% to 1.0%, highlighting a trade-off between improved density separation and noise rejection. We validate the framework on two benchmark systems: Si/Si$_x$Ge$_{1-x}$ epitaxial layers with continuous lattice variation and deformation, and multiphase Ti-Nb alloys containing distinct phases and orientation variants. Comparisons with a pretrained vanilla autoencoder and model-free pattern vectorization show that the physics-informed geometric descriptor provides higher cluster fidelity, greater interpretability, and substantially lower dimensionality.

</details>


### [115] [A data-driven approach for systematic correction of electronic bandgaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10889)
*Pamela C. Carvalho,Asa Marif,Erna K. Delczeg-Czirjak,Sergei Simak,Utkarsh Singh,Olle Eriksson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文证明，通过对密度泛函理论（DFT）计算得到的带隙施加单一的机器学习修正，可使带隙接近实验值，从而解决高效电子结构方法难以给出可靠带隙的经典难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效电子结构方法（如DFT）长期以来存在的经典问题是：使用常用能量泛函计算得到的带隙与实验值存在系统偏差，难以直接与实验对比；而更精确的GW近似或杂化泛函计算代价过高，无法大规模应用于材料设计流程。

Method: 采用针对材料特定设计的机器学习特征工程方法，对两种最常用能量泛函的DFT计算结果施加单一的带隙修正，训练机器学习模型来预测并校正带隙。

Result: 经机器学习修正后的带隙与实验值非常接近，其精度可与计算成本更高的GW近似或杂化泛函方法相媲美。

Conclusion: 该工作使高效计算可应用于需要可靠带隙的材料设计工作流，对面向大型计算数据库的研究或纯预测性质的研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: We demonstrate that a single machine-learning correction, applied to bandgaps from density functional theory calculations with two of the most frequently used energy functionals, results in bandgaps that are close to the experimental values. This solves a classical problem of computationally efficient electronic structure methods, to give reliable bandgaps that can be compared to experiments. The employed approach relies on materials specific feature engineering of machine learning models, and is shown to have close accuracy to that of much more expensive calculations based on the GW-approximation or hybrid functionals. The findings reported here open the possibility to use efficient calculations in materials design workflows that need reliable bandgaps, with implications for investigations targeting large computational databases or for investigations that are entirely predictive in nature.

</details>


### [116] [Lifting mechanical zero modes without dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10937)
*Joohan Nam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究软框架（floppy frameworks）中零频机制平带如何被在位张量（on-site tensor）抬升，并给出不产生一阶色散的精确条件；通过局部正则模、不变对称扇区和平衡律三种结构，解释了众多平带的来源，并以氟石网络、对偶铺陈和可打印梁框架晶格为例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软框架携带的是零频机制的平带，没有有限频率共振；通常耦合会使这些平带展开产生色散。作者希望弄清：什么样的在位张量能在抬升机制带的同时保持其平带性（无最低阶色散），以及这种平带背后的几何与对称性判据是什么。

Method: 作者分析投影到机制子空间上的在位张量，证明当它在该子空间上表现为对正交分块分别取波矢无关标量的形式时，机制带才可被抬升而不出现一阶色散。归纳出三种实现结构：(1) 局部正则模（local normal modes）、(2) 不变对称扇区（invariant symmetry sectors）、(3) 平衡律（balance law）。平衡律要求机制在连接两个顶点类的每条键两端沿键方向等量运动，且类间键张量为类标量时，机制带抬升值的范数份额乃至平带值可仅由几何确定。并以氟石网络（平带处于张量谱之外，为一阶接触刚度的14/9倍，且在固定笼架中到所有阶都保持平带）、保持共同对称性的对偶铺陈嵌入，以及可打印梁框架晶格中锁定节点转动的接触为实例。

Result: 证明机制平带被抬升且无一阶色散当且仅当投影后的在位张量在各正交分块上为波矢无关标量。氟石网络平带在固定笼架中到所有阶均保持平带，一阶值为接触刚度的14/9。十个编录的对偶对中半数可抬升为平带，其中一半是特征值情形且与计数一致；平衡律还能解释随机笼架中的平带。对偶性使刚性笼架成为共同条件。在可打印梁框架晶格中，锁定节点转动且键合机制为正交局部正则模时，一阶弯曲色散被消除。

Conclusion: 在位张量不引入最低阶色散地抬升机制平带的充要条件是它投影到机制上后在正交分块上取波矢无关标量；局部正则模、不变对称扇区与平衡律三种结构足以覆盖并解释各类平带实例，且该机制从几何、对称性直到随机笼架和可打印梁框架晶格中都保持一致的有效性。

Abstract: Floppy frameworks carry flat bands of zero-frequency mechanisms with no finite-frequency resonance, and couplings that lift them usually spread them. We show that an on-site tensor lifts a mechanism band without first-order dispersion exactly when, projected onto the mechanisms, it acts as wavevector-independent scalars on orthogonal pieces. Three structures achieve this: local normal modes, invariant symmetry sectors, and a balance law. A mechanism moves both ends of every bond between two vertex classes equally along it, and where the inter-class bond tensors are class scalars this balance law fixes its norm shares and hence the flat value from geometry alone. For the fluorite net the band lies outside the tensor spectrum, at 14/9 of the contact stiffness to first order, and stays flat to all orders in a fixed cage. A placement inside a tiling dual that keeps the common symmetry supplies such tensors, because each contact star inherits its site symmetry. Ten catalogued dual pairs lift flat; half are eigenvalue cases a resolved count matches, and the balance law explains flat bands in random cages too. Duality makes the rigid cage common. In a printable beam-frame lattice, contacts that lock joint rotation remove first-order bending dispersion whenever bonded mechanisms are orthogonal local normals.

</details>


### [117] [In-Band Boundary Resonances from Coalescing Zero-Group-Velocity Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10939)
*Amir Rajabpoor Alisepahi,Charles A. Downing,Jihong Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在互易、拓扑平凡的厄米晶格中实现带内边界共振的新机制：通过两个零群速度（ZGV）点的合并，使群速度与逆有效质量同时消失，从而在传播谱内产生边界增强型共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界态通常只存在于带隙中，而带内局域通常需要与传播模式精确解耦。作者希望找到一种无需精细调谐或拓扑保护、在互易厄米体系中实现带内边界共振的通用体态机制。

Method: 作者提出零群速度（ZGV）点合并机制：在合并点处群速度和逆有效质量同时为零，对应 Bloch 根具有更高重数；合并后的根仍为传播模式，而邻近的根分裂产生弱衰逝通道，配合体匹配的截断端，在传播谱内形成边界加权共振。该机制首先在两腿梯子模型中通过时间反演相关的 ZGV 合并解析实现，并推广到由长程相互作用产生的不同 ZGV 合并。通过有限尺寸标度区分该共振与扩展态和缺陷束缚态，并在 3D 打印弹性梯子上用扫描激光多普勒测振仪验证。

Result: 解析和数值结果表明 ZGV 合并可在传播谱内产生边界增强共振；有限尺寸标度能够将其与扩展态和缺陷束缚态区分开；实验在近平带区域观测到边界增强，与略低于临界点（亚临界）操作一致。

Conclusion: 零群速度合并为互易厄米晶格中的带内边界共振提供了一个基于体态的统一框架，无需拓扑保护或精确解耦即可实现带内边界增强。

Abstract: Boundary states generally reside in band gaps, whereas in-band confinement typically requires exact decoupling from propagating modes. We demonstrate an alternative mechanism in reciprocal, topologically trivial Hermitian lattices arising from the coalescence of two zero-group-velocity (ZGV) points. At coalescence, the group velocity and inverse effective mass vanish simultaneously, and the corresponding Bloch root acquires higher multiplicity. The coalesced root remains propagating, whereas nearby root splitting yields weakly evanescent channels that, together with a bulk-matched termination, generate a boundary-weighted resonance within the propagating spectrum. The mechanism is realized analytically in a two-leg ladder model through the coalescence of time-reversal-related ZGVs and extends to distinct ZGVs produced by longer-range interactions. Finite-size scaling distinguishes the resulting resonance from extended and defect-bound states. Scanning laser Doppler vibrometry on a 3D-printed elastic ladder reveals boundary enhancement in a nearly flat band region, consistent with operation slightly below criticality. ZGV coalescence thus provides a bulk framework for in-band boundary resonances in reciprocal Hermitian lattices.

</details>


### [118] [Deintercalation Equilibria Control the Composition, Structure, and Electronic Quality in the Synthesis of 2$M$-WS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10941)
*Brenna C. Bierman,Taeran Yang,Jon Ramos,Sara Yarworth,James Lazarcik,Daniel A. Rhodes*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了碱金属插层-脱插层（AMID）中脱插层过程，通过34种处理探究K⁺从2M-KₓWS₂中的脱出机理，发现其受热力学控制的氧化还原与离子交换耦合平衡支配，并据此优化处理条件提升2M-WS₂的晶体质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMID是制备亚稳态层状材料（如2M-WS₂）的重要途径，但以往机理研究集中于插层过程，对脱插层如何发生及其对晶体性能的影响了解甚少。

Method: 作者评估了34种针对2M-KₓWS₂的脱插层处理，系统考察溶剂极性、非氧化性冠醚螯合剂、稀碱/中性盐溶液再插层行为、酸化K₂Cr₂O₇处理等因素，并利用X射线衍射、ICP-MS、残余电阻率比（RRR）等手段表征产物组成、结构与电子质量。

Result: 脱插层由氧化还原与离子交换耦合平衡控制，K⁺脱出量随溶剂极性增大而增加，加入冠醚可使脱插层接近完全。2M-WS₂在稀KOH中会自发再插层，而在KCl中不会，表明反应受热力学控制，取决于溶液中K⁺的稳定性。六小时酸化K₂Cr₂O₇处理获得最高平均RRR。所有样品均残留K⁺（ICP-MS定量），且残钾似乎为RRR设置了上限。某些处理未得到纯2M-WS₂，在漂白剂处理的样品中观察到两种新结构。

Conclusion: 脱插层不仅是AMID中的工艺步骤，更是一个机理丰富的反应，可通过合理调控平衡来提高和可控地制备亚稳态材料。

Abstract: Alkali-metal intercalation-deintercalation (AMID) enables access to metastable layered materials such as 2$M$-WS$_2$. Mechanistic studies of AMID have focused on intercalation; how deintercalation proceeds and influences crystal properties is comparatively poorly understood. Here, we assess 34 deintercalation treatments of 2$M$-K$_x$WS$_2$ and find that K$^+$ removal proceeds through coupled redox and ion-exchange equilibria, whose positions set the composition, structure, and electronic quality of the products. Across a series of neat solvents, K$^+$ deintercalation amount increased with solvent polarity and was driven to near-completion upon addition of nonoxidizing crown ether chelators, regardless of solvent polarity. Spontaneous reintercalation occurred when 2$M$-WS$_2$ was placed in dilute aqueous solutions of the strong base KOH but not the neutral salt KCl. These results suggest that the extent of deintercalation is governed by the stability of K$^+$ in solution, rather than oxidant strength alone, consistent with a reaction under thermodynamic control. Treatments that bias the deintercalation equilibrium most strongly toward the products while avoiding deleterious side reactions produce the highest quality 2$M$-WS$_2$. A six-hour treatment with acidified K$_2$Cr$_2$O$_7$ gave the highest average residual resistivity ratio ($\overline{RRR}$). Residual K$^+$, quantified by ICP-MS, was found in all treated samples and appeared to impose an upper bound on $\overline{RRR}$. Treatments yielding crystals with $\overline{RRR}$ below this bound did not always afford 2$M$-WS$_2$; we observed two new structures in bleach-treated samples and signatures of alternative phases under other conditions. This work demonstrates that deintercalation is not merely a processing step in AMID but a mechanistically rich reaction that might be rationally tuned to improve and control metastable materials.

</details>


### [119] [Efficient Plane-to-Point Energy Transfer from Supramolecular Excitonic Systems to Molecular Acceptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11000)
*Dimuthu H. Thanippuli Arachchi,Oliver M. Nix,Jianshu Cao,Moungi G. Bawendi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文展示了在二氧化硅封装的二维 TDBC J-聚集体片与共价连接的 ATTO 590 分子受体之间实现高效能量转移，效率达 75-81%，并发展了适用于扩展平面供体到局域分子受体的广义 FRET 模型，理论效率约 91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用超分子组装体中的集体激子相互作用实现高效光捕获和定向能量传输，并解决供体平面与受体间的受控耦合问题。

Method: 采用二氧化硅封装二维 TDBC J-聚集体片以保持激子性质，并在与供体平面既定距离处共价连接 ATTO 590 受体；通过稳态荧光淬灭、受体敏化及时间分辨光致发光测量表征，并建立扩展平面供体耦合局域分子受体的广义 Forster 共振能量转移模型。

Result: 溶液中能量转移效率为 75-81%，供体寿命缩短；模型预测转移速率与供体-受体间距呈反四次方依赖关系，计算得转移速率 4.77 x 10^10 s^-1，约为供体本征衰减速率的十倍，理论效率约 91%。

Conclusion: 该工作为发展用于光捕获的混合激子结构提供了框架，阐明了扩展平面供体与局域受体间能量转移的机制与高效性。

Abstract: Collective excitonic interactions in supramolecular assemblies offer opportunities for efficient light harvesting and directed energy transport. Here, we demonstrate efficient energy transfer from two-dimensional silica-encapsulated TDBC J-aggregate sheets to covalently attached ATTO 590 molecular acceptors. Silica encapsulation preserves the J-aggregate excitonic properties while enabling acceptor functionalization at a defined separation from the donor plane. Steady-state donor quenching and acceptor sensitization yield energy-transfer efficiencies of 75-81% in solution, while time-resolved photoluminescence measurements reveal donor lifetime shortening. We develop a generalized Forster resonance energy-transfer model for an extended planar donor coupled to localized molecular acceptors, predicting an inverse fourth-power dependence of the transfer rate on donor-acceptor separation. Using experimental spectroscopic data and estimated structural parameters, the model predicts an energy-transfer rate of 4.77 x 10^10 s^-1, approximately ten times faster than the intrinsic donor decay rate, corresponding to a theoretical energy-transfer efficiency of approximately 91%. These findings provide a framework for developing hybrid excitonic architectures for light harvesting.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Electronic Band Structures Across the s, p, d Blocks with an Empirical Pseudopotential Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11070)
*Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: EpNet 是一种学习型经验赝势模型，能直接从原子坐标预测完整电子能带结构，速度约为 DFT 的 1% 成本，误差仅为现有模型的一半以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子能带结构决定晶体的导电和光学性质，但 DFT 计算需要昂贵的自洽迭代；现有机器学习模型要么只预测孤立的标量性质，要么输出仍需 DFT 求解器处理的哈密顿量。作者希望构建一个能直接从原子坐标输出完整能带结构的模型，摆脱对 DFT 求解器的依赖。

Method: 提出 EpNet，一种覆盖 s、p、d 区共 63 种元素的学习型经验赝势模型。采用等变神经网络，从物理启发的局域势和非局域投影子出发构建哈密顿量，仅以能带能量为训练目标进行训练。

Result: 在未见过的化学体系上，能带能量的中位误差为 \winAll eV，带隙误差为 \gapAll eV。在独立数据库的 60 万个结构上，带隙误差整体低于现有模型的一半，计算成本约为 DFT 的 1%。

Conclusion: EpNet 能够实现材料发现中的电子性质快速筛选以及大体系电子结构模拟，为高通量材料筛选和大规模体系计算提供了高效工具。

Abstract: Electronic band structures govern how crystals conduct electrons and absorb light. Density-functional theory (DFT) computes them through costly self-consistent iterations, while existing machine-learning models either predict isolated scalar properties or output Hamiltonians that still require DFT solvers. Here we present EpNet, a learned empirical pseudopotential for crystals spanning 63 elements across the \textit{s}, \textit{p} and \textit{d} blocks. An equivariant neural network builds its Hamiltonian from physics-motivated local potentials and nonlocal projectors. Trained solely on band energies, EpNet predicts complete band structures directly from atomic coordinates. On unseen chemical systems, its median errors are \winAll\ eV for band energies and \gapAll\ eV for band gaps. On 600,000 structures from an independent database, its band-gap errors are overall less than half those of existing models, at about 1\% of DFT cost. EpNet thus enables fast screening of electronic properties for materials discovery and electronic-structure simulations of large systems.

</details>


### [121] [Effect of Correlation on the Pressure-Induced Insulator to Metal Transition in Complex Spin-Orbit Coupled Double Perovskite La$_2$CoIr$_{1-x}$Ti$_x$O$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11253)
*Vineeta Yadav,Sromona Nandi,Rudra Sekhar Manna*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用DFT第一性原理计算，研究了电子关联(U)和静水压力对双钙钛矿La2CoIr1-xTixO6(x=0,0.5,1)电子性质的影响。结果表明，U增大使带隙增大并稳定绝缘态，而静水压力则通过关闭带隙诱导绝缘体-金属转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索电子关联与静水压力对关联氧化物电子性质的调控机制，为通过电子关联和压力进行材料设计提供理论依据。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论(DFT)框架下的第一性原理电子结构计算。在常压下系统改变现场库仑相互作用(电子关联U)，研究其对带隙和结构参数的影响；同时对三种成分施加静水压力，并跟踪带隙随压力的演化来确定带隙闭合压力。

Result: 增加U值会增大三种成分的带隙，而对结构参数仅产生微小变化。带隙在约39 GPa(x=0)、42 GPa(x=0.5)和53 GPa(x=1)处闭合，对应压力诱导的绝缘体-金属转变。

Conclusion: 电子关联稳定绝缘态，而静水压力通过关闭带隙促进金属态。该研究揭示了电子关联与压力对关联氧化物的可调性，为材料设计提供启示。

Abstract: We study the effect of electron correlation ($U$) and hydrostatic pressure on the electronic properties of double perovskites La$_2$CoIr$_{1-x}$Ti$_x$O$_6$ for $x$ = 0, 0.5, and 1, using first-principles electronic structure calculations within density functional theory (DFT). At ambient pressure, we systematically vary the electron correlation, i.e., on-site Coulomb interaction to study its effect on band gap and structural parameters. Increasing the value of $U$ enhances the band gap of all three compositions while producing only a slight variation in structural parameters. The effect of hydrostatic pressure is also studied for the same compositions. The estimated band gap values at ambient pressure closes at approximately 39, 42, and 53 GPa for $x$ = 0 (for $U_\mathrm{Co}$ = 5 eV and $U_\mathrm{Ir}$ = 2 eV), 0.5 (for $U_\mathrm{Co}$ = 5 eV and $U_\mathrm{Ir}$ = 2 eV), and 1 (for $U_\mathrm{Co}$ = 4 eV), respectively, indicating pressure-induced insulator-to-metal transitions. These results show that electron correlation stabilises the insulating state, whereas hydrostatic pressure favours the metallic state by closing the band gap. These findings highlight the tunability of correlated oxides through electron correlations and pressure, offering insights for materials design.

</details>


### [122] [Engineering ferroelectric nanocapacitors through interfacial phonon bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11256)
*Iñigo Robredo-Magro,Urmimala Dey,Suehyun park,Karam Cho,Raseong Kim,Jorge Íñiguez-González*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出"界面声子偏置"这一新机制，即电极的声子能量景观可跨越界面重塑邻近铁电材料的声子能量景观。作者通过机器学习原子间势和分子动力学模拟，在对称BiFeO3纳米电容器中证明，独立调控电极八面体倾斜取向与幅度，可在固定弹性/电学边界条件下改变BiFeO3竞争相的稳定性、移动铁电转变温度，甚至在室温诱导类反铁电开关行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着铁电器件进入纳米尺度，其行为对边界条件高度敏感。外延应变和静电屏蔽虽已确立为调控参数，但电极的晶格动力学状态（声子能量景观）尚未被作为独立调控手段加以分离研究，尽管其被认为对铁电超薄膜的外延稳定有贡献。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索电极声子态对邻近铁电体的耦合调控作用。

Method: 采用第一性原理精度的机器学习原子间势与分子动力学模拟，研究对称BiFeO3纳米电容器（与金属性钙钛矿氧化物电极接触）。在保持常规弹性与电学边界条件固定的前提下，独立控制电极八面体倾斜景观的取向和幅度，从而分离出"界面声子偏置"这一单一变量，考察其对铁电相稳定性的影响。

Result: 电极的声子能量景观能够改变BiFeO3竞争相的稳定性：可移动铁电转变温度，并能在室温条件下诱导类反铁电开关行为。这证明界面声子偏置是与外延应变、静电屏蔽并列的、可独立调控铁电超薄膜性质的强有力策略。

Conclusion: 界面声子偏置是一种互补且有效的策略，可用于修改和优化超薄外延铁电薄膜的性能，为纳米尺度铁电器件的设计与调控提供了新的自由度。

Abstract: As ferroelectric devices approach the nanometer scale, their behavior becomes increasingly sensitive to boundary conditions. In perovskite oxide nanocapacitors, epitaxial strain and electrostatic screening are well-established control parameters. Then, while expected to contribute to the epitaxial stabilization of ferroelectric ultrathin films, the lattice-dynamical state of the electrodes has not been isolated as an independent tuning knob. Here we call this poorly explored coupling interfacial phonon bias, whereby the electrodes' phonon energy landscape reshapes that of the adjacent ferroelectric across their shared interface. Using first-principles-accurate machine-learned interatomic potentials and molecular-dynamics simulations, we examine this mechanism in symmetric BiFeO${}_3$ nanocapacitors in contact with a metallic perovskite oxide. By independently controlling the orientatio and amplitude of the electrodes' octahedral-tilt landscape, while keeping conventional elastic and electric boundary conditions fixed, we show that the electrodes' phonon energy landscape can modify the stability of competing BiFeO${}_3$ phases. In particular, this allows us to shift the ferroelectric transition temperature and even induce antiferroelectric-like switching at ambient conditions. We thus showcase interfacial phonon bias as a complementary and powerful strategy to modify and optimize the properties of ultrathin epitaxial films.

</details>


### [123] [Strain Controlled Hydrogen Interstitial Site Preference and Hydride Phase Suppression in Epitaxial Vanadium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11276)
*Xin Xiao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合DFT计算与原位电学测量，揭示外延应变如何调控钒中氢的间隙占位选择（优先四面体位）与氢化物相稳定性；实验发现临界膜厚依赖的相变行为转变：50 nm膜呈一阶α–β氢化物相变，10 nm膜因强共格应变抑制成核而呈连续固溶行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解过渡金属中氢的行为对储能与传感技术至关重要，但外延应变如何影响氢的间隙位点选择与相稳定性尚缺乏统一认知，亟需建立从原子尺度轨道物理到介观相热力学的连接。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算解析氢在钒中四面体（T）与八面体（O）间隙位的热力学偏好及应变对H 1s–V 3d轨道杂化的调制；实验上在MgO(001)上外延生长不同厚度（10 nm与50 nm）的钒薄膜，并结合原位电学测量表征氢化过程中的相变行为。

Result: DFT计算表明四面体位在热力学上稳定优于八面体位，其能量差受晶格各向异性通过应变可调的H 1s–V 3d杂化调制；实验显示50 nm膜呈现特征性的一阶α–β氢化物相变，而10 nm膜因强共格应变拉平自由能面、抑制氢化物成核而表现为连续固溶行为且相变完全被抑制。这一转变由膜厚增加导致的外延应变渐进弛豫所主导，并辅以弹性能场的有限尺寸截断与界面电子重构的强化。

Conclusion: 本文建立了连接原子尺度轨道物理与介观相热力学的统一框架，为应变工程化的金属氢化物体系提供了设计准则。

Abstract: Understanding hydrogen behavior in transition metals is crucial for energy storage and sensing technologies. Here we combine density functional theory (DFT) calculations with in-situ electrical measurements to reveal how epitaxial strain governs hydrogen interstitial site selection and phase stability in vanadium. Our calculations demonstrate a robust thermodynamic preference for tetrahedral (T) over octahedral (O) sites, with the energy difference modulated by lattice anisotropy through strain-tunable H 1s-V 3d orbital hybridization. Experimentally, epitaxial V films on MgO(001) exhibit a critical thickness-dependent crossover: 50 nm films show characteristic first-order alpha-beta hydride transitions, while 10 nm films display continuous solid-solution behavior with complete transition suppression. This crossover is governed by the progressive relaxation of epitaxial strain with increasing film thickness: the 10 nm films retain strong coherent strain that flattens the free-energy landscape and suppresses hydride nucleation, whereas the 50 nm films approach the strain-relaxed bulk limit where long-range elastic interactions restore first-order phase behavior. Finite-size truncation of elastic fields and interfacial electronic reconstruction further reinforce this dimensional crossover. Our work establishes a unified framework connecting atomic-scale orbital physics to mesoscopic phase thermodynamics, providing design principles for strain-engineered metal-hydride systems.

</details>


### [124] [sdos: spectral densities of large sparse Hermitian and generalized eigenproblems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11310)
*David A. Drabold*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: sdos 是一个开源 Python 包，通过随机向量估计的 Chebyshev 矩，计算大规模稀疏厄米矩阵（含广义问题 Aψ=λBψ）的态密度及相关谱量，默认用核多项式方法（KPM）或最大熵（Maxent）重建密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大规模稀疏厄米矩阵（电子与振动问题，可到数百万原子/原子轨道）难以用稠密对角化直接求解谱密度的问题，提供一个高效、低内存、可扩展的开源工具，统一计算 DOS、费米能级、局域/投影密度、热力学量、HOMO-LUMO 间隙及谱窗内本征对等。

Method: 用随机向量估计 Chebyshev 矩，再通过核多项式方法（KPM，默认）或最大熵（Maxent）重建态密度；同一接口支持带能、费米能级、局域/投影密度、谐波热力学、谱窗内精确本征对及 HOMO-LUMO 间隙。接口覆盖电子（经验紧束缚、DFTB、SIESTA）和振动（无矩阵 LAMMPS Hessian）问题。作者用稠密对角化或独立代码核验了大部分成分（未核验项列于 Sec. V）。

Result: 在 4 线程、19 GB 工作站上：884,736 原子非晶硅（4096 原子胞的 6³ 平铺，350 万轨道，Kwon 经验紧束缚正交模型）的 DOS 耗时 71 s（M=100, R=2，无精确矩；默认精确矩为 383 s）；无矩阵的 2,097,152 原子晶体耗时 10 min、内存 1.8 GB。216 原子非晶胞的 17³ 复制（1,061,208 原子）与精确 Bloch 谱一致：电子 DOS 229 s，单 GPU 上无矩阵 ACE 振动 DOS 2.2 h。不可扩展的部分：Shift-invert、不完全 Cholesky 预处理、高阶精确矩以及高矩数下的最大熵。底层方法源自 Silver、Roder、Kress、Voter、Drabold、Sankey 及合作者。

Conclusion: sdos 成功将 KPM/最大熵等谱方法工程化为可扩展的开源包，能在工作站甚至单 GPU 上处理数百万原子体系，且与精确谱结果吻合；同时指明了若干不具可扩展性的组件，明确了方法的适用范围与局限。

Abstract: sdos is an open Python package that computes the density of states (DOS) and related spectral quantities of very large sparse Hermitian matrices A, and of generalized problems A psi = lambda B psi, from Chebyshev moments estimated with random vectors. The density is reconstructed by the kernel polynomial method (KPM, the default) or by maximum entropy (Maxent); the same interface gives band energies, Fermi levels, local and projected densities, harmonic thermodynamics, exact HOMO-LUMO gaps and eigenpairs in a spectral window. Interfaces cover electronic (empirical tight-binding, DFTB, SIESTA) and vibrational (matrix-free LAMMPS Hessians) problems. I checked most ingredients against dense diagonalization or an independent code; the exceptions are listed in Sec. V. On a workstation (4 threads, 19 GB) the DOS of an 884,736-atom amorphous silicon model (a 6^3 tiling of a 4096-atom cell; 3.5 million orbitals; the orthogonal empirical tight-binding model of Kwon et al.) takes 71 s (M=100, R=2, no exact moments; 383 s with the default exact moments), and a matrix-free 2,097,152-atom crystal 10 min in 1.8 GB. A 17^3 replica of a 216-atom amorphous cell (1,061,208 atoms) agrees with exact Bloch spectra: electronic DOS in 229 s, matrix-free ACE vibrational DOS in 2.2 h on one GPU. Shift-invert, incomplete-Cholesky setup, exact moments of high order and Maxent at high moment counts do not scale. The underlying methods are due to Silver, Roder, Kress, Voter, Drabold, Sankey and collaborators; the Introduction states what is added.

</details>


### [125] [Orbital texture of valence band and interband transitions of the type-II multiferroic van der Waals NiI$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11425)
*A. De Vita,O. E. Giehl,T. Mukherjee,M. Wolf,L. Rettig,R. Ernstorfer,T. Pincelli,L. T. Lloyd*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用时间分辨与角分辨光电子能谱，结合第一性原理计算，系统揭示了范德华半导体NiI₂体材料的电子能带结构、Ni与I轨道的组成，以及最低价带至导带跃迁的单粒子电子激发特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NiI₂具有复杂的电子与磁相，具备功能应用的潜力，但对其能带结构及激发的清晰认识是理解其电子与输运性质的前提，目前缺乏系统的实验与理论相结合的描述。

Method: 采用时间分辨与角分辨光电子能谱（tr-ARPES）实验，辅以第一性原理计算，对体材料NiI₂的能带结构、轨道特性及最低价带到导带跃迁进行综合表征与理论对照。

Result: 给出了Ni与I电子态流形的轨道特征，识别了价带的主要动量分辨特征以及导带的浅极小值位置，明确了最低价带到导带的单粒子带间电子跃迁。

Conclusion: 本研究为NiI₂的电子结构及其单粒子带间激发提供了基础性认识，可作为理论模型的基准，并为该材料未来的研究提供框架。

Abstract: NiI$_2$ is a van der Waals semiconductor characterized by complex electronic and magnetic phases, holding promise towards functional applications. For this purpose, a detailed understanding of its band structure and its excitations is essential to elucidate the electronic and transport properties. We present a comprehensive description of the band structure and of the lowest valence-to-conduction band transition in bulk NiI$_2$, by means of time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy supported by first-principles calculations. We describe the orbital character of Ni and I manifolds, and identify the main momentum-resolved features of the valence band and the shallow minima of the conduction band. With this study, we provide a foundational understanding of the electronic structure of NiI$_2$ and its single-particle interband electronic excitation, a benchmark for theoretical models, and a framework for future investigations on this material.

</details>


### [126] [Giant gas permeance through a nanoporous crystalline 2D polymer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11518)
*Jonathan D'Emidio,Daniel J. Murray,William B. Thompson,Jarvis C. Humlick,K. C. Schwab,Adrian Del Maestro,Benjamin T. King*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究者测量了气体通过悬浮的单分子厚纳米多孔二维聚合物（poly(carboxyfantrip)）的输运，其归一化气体渗透率接近泻流极限，比石墨烯筛分膜高出3-4个数量级，并遵循格雷姆定律、趋近无相互作用的弹道输运理想极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学分离过程是工业能耗的主要来源之一，亟需更高效的分离策略。传统自上而下制造难以实现原子级精确的孔隙，而二维材料提供了实现原子级精确多孔性的途径，有望通过提高通量来提升分离性能。

Method: 制备悬浮的单分子厚、纳米多孔的二维聚合物poly(carboxyfantrip)薄膜，其孔隙为相同纳米尺寸且覆盖约四分之一表面。测量H₂、He、N₂、CO₂、SF₆等分子量跨近两个数量级的气体输运，并结合模拟在实验误差范围内重现渗透率并提供原子尺度输运图像。

Result: 该二维聚合物兼具极限薄度与高面积孔隙密度，归一化气体流量（渗透率）接近泻流极限，比气体筛分石墨烯膜高3-4个数量级；气体遵循格雷姆定律，趋近无相互作用弹道输运的理想极限，尽管存在物种依赖的气体-孔相互作用。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了合成晶体二维聚合物在化学分离、水过滤和量子传感领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Chemical separation processes represent one of the largest contributors to industrial energy consumption, underscoring the need for the most efficient separation strategies. Many separation processes rely on selective transport through porous media. In this context, two-dimensional (2D) materials offer a promising route to atomically precise porosity beyond the reach of traditional top-down fabrication, with the potential to enhance separation performance by increasing flow. Here we report measurements of gas transport through a one-molecule-thick layer of suspended, nanoporous poly(carboxyfantrip), a 2D polymer that has identical, nanometer-sized pores that cover nearly a quarter of the surface. This combination of ultimate thinness with high areal pore density gives normalized gas flows (permeances) that approach the effusion limit, three to four orders of magnitude above gas-sieving graphene membranes. We find that gases spanning nearly two orders of magnitude in molecular weight (H$_2$, He, N$_2$, CO$_2$, and SF$_6$) obey Graham's law and approach the ideal limit of non-interacting ballistic transport, despite strong, species-dependent gas-pore interactions. Simulations reproduce the measured permeances within experimental uncertainty and provide an atomistic picture of the transport process. This work demonstrates the potential of synthetic crystalline 2D polymers for chemical separation, water filtration, and quantum sensing.

</details>


### [127] [Overcoming speed-accuracy trade-offs with medium-sized interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11544)
*Flynn Walsh,Nicolas Bertin,Robert E. Rudd,Aurélien Perron*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种介于小型快速模型与大型表达网络之间的原子簇展开（ACE）机器学习势，以接近最低成本实现接近可迁移精度的极限，并用其完成了十亿原子规模、数十万时间步的辐照损伤级联模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习原子间势在“小型快速模型”与“大型强表达力网络”之间缺乏折中：小型模型速度快但精度有限，大型模型精度高但计算成本昂贵。作者希望设计一种中等规模势函数，能以最廉价形式主义的成本逼近可迁移精度的极限，从而支撑大规模、长时间的分子动力学模拟。

Method: 采用原子簇展开（ACE）方法，将每个多项式的径向成分建模为独立的线性变换，并嵌入大量具有“可学习指数”的密度项；针对这一新型结构开发了优化实现。模型在系统枚举的小结构上训练，用于含氦的 W-Ta-Hf-Cr-V-Ti 合金体系。

Result: 训练（近训练集）损失主要取决于模型规模；但在独立验证集上，该中等规模模型的误差与参数量为其十倍的领先势函数相当。运行速度比大型模型快一个数量级以上，比相同多项式的更小型模型快数倍。模型还能泛化到点缺陷与平面缺陷，并可扩展到更复杂化学体系。

Conclusion: 通过新的 ACE 结构设计，中等规模势函数能够以低成本实现接近可迁移精度极限的性能，并在十亿原子、数十万时间步的辐照损伤级联模拟中得到验证，展示了面向极端规模与复杂化学环境的潜力。

Abstract: Current machine-learned interatomic potentials offer a choice between small, fast models and large, expressive networks. This study demonstrates how an intermediate potential can approach the limit of transferable accuracy at the cost of the cheapest existing formalisms. Specifically, we model He-bearing W-Ta-Hf-Cr-V-Ti alloys with an atomic cluster expansion that learns the radial components of each polynomial as independent linear transforms and embeds numerous densities with learned exponents. While (near-)training loss appears largely a function of model size, the error on a distinct validation set is close to that of leading potentials with ten times more parameters. Taking advantage of the new structure, we develop optimized implementations that run more than an order of magnitude faster than the larger models and many times faster than smaller models with equivalent polynomials. The potential is trained on systematically enumerated small structures but generalizes to point and planar defects and can be extended to even more complex chemistries. As a demonstration, we simulate radiation-damage cascades in billion-atom systems for hundreds of thousands of timesteps.

</details>


### [128] [Hydrogen Thermodiffusion in Tungsten: Trapped Hydrogen Distorts Heat of Transport Estimation from Total Concentration Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11702)
*Ville Jantunen,Daniel R. Mason,Sanjeet Kaur*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过多尺度模拟研究钨中氢在温度梯度下的扩散，发现氢的输运热在低温下为正（疏热）并在约1000 K变号，且简谐过渡态理论加弹性热膨胀修正不足以解释该行为；同时纳入俘获位效应的研究表明，实验测得的输运热可能被俘获氢的浓度温度依赖性主导，不能直接用于显式考虑俘获/脱俘过程的连续尺度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢在钨中的热扩散（thermodiffusion）对核聚变应用中面向等离子体材料的行为至关重要，但输运热（heat of transport）的物理起源尚不清楚，且俘获位（trapping sites）对氢热扩散的作用此前未被系统研究。

Method: 采用多尺度模拟方法：分子动力学模拟研究钨中氢在温度梯度下的输运热及其随温度的变化；用简谐过渡态理论结合弹性热膨胀修正分析输运热起源；在模拟与解析模型中引入俘获位效应，并区分移动氢与俘获氢浓度。

Result: 氢在钨中的输运热在低温下为正（疏热行为），约在1000 K附近改变符号；简谐过渡态理论加弹性热膨胀修正不足以解释观测行为，提示局部热力学平衡可能失效或存在不可忽略的非简谐贡献；当俘获氢占总氢浓度比例显著时，由总浓度测量推断的输运热主要反映俘获氢浓度的温度依赖性，而非移动氢浓度梯度。

Conclusion: 实验测得的输运热一般不能直接用于显式考虑俘获与脱俘的连续尺度模型，此类模型的输运热应仅基于移动氢浓度，以避免重复计入俘获效应。

Abstract: We investigate hydrogen diffusion in tungsten under thermal gradients in the presence of trapping sites using multiscale simulations. Using molecular dynamics simulations, we find that the heat of transport, which quantifies the coupling between temperature gradients and hydrogen transport, for hydrogen in tungsten is positive (thermophobic) at lower temperatures but changes sign near $1000$ K. We investigate the origin of the heat of transport and find that harmonic transition state theory with elastic thermal-expansion corrections is insufficient to explain the observed behaviour, consistent with either a breakdown of local thermodynamic equilibrium or non-negligible anharmonic contributions.
  We further extend previous studies of hydrogen thermodiffusion by including the effect of trapping sites in both simulations and analytical models. We show that when trapped hydrogen accounts for a significant fraction of the total hydrogen concentration, the heat of transport inferred from measurements of the total concentration is dominated by the temperature dependence of the trapped hydrogen population and no longer reflects the gradient in the mobile hydrogen concentration. Consequently, experimentally measured heats of transport cannot in general be used directly in continuum-scale models that explicitly account for trapping and detrapping. In such models, the heat of transport should be based on mobile hydrogen concentrations only to avoid incorporating trapping effects twice.

</details>


### [129] [Revealing laser-induced modification of weak ferromagnetism through a spin-reorientation phase transition in iron borate Fe$_3$BO$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12460)
*A. V. Kuzikova,S. N. Barilo,R. A. Lanovsky,A. V. Sadovnikov,R. V. Pisarev,A. M. Kalashnikova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用磁光泵浦-探测技术研究了 Fe₃BO₆ 中由飞秒激光触发的自旋重取向（SR）转变 Γ₂→Γ₄ 的超快动力学，揭示弱铁磁矩的骤降源于 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用（DMI）与各向异性贡献的非热调控，且该过程由电荷转移跃迁（2p→3d）而非局域 3d→3d 跃迁驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fe₃BO₆ 的弱铁磁性来自两种非等价 Fe³⁺ 位点（4c 与 8d）上的双位点 DMI 与单位点各向异性的竞争，其 SR 转变 Γ₂→Γ₄ 独特地包含 90° 自旋翻转和弱铁磁矩 35% 的变化。作者希望以该转变为"探针"，探究飞秒激光如何改变弱铁磁性的两种微观贡献，从而理解超快磁调控的机制与物理根源。

Method: 采用磁光泵浦-探测技术，监测激光脉冲诱导的 SR 转变超快动力学；通过调节泵浦光子能量，使其落在不同光学吸收区间（电荷转移 2p→3d 跃迁与局域 3d→3d 跃迁）进行对比，以分辨热效应与非热效应；结合对弱铁磁矩、转变阈值、延迟时间及末态的测量，分析激光诱导的 DMI 与各向异性（自旋倾斜）修正。

Result: 结果显示 SR 转变由 Γ₂ 相中弱铁磁矩的突然减小触发，该减小归因于激光对 DMI 与各向异性贡献的调制；改变泵浦光子能量表明，激光对自旋倾斜的影响具有非热特征，主要通过 Fe³⁺O₆²⁻ 八面体内的电荷转移跃迁 2p→3d 实现，而非 Fe³⁺ 的局域 3d→3d 跃迁；减小的弱铁磁矩决定了 SR 转变的阈值与延迟时间，并影响末态——末态通过激光诱导加热驱动的进动翻转进入 Γ₄ 相，且表现为 Γ₄ 与 Γ₂ 相共存，其修正后的自旋倾斜可持续数百皮秒。

Conclusion: Fe₃BO₆ 中 SR 转变的弱铁磁矩骤降由激光非热性地改变 DMI 与各向异性（自旋倾斜）贡献引起，且该过程由八面体内 2p→3d 电荷转移跃迁主导；减小的弱铁磁矩控制着转变阈值与延迟，并在激光加热辅助下通过进动翻转形成 Γ₄ 与 Γ₂ 共存的持久末态。

Abstract: In the iron oxide Fe$_3$BO$_6$ weak ferromagnetism stems from competing contributions from two-site Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions (DMI) and from on-site anisotropies at the two $4c$ and $8d$ types of nonequivalent Fe$^{3+}$ sites. As a result, spin reorientation (SR) transition $Γ_2$ $\rightarrow$ $Γ_4$ in Fe$_3$BO$_6$ uniquely comprises 90$^\circ$ spin switching and a 35% change of weak ferromagnetic moment. We explore ultrafast kinetics of such a transition as a proxy for weak ferromagnetism contributions modified by femtosecond laser pulses. Using magneto-optical pump-probe technique we reveal that the SR transition is triggered by an abrupt decrease of the weak ferromagnetic moment in the $Γ_2$ phase, and relate it to the laser-induced modification of the DMI and anisotropy contributions to the spin canting. By tuning the pump pulse phonon energy between different optical absorption ranges, we show that laser-driven impact on spin canting possesses a nonthermal character and is achieved through the charge-transfer transitions $2p\rightarrow3d$ within Fe$^{3+}$O$^{2-}_6$ octahedra rather than by localized $3d\rightarrow3d$ transitions at Fe$^{3+}$. The decreased weak ferromagnetic moment controls the threshold and the latency of the SR transition, as well as a final state. The latter is achieved via the precessional switching to the $Γ_4$ phase activated by the laser-induced heating. Final state is found to be a coexistence of $Γ_4$ and $Γ_2$ phase with modified spin canting persisting even at hundreds of picoseconds.

</details>


### [130] [Strain-dependent Dynamics of Interactions of Iodine-vacancy pairs in CsPbI3: A DFT and Machine-Learning Molecular Dynamics Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11765)
*Donggeun Kang,Seongju Lee,Yonggoon Jeon*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合机器学习分子动力学和密度泛函理论，本文研究了倾斜CsPbI3中相邻碘空位在各向同性与双轴应变下的稳定性与重排，发现短时跃迁分数随应变呈非单调变化，且双轴压缩产生取向依赖的不同响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碘空位的迁移与缔合对卤化物钙钛矿的结构稳定性至关重要，但相邻空位对在有限温度晶格变形下的稳定性与重排机制尚不清楚。

Method: 采用机器学习分子动力学（MLMD）与密度泛函理论（DFT）相结合的方法，研究倾斜CsPbI3中相邻碘空位在各向同性和双轴应变下的行为，并用SevenNet-0势函数进行互补静态计算。

Result: MLMD轨迹显示短时跃迁分数对应变呈非单调依赖：强各向同性压缩有利于点状分离，拉伸则促进保持成对的接力跃迁。双轴压缩呈现取向依赖响应：初始与压缩面平行的空位对倾向于分离，而垂直于压缩面的空位对主要表现为接力跃迁。DFT与SevenNet-0静态计算表明所有构型均为排斥相互作用。

Conclusion: 有限温度动力学和应变依赖的局域几何对理解卤化物钙钛矿中空位对的稳定性与重排至关重要。

Abstract: Iodine-vacancy migration and association are important factors in the structural stability of halide perovskites. Although neighboring vacancies have been suggested to form energetically favorable pairs, their stability and reconfiguration under finite-temperature lattice deformation remain unclear. Here, we combine machine-learning molecular dynamics (MLMD) and density functional theory (DFT) to investigate neighboring iodine vacancies in tilted CsPbI3 under isotropic and biaxial strain. The MLMD trajectories reveal a nonmonotonic dependence of short-time hopping fractions on strain, with strong isotropic compression favoring point-like separation and tension promoting pair-preserving relay hopping. Notably, biaxial compression produces distinct orientation-dependent responses: vacancy pairs initially aligned with the compressed plane preferentially undergo separation, whereas those perpendicular to the plane predominantly exhibit relay hopping. Complementary static calculations using DFT and the SevenNet-0 potential yield repulsive interactions for all configurations examined. These findings highlight the importance of finite-temperature dynamics and strain-dependent local geometry in understanding vacancy-pair stability and reconfiguration in halide perovskites.

</details>


### [131] [Misorientations in dislocation cell structures: Modelling and statistical analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12165)
*Lei Zeng,Yufan Zhang,Michael Zaiser*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过对位错花样形成过程中晶格旋转与取向差进行介观模拟与统计分析，揭示滑移系活性发生对称性破缺分岔时，位错胞壁取向差统计从单一群体过渡到“附生（incidental）”与“几何必需（geometrically necessary）”两个亚群体的转变，且该区分无需事先图像分割、具有统计稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位错花样形成伴随晶格旋转及位错胞壁两侧的取向差，但此前缺乏系统性的模拟研究；作者旨在利用介观建模新进展，首次系统模拟并统计刻画面心立方与岩盐结构晶体中的晶格旋转与取向差花样，阐明位错胞壁类型的转变机制。

Method: 采用介观位错花样建模方法，对面心立方（fcc）与岩盐（rocksalt）结构晶体的位错花样形成进行模拟；对胞壁两侧的取向变化进行统计分析（统计检验），结合数据解析方法判断滑移系活性中的对称性破缺分岔，并区分“附生”与“几何必需”两类位错胞壁，且无需预先进行图像分割。

Result: 统计检验显示：在分岔以下，仅存在单一群体的取向差胞壁；在分岔以上，出现“附生”与“几何必需”两个亚群体。该区分不依赖图像分割，且统计上稳健，表明胞壁类型的二分与滑移系活性的对称性破缺分岔密切相关。

Conclusion: 位错胞壁取向差统计性质随滑移系活性对称性破缺分岔而发生转变，可据此区分“附生”与“几何必需”位错胞壁；该数据驱动方法无需图像分割，具有统计稳健性，为理解位错花样的取向差结构提供了新途径。

Abstract: Dislocation pattern formation is accompanied by the emergence of lattice rotations and concomitant misorientations across dislocation walls (cell boundaries). Recent advances in mesoscopic modelling of dislocation patterns enable for the first time a systematic simulation study of this phenomenon. We present simulation results for lattice rotation and misorientation patterns in crystals with face-centered cubic and rocksalt lattice structure. Statistical analysis of the orientation changes across cell walls reveals a transition associated with a symmetry-breaking bifurcation in slip system activity: Below the bifurcation, statistical tests indicate the presence of a single population of misoriented walls whereas, above the bifurcation, two sub-populations of 'incidental' and 'geometrically necessary' dislocation walls are present. Our data analytic approach demonstrates that this distinction does not require prior image segmentation and emphasizes the statistical robustness of the distinction between two types of dislocation walls.

</details>


### [132] [Designed atomic displacements for finite-temperature phonons from few force calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12218)
*Albert Linda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出特殊最优热力学胞（SOTC-D）方法，通过精心设计而非随机生成超胞位移来高效拟合有限温度声子，能以极少的第一性原理力计算获得高精度声子色散与热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限温度声子拟合依赖对随机位移超胞进行大量第一性原理力计算，每次计算成本高昂。作者旨在通过"设计"而非"随机"的方式构造位移，以更少的力计算达到同等或更高的精度，从而显著降低计算开销。

Method: 作者提出特殊最优热力学胞（SOTC-D）方法：从谐振试算模型出发，赋予每个超胞声子模态其量子热振幅；通过选择每个模态的符号使力常数得到最佳确定；将每个构型与其反演构型配对以抵消三次力贡献。更新试算模型可得到自洽谐振解。文中还对比了另一种"匹配位移关联"的胞设计（有良好热力学但色散无提升），并将SOTC-D与随机采样、两迭代随机采样（sTDEP）等方法在11种晶体上进行比较。

Result: 在11种晶体上，8次力计算即可将有效色散复现到0.03–0.31 THz以内，SOTC-D在9种晶体中是五种设计里最准确的。经过一次试算更新后，在4–16次计算时它全面超越随机采样，并在8次（超胞基）时全面超越两迭代随机采样（sTDEP）。在模型势上，自洽形式在14例中的13例所需计算次数仅为随机自洽谐振近似的1/16–1/2。使用HSE06杂化泛函时，在Si和NaCl中于4–16次计算优于汇总sTDEP，且300K下Al的热膨胀与实验误差在3%以内。其局限性包括：超过100原子的Al和Si胞中，sTDEP在4–16次计算之后更精确（NaCl在16次内并非如此）；对动态稳定的bcc Zr，结果依赖种子；400K以上自由能曲率无法分辨。

Conclusion: SOTC-D是一种高效、精确的有限温度声子拟合方法，通过确定性设计的位移与反演配对，在极少数力计算下即可高精度复现声子色散和热力学性质，并在多种晶体与模型体系中优于随机与sTDEP方法，适用于昂贵的杂化泛函计算。其局限性主要出现在大胞、动态稳定相、种子依赖以及高温自由能曲率分辨等方面。

Abstract: Finite-temperature phonons are usually fitted to first-principles forces in randomly displaced supercells, and each force calculation can be expensive. We introduce special optimal thermal cells (SOTCs), in which the displacements are designed rather than drawn at random. Our design, SOTC-D, starts from a harmonic trial model. It gives every supercell phonon mode its quantum-thermal amplitude, chooses the sign of each mode so that the force constants are best determined, and pairs each configuration with its inverse so that cubic forces cancel. Updating the trial leads to the self-consistent harmonic solution. Cells that match displacement correlations instead give good thermodynamics but not better dispersions. For eleven crystals, eight force calculations reproduce the effective dispersions within 0.03-0.31 THz, and SOTC-D is the most accurate of five designs in 9 of them. After one trial update, it beats random sampling in all crystals at 4-16 calculations and two-iteration stochastic sampling (sTDEP) in all at eight (supercell basis). On model potentials, the self-consistent form needs 2-16 times fewer calculations than the stochastic self-consistent harmonic approximation in 13 of 14 cases. With the HSE06 hybrid functional, it is ahead of pooled sTDEP at 4-16 calculations in Si and NaCl, and it gives the thermal expansion of Al within 3% of experiment at 300K. The method has limits: in Al and Si cells of more than 100 atoms, sTDEP is more accurate beyond 4-16 calculations (not so in NaCl up to 16); for dynamically stabilized bcc Zr, the outcome depends on the seed; and above 400 K, the free-energy curvature is not resolved.

</details>


### [133] [Multichannel Phonon Interconversion Drives 90 Polarization Rotation for Stable Domain Walls in Hafnium Oxide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12264)
*Fatoye Sawyerr,Hamoon Fahrvandi,Pawan Kumar,Zekun Zhang,Shuning Lv,Yue Yu,Xuan Gao,Kang Jia,Shifeng Wen,Zheng Wen,Li-Min Liu,Xiaoli Fan,Jun Hee Lee,Tengfei Cao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文结合凝聚声子模分解与第一性原理计算，揭示了HfO2铁电相中90°极化旋转起源于布里渊区不同波矢、相位等价声子模的耦合与协同再取向，并阐明了90°畴壁的结构机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化旋转是铁电材料的基本机制，但萤石结构铁电体中90°极化旋转如何在微观尺度上产生并形成不同畴壁仍不明确。而声子模为这类结构相变提供了微观途径，因为当其特征波长与相关物理尺寸相当时，几何取向会改变其频率、色散关系和振动本征态。

Method: 以立方母相为参考，结合凝聚声子模分解与第一性原理计算，系统研究HfO2铁电相中90°极化旋转的起源，分析不同波矢处多个对称等价声子模的耦合与协同再取向，并通过STEM成像实验观测稳定90°畴壁中的原子模畸变。

Result: 90°极化旋转源于布里渊区不同波矢处、具有不同本征态的多个对称等价声子模，其耦合与协同再取向连续演化产生不同90° Pca21变体与切换路径。在90°变体中，经Pmn21的最低能量切换路径的能垒与经P42/nmc相的180°极化反转路径相当且略低，表明90°极化旋转是能量上可行的替代切换路径。90°畴壁中心出现受挫声子模，凝聚模共存与竞争促进晶格畸变的局域重构，导致畴壁能量升高。STEM成像直接观测到稳定90°畴壁中的原子模畸变，与理论预测的低能畴壁一致。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了90°切换动力学、竞争性畴壁物理以及宏观铁电行为背后的基本结构机制，为理解萤石铁电体的极化旋转与畴壁形成提供了微观理论基础。

Abstract: Polarization rotation is a fundamental mechanism in ferroelectric materials, yet how such rotation arises microscopically in fluorite ferroelectrics and gives rise to distinct domain walls (DW) remains unresolved. Phonon modes offer a microscopic route to such structural transformations, as geometric orientations can modify their frequencies, dispersion relations, and vibrational eigenstates when their characteristic wavelengths become comparable to relevant physical dimensions. Here, by combining condensed phonon mode decomposition with first-principles calculations, we investigate the origin of 90 polarization rotation in the ferroelectric phase of HfO2. Using the cubic parent phase as reference, we show that polarization rotation originates from multiple symmetry equivalent phonon modes of distinct eigenstates at different wavevectors in the Brillouin zone, whose coupling and cooperative reorientation continuously evolve to generate distinct 90 Pca21 variants and switching pathways. Among the 90 variants, the lowest-energy switching pathway through the Pmn21 exhibits an energy barrier comparable to, but slightly lower than, that of the 180 polarization reversal pathway through the P42/nmc phase, indicating that 90 polarization rotation is an energetically accessible alternative switching route. We further analyze 90 DW and find that frustrated phonon modes emerge at the wall center, where the coexistence and competition of condensed modes promote local reconfiguration of the lattice distortions, contributing to the elevated DW energies. Importantly, experimental observations with STEM imaging provide direct evidence of atomic mode distortions in stable 90 DW, consistent with the theoretically identified low-energy DW. These findings provide the fundamental structural mechanism underlying 90 switching dynamics, competing domain-wall physics, and macroscopic ferroelectric behavior.

</details>


### [134] [Defects and Structural Evolution in CrSBr: Machine-Learning Potentials for Atomistic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12267)
*Arsalan Hashemi,Daniil Kruklinskii,Xiaoya Chang,Tapio Ala-Nissila,Mikko Karttunen,Arkady V. Krasheninnikov,Mahdi Ghorbani-Asl*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发并验证了用于Cr-S-Br体系的机器学习原子间势（MLIP），其中电荷感知的qNEP变体在预测晶格常数、形成焓、空位能量学和层状o-CrSBr的各向异性压力响应方面达到接近DFT的精度，并通过NEB和位移阈值计算揭示了S空位对Cr缺陷迁移的促进作用及Br最易被电子束位移的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状范德华半导体CrSBr兼具本征磁序与半导体、光学性质，是多功能电子与光子应用的候选材料。然而，在实验相关条件下理解缺陷与结构转变如何影响其性质，对第一性原理模拟而言受限于长度和时间尺度，因此需要能进行大规模原子模拟的高精度势函数。

Method: 开发并基准测试Cr-S-Br体系的机器学习原子间势（MLIP），采用电荷感知变体qNEP，并开展逼近DFT精度的大规模原子模拟。通过弹性带（NEB）计算研究Cr Frenkel缺陷对的迁移势垒受S空位的影响，并通过位移阈值计算评估不同原子种类在电子束诱导位移下的相对难易程度。

Result: qNEP在晶格常数、形成焓、空位能量学及o-CrSBr各向异性压力响应方面达到接近DFT的精度。NEB计算表明，预先存在的S空位可将Cr Frenkel对的迁移势垒降低最多1.1 eV，为束诱导的Cr再分布和结构转变提供了原子尺度机制。位移阈值计算显示Br是最易被电子束诱导位移的物种，明确了去除不同原子种类的相对容易程度。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个经过验证的计算框架，用于研究CrSBr中的原子尺度无序与结构转变，为未来磁光电子应用中的缺陷工程提供了基础。

Abstract: The layered van der Waals semiconductor CrSBr combines intrinsic magnetic order with semiconducting and optical properties, making it a promising material for multifunctional electronic and photonic applications. Understanding how defects and structural transformations affect its properties under experimentally relevant conditions, however, remains challenging for first-principles simulations due to the relevant length and time scales. Here, we develop and benchmark machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for the Cr-S-Br system, enabling large-scale atomistic simulations of CrSBr with accuracy approaching that of density functional theory (DFT). The charge-aware variant qNEP achieves near-DFT accuracy in predicting lattice constants, formation enthalpies, vacancy energetics, and the anisotropic pressure response of o-CrSBr. Nudged elastic band calculations reveal that pre-existing S vacancies lower the Cr Frenkel-pair migration barrier by up to 1.1 eV, providing an atomistic mechanism for beam-induced Cr redistribution and structural transformations. Displacement threshold calculations further show that Br is the most susceptible species to electron-beam-induced displacement, identifying the relative ease of removing different atomic species. Together, these results establish a validated computational framework for investigating atomic-scale disorder and structural transformations in CrSBr, providing a basis for defect engineering in materials for future magneto-optoelectronic applications.

</details>


### [135] [Ultrafast Liquid Jet X-ray Total Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12290)
*Adam F. Sapnik,Philip A. Chater,Dean S. Keeble,Tobias A. Bird,John S. O. Evans,Federica Bertolotti,Antonietta Guagliardi,Norberto Masciocchi,Daniel T. Bowron,Lise J. Støckler,Elodie A. Harbourne,Anders B. Borup,Rebecca S. Silberg,Viktoria A. Kaznelson,Stefanie Punke,Mariia Svyrydenko,Maryna I. Bodnarchuk,Dmitry N. Dirin,Simon C. Boehme,Maksym V. Kovalenko,Bo B. Iversen,Emil S. Bozin,Kirsten M. Ø. Jensen,Gabriele Ansaldi,Karen Appel,Alexander Gierke,Jörg Hallmann,Aliaksandr Leonau,Anders Madsen,Johannes Möller,Jan-Etienne Pudell,Angel Rodriguez-Fernandez,Andreas Schmidt,Roman Shayduk,Cornelius Strohm,Joana Valerio,Ulrike Boesenberg,David A. Keen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文展示了利用欧洲XFEL的MID仪器从液体射流样品中获得高质量全散射与PDF数据，实现了溶液体系的时间分辨结构动力学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将全散射与PDF分析拓展到飞秒至皮秒时间尺度，以追踪溶液中的超快结构动力学，并克服此前主要限于非液相样品的局限。关键是解决液体射流带来的巨大空气与溶剂背景干扰，使定量结构分析成为可能。

Method: 采用欧洲XFEL的Materials Imaging and Dynamics (MID)仪器，分别对常规毛细管样品和液体射流输送样品采集全散射数据（Qmax = 16.4 Å⁻¹）；对CsPbBr₃纳米晶体液体射流进行泵浦-探测全散射实验；并对水相Keggin团簇的XFEL全散射数据进行经验势结构精修（EPSR）。

Result: 从液体分散体和溶液中的分子团簇中成功回收了适合定量结构分析的PDF；解析了CsPbBr₃纳米晶体中依赖通量的光致各向同性晶格应变；EPSR揭示Keggin团簇的位点特异性水合环境，实现了周围溶剂结构的三维映射。

Conclusion: 该工作为追踪溶液中超快结构变化乃至支配分子行为与反应性的动态溶剂环境开辟了道路。

Abstract: X-ray free-electron lasers (XFELs) provide the temporal resolution needed to extend total scattering and pair distribution function (PDF) analysis to investigate structural dynamics on femtosecond-to-picosecond timescales. We recently demonstrated that high-quality total scattering data and PDFs can be obtained from individual XFEL pulses; here, we take the next step towards time-resolved studies of structural dynamics in solution. Using the Materials Imaging and Dynamics (MID) instrument at the European XFEL, we obtain total scattering data up to $Q_{\max} = 16.4\,\mathring{\mathrm{A}}^{-1}$ from both conventional capillary samples and, critically, from samples delivered by a liquid jet. Despite the large air and solvent backgrounds inherent to this setup, PDFs suitable for quantitative structural analysis are recovered from liquid dispersions and molecular clusters in solution. We further demonstrate pump-probe total scattering from $\mathrm{CsPbBr}_3$ nanocrystals in a liquid jet, resolving a fluence-dependent photoinduced isotropic lattice strain. Additionally, empirical potential structure refinement (EPSR) of XFEL total scattering data from an aqueous Keggin cluster reveals site-specific hydration environments and enables three-dimensional mapping of the surrounding solvent structure. Together, these advances open the door to tracking not only ultrafast structural changes in solution, but also the evolving solvent environments that govern molecular behaviour and reactivity.

</details>


### [136] [Atomically Sharp Magnetic Domain Walls beyond Micromagnetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12303)
*Guokai Liao,Stefan Blügel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维 J-K-D 自旋晶格模型中原子尺度窄磁畴壁的结构与稳定性，发现当交换与各向异性比 J/K=3/2 时发生向原子级尖锐易轴畴壁的连续相变，并揭示了 DMI 对这种畴壁的修饰作用以及传统微磁学拟合对交换和各向异性的系统性误估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当磁畴壁宽度接近原子晶格间距时，微磁学所依赖的连续介质近似变得不可靠，而原子级窄畴壁广泛出现在超薄膜、范德华磁体和具有竞争交换作用的体系中，因此需要建立超越连续近似的原子尺度理论来理解其真实结构与稳定性。

Method: 作者采用一维最近邻 J-K-D 自旋晶格模型，包含海森堡交换作用、单轴各向异性和 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用（DMI）。通过解析分析证明了在无 DMI 时临界比 J/K=3/2 处发生向原子级尖锐易轴畴壁的连续相变（由局域化 Hessian 模式软化表征），并用原子自旋动力学模拟加以验证；进一步考虑有限 DMI 对畴壁结构的修正，推导了强各向异性极限下 DMI 诱导倾斜的解析表达式。

Result: 无 DMI 时，体系在 J/K=3/2 处经历连续相变，形成原子级尖锐的易轴畴壁；用传统微磁学解拟合离散畴壁轮廓会系统性低估交换作用并高估各向异性。有限 DMI 消除了完全共线畴壁这一静态构型，使相变退化为向强局域但倾斜畴壁的平滑过渡，该倾斜畴壁仅在强各向异性极限下渐近逼近共线易轴构型；在临界比附近，DMI 诱导的倾斜响应显著增强。

Conclusion: 研究确立了微磁学描述在原子级窄畴壁情形下的局限性，并表明通过调控各向异性和 DMI 可以控制此类畴壁的结构，为相关实验和器件设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Atomically narrow magnetic domain walls can arise in ultrathin magnetic films, van der Waals magnets, and systems with reduced effective exchange stiffness due to competing exchange interactions. As their width approaches the atomic lattice spacing, the continuum approximation underlying micromagnetics becomes increasingly unreliable. We investigate the structure and stability of such domain walls in a one-dimensional nearest-neighbor J-K-D spin-lattice model incorporating Heisenberg exchange, uniaxial anisotropy, and Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI). In the absence of DMI, we demonstrate analytically a continuous transition to an atomically sharp easy-axis wall at the critical exchange-to-anisotropy ratio J/K = 3/2, characterized by the softening of a localized Hessian mode. Atomistic spin-dynamics simulations confirm this transition and reveal that fitting discrete wall profiles with the conventional micromagnetic solution, as commonly done in experimental analyses, underestimates the exchange interaction and overestimates the anisotropy. Finite DMI removes the perfectly collinear wall as a stationary configuration, replacing the transition by a smooth crossover toward a strongly localized but canted wall that approaches the collinear easy-axis configuration only asymptotically in the strong-anisotropy limit. We derive an analytical expression for the DMI-induced canting in this regime and demonstrate its strongly enhanced canting response near the critical ratio. These findings establish the limitations of micromagnetic descriptions of atomically narrow domain walls and suggest opportunities for controlling their structure through anisotropy and DMI.

</details>


### [137] [Ultrafast Inter-Aggregate Energy Transfer Drives Efficient Charge Generation in Organic Homojunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12322)
*Shanchao Ouyang,Hang Yuen Ho,Sudhi Mahadevan,Ziyao Yue,Haochen Liu,Zhiqiang Guan,Chun-Sing Lee,Shu Kong So,Sai-Wing Tsang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文揭示Y6薄膜中结构不同的聚集体具有互补的电荷转移（CT）特性，从而驱动定向能量转移和增强激子离域，并建立了一个通用的多组分FRET框架，定量关联分子聚集、CT特性与激发态动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Y6基同质结有机光伏器件已取得超高功率转换效率，尽管已报道异常长的激子扩散长度和高效激子解离，但其优异性能背后的根本机制仍存在争议，亟需阐明其能量迁移与激子动力学的物理本质。

Method: 以Y6作为模型非富勒烯受体（NFA）体系，采用超快光谱技术，识别Y6聚集体间的高效Förster共振能量转移（FRET），并测定其系综平均效率（65%）与发生时间尺度（<1 ps）；在此基础上建立通用的多组分FRET框架，定量关联分子聚集、CT特性与激发态动力学。

Result: 发现Y6薄膜中结构不同的聚集体具有互补的CT特性，可实现定向能量转移和增强激子离域；聚集体介导的FRET效率达65%，在亚皮秒（<1 ps）时间尺度内完成，驱动长程能量迁移，从而解释了同质结器件即使在缺乏传统给体-受体体异质结形貌、且存在大量弱CT组分的情况下，仍能获得显著外量子效率的原因。

Conclusion: 结构不同的聚集体共存于NFA薄膜中并具备互补CT特性，该聚集体介导的能量转移途径是Y6基同质结器件高性能的关键机制。所建立的多组分FRET框架为开发下一代高性能有机电子材料与器件提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Y6-based homojunction organic photovoltaic devices have recently achieved exceptional power conversion efficiencies, surpassing those of conventional organic photovoltaic materials. Although unusually long exciton diffusion lengths and efficient exciton dissociation have been reported, the fundamental mechanism underlying this outstanding performance remains under debate. Here, we report that structurally distinct aggregates in Y6 thin films exhibit complementary charge-transfer (CT) characters that enable directional energy transfer and enhanced exciton delocalization. Using ultrafast spectroscopy, we identify highly efficient Förster resonance energy transfer (FRET) between Y6 aggregates, with an ensemble-averaged efficiency of 65% occurring on a sub-picosecond timescale (<1 ps). This aggregate-mediated energy-transfer pathway drives long-range energy migration in Y6 thin films and accounts for the remarkable external quantum efficiencies observed in homojunction devices, even in the absence of conventional donor-acceptor bulk heterojunction morphologies and despite substantial fractions of weak-CT components. Here, using Y6 as a model non-fullerene acceptor (NFA) system, we show that structurally distinct aggregates coexisting in NFA thin films possess complementary CT characters, which are expected to enable directional energy transfer and enhanced exciton delocalization. We establish a general multi-component FRET framework that quantitatively links molecular aggregation, CT character, and excited-state dynamics in systems with multiple packing motifs. This work provides insight into the development of next-generation high-performance organic electronic materials and devices.

</details>


### [138] [Kagome Flat Bands from Self-Assembled Water on Non-van der Waals 2D Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12371)
*Tom Barnowsky,Madeleine Christie,Anastasiia Nihei,Rico Friedrich*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了非范德华二维材料表面的水钝化现象，发现分子态水吸附在能量和动力学上均有利，且常保持完整并形成有序覆盖层。这些水分子覆盖层可呈 kagome 晶格构型，在价带顶产生平带，并表现出强分子间孤对电子耦合与静电受挫表面势，表明水不仅是稳定剂，还可用于电子结构工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料性质不仅取决于本征电子结构，还受表面化学及环境相互作用影响。非范德华二维材料表面本征活泼，在真实实验条件下需要化学稳定化，因此需要系统研究水对其表面的钝化作用及其可能的电子结构效应。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）计算方法，系统研究非范德华二维材料的水钝化过程；分析分子态水的能量与动力学吸附行为、有序表面覆盖层的形成及其几何构型，并对 kagome 拓扑下的电子结构、分子间孤对电子耦合及表面静电势进行解析。

Result: 分子态水吸附在能量和动力学上对多种体系均有利；水分子通常保持完整而非解离，形成有序表面覆盖层。该覆盖层可呈现 kagome 晶格几何，使价带顶直接产生平带；电子结构分析显示 kagome 拓扑中相邻水分子孤对电子间存在强耦合，同时锁定分子取向产生静电受挫的表面势。

Conclusion: 水不仅可作为非范德华二维材料表面的稳定化剂，还可作为主动的电子结构工程调控要素，通过诱导 kagome 覆盖层与平带实现表面的电子性质调控。

Abstract: The properties of two-dimensional (2D) materials are governed not only by their intrinsic electronic structure but also in particular by their surface chemistry and interactions with the environment. The surfaces of non-van der Waals (non-vdW) 2D materials are intrinsically reactive and may require chemical stabilization under realistic experimental conditions. Here, we perform a systematic first-principles study of water passivation of non-vdW 2D materials and demonstrate that molecular water adsorption is energetically and dynamically favored for a wide range of systems. Rather than dissociating, water molecules frequently remain intact and form adsorbed ordered surface overlayers. These overlayers can adopt a kagome lattice geometry, leading to the emergence of flat electronic bands directly at the valence-band edge. Analysis of the electronic structure reveals a strong intermolecular coupling between neighboring water lone pairs within this kagome topology, while the locked molecular orientations simultaneously produce an electrostatically frustrated surface potential. These findings establish water not only as a stabilizing agent but also as an active element for electronic structure engineering of non-vdW 2D surfaces.

</details>


### [139] [Quantum Embedding from Molecular to Crystalline Environments at the Cost of a Gas-Phase Calculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12399)
*Manas Sharma,Marek Sierka*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种直接空间表述的量子密度泛函理论（DFT）嵌入方法，可将任意维度冻结环境（从有限分子到周期体系）中的活性子体系进行嵌入计算，并实现解析核梯度、激发态波函数理论和实时含时DFT的耦合，大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子嵌入方法在处理从有限分子到扩展周期体系的冻结环境时，往往难以统一描述任意维度的宿主，且在创建空位、进行结构弛豫、激发态与实时动力学模拟方面存在限制。作者希望发展一种只需求解一次宿主、能以电子密度和核电荷表示环境、无需创建超胞即可处理缺陷/空位、并支持解析梯度和高级电子结构方法耦合的高效嵌入方案。

Method: 采用直接空间表述的DFT嵌入方法：宿主环境仅计算一次（不含嵌入式分子），其电子密度用原子中心辅助高斯基拟合，连同核电荷一起表示，并将周期静电分离为显式近场与连续快速多极远场算子。该方法允许从表示中移除宿主原子以创建空位或空隙，无需构建超胞。同时实现了解析核梯度以支持活性子体系的结构弛豫，并可耦合RT-TDDFT及CC2等激发态相关波函数方法。

Result: CO吸附于MgO(001)的DFT-in-DFT计算快速收敛，在将成本降低27倍的同时，周期相互作用能误差在0.44 kJ/mol以内；周期水中的亚甲基环丙烯的RT-TDDFET模拟重现约0.6 eV的溶剂化蓝移；六方氮化硼（hBN）上CO2的强场传播以相当于气相传播的成本复现了基底诱导效应；与CC2耦合时，溶剂化位移与超分子CC2参考值偏差在0.01 eV以内，计算成本降低超过500倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的直接空间DFT嵌入方法具有通用性和高精度，能够高效、准确地描述从有限到周期性环境的嵌入体系，支持结构弛豫、实时含时传播及激发态波函数理论耦合，在保持接近参考精度（能量误差<0.5 kJ/mol、<0.01 eV）的同时实现27倍至500倍以上的加速。

Abstract: We present a direct-space formulation and implementation of quantum density functional theory (DFT) embedding for molecular active subsystems embedded in frozen environments (hosts) of arbitrary dimensionality, from finite molecular hosts to extended systems. The host is computed once without the embedded molecule (the guest) and is represented only by its electron density fitted in an atom-centered auxiliary Gaussian basis together with its nuclear charges with the periodic electrostatics separated into an explicit near-field and a continuous fast-multipole far-field operator. The method allows the removal of host atoms from this representation to create vacancies or other voids without requiring a supercell creation. Analytic nuclear gradients are also implemented, enabling structural relaxation of the active subsystem. We demonstrate the versatility of the approach through applications to adsorbates on periodic surfaces and molecules embedded in bulk solvent environments. For CO on MgO(001), we show that DFT-in-DFT converges rapidly and reproduces the periodic interaction energy within 0.44 kJ/mol while reducing the cost by a factor of 27. Real-time time-dependent embedded DFT (RT-TDDFET) of methylenecyclopropene in periodic water is showcased to produce a ~0.6 eV solvatochromic blue shift, while strong-field propagation of CO2 on hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) yields substrate-induced effects at the cost of a gas-phase propagation. Coupling the embedding scheme to excited-state correlated wave function theory methods such as CC2 yields a solvent shift within 0.01 eV of supermolecular CC2 references, while reducing the computational cost by over 500 times.

</details>


### [140] [Lattice dynamical properties and interatomic force constants of transition metal oxide perovskite superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12432)
*Xiao-Lin Chen,Jin-Zhu Zhao,Philippe Ghosez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，系统分析并比较了多种钙钛矿超晶格结构（(PbTiO3)n/(SrTiO3)m、(ATiO3)n/(AO)2 及 Ruddlesden-Popper 相）的动力学性质，发现随层厚增加，钙钛矿块的动力学迅速收敛于其块体对应物，并提出了一种利用块体和短周期超晶格动力学量构建可转移模型势的直接策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效的模型势对于模拟钙钛矿异质结构至关重要，但直接为复杂层状氧化物构建精确的可转移模型势十分困难。作者希望探究能否通过传递块体系统和短周期超晶格中相关的动力学量，来构建适用于逐层增厚层状氧化物化合物的高效模型势。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，对 (PbTiO3)n/(SrTiO3)m 超晶格、(ATiO3)n/(AO)2 超晶格（A = Sr、Ba、Pb）以及 An+1TinO3n+1 Ruddlesden-Popper 相等多种钙钛矿基结构进行综合分析与比较。从倒空间的声子态密度（phonon density of states）和实空间的原子间力常数（inter-atomic force constants）两个角度，考察动力学性质随结构厚度的演化与收敛行为。

Result: 随着这些结构厚度增加，钙钛矿块的动力学性质会迅速收敛于其块体对应物的动力学性质；这一收敛趋势在倒空间（声子态密度）和实空间（原子间力常数）层面均得到清晰的体现与讨论。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过从块体和短周期超晶格中转移相关动力学量，可以构建出能准确描述层数逐渐增厚的层状氧化物化合物动力学的可转移模型势，为高效模型势的开发提供了一条简洁可行的策略。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate the construction of efficient model potentials for perovskite heterostructures by transferring relevant dynamic quantities from bulk systems and short-period superlattices. Through first-principles calculations, we provide a comprehensive analysis and comparison of the dynamical properties of different perovskite-based superlattice structures, including (PbTiO3 )n /(SrTiO3 )m superlattices, (ATiO3 )n /(AO) 2 superlattices, and An+1 Tin O 3n+1 Ruddlesden-Popper phases, where A can be Sr, Ba, or Pb. We demonstrate that as the thickness of these structures increases, the dynamical properties of the perovskite blocks converge rapidly towards their bulk counterparts. We discuss this convergence in terms of phonon density of states in reciprocal space and inter-atomic force constants in real space. Our findings suggest a straightforward strategy for developing transferable model potentials that accurately capture the dynamics of layered oxide compounds with progressively thicker layers.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum benchmarks for magnetic energies and transition temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12566)
*Mojtaba Alaei,Ilia Mikhailov,Alireza Qaiumzadeh,Artem Oganov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文通过量子蒙特卡洛基准测试，评估了预测磁性有序温度所需的两个关键条件：准确的交换作用与恰当的自旋热力学处理。结果显示DMC的能量差达到最优泛函水平，而(S+1)/S重标定可将经典-量子温度误差从81.7 K降至8.2 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测磁性有序温度需要既准确的交换作用又恰当的自旋热力学处理。现有电子结构方法在磁性能量尺度上的精度尚缺乏直接量子基准，同时经典蒙特卡洛对量子自旋热力学的处理常引入较大误差。作者旨在通过互补的量子蒙特卡洛基准系统评估这两方面的需求。

Method: 针对5种绝缘反铁磁体，将扩散蒙特卡洛（DMC）与密度泛函理论计算的铁磁-反铁磁能量差，与非弹性中子散射（INS）重建的交换作用参考值进行比较；对17种化合物，利用INS导出的海森堡哈密顿量，将经典蒙特卡洛温度与量子随机级数展开（SSE）模拟结果直接对比，并检验(S+1)/S重标定方案。

Result: DMC低估了INS参考值，但其平均绝对误差与测试中表现最优的泛函相当；(S+1)/S重标定将经典-量子温度的平均绝对差从81.7 K降至8.2 K，平均绝对百分比差为5.9%。

Conclusion: 这些结果在所测模型内为重标定建立了直接的量子基准，并强调在改进电子磁性能量尺度（交换作用精度）的同时，必须同样重视量子自旋热力学（温度修正）的处理。

Abstract: Predicting magnetic ordering temperatures requires accurate exchange interactions and an appropriate treatment of spin thermodynamics. Here we assess these requirements through complementary quantum Monte Carlo benchmarks. For five insulating antiferromagnets, we compare ferromagnetic-antiferromagnetic energy differences from diffusion Monte Carlo (DMC) and density functional theory with references reconstructed from inelastic neutron scattering (INS) exchanges. DMC underestimates these references but achieves a mean absolute error comparable to the best-performing functionals tested. Separately, we directly test the previously proposed (S+1)/S rescaling of classical Monte Carlo temperatures against quantum stochastic series expansion simulations for 17 compounds using corresponding INS-derived Heisenberg Hamiltonians. Rescaling reduces the mean absolute classical-quantum temperature difference from 81.7 to 8.2 K, with a mean absolute percentage difference of 5.9 %. These results establish a direct quantum benchmark for the rescaling within the tested models and emphasize that improvements in electronic magnetic energy scales should be accompanied by attention to quantum spin thermodynamics.

</details>


### [142] [Harmonic origin of local symmetry breaking in PbTe revealed using machine-learned potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12598)
*Seongjoo Jung,Nicola A. Spaldin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合机器学习原子间势与高阶原子间力常数模型，证明PbTe中有限程极性关联源于长程谐波相互作用而非晶格非谐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清PbTe等中心对称材料中极性纳米区/有限程极性关联的微观起源，区分其究竟由晶格非谐性还是长程谐波相互作用主导。

Method: 采用机器学习原子间势与高阶原子间力常数模型，进行分子动力学模拟并测量空间极性关联范围；通过选择性原子间力常数映射分离谐波与非谐贡献；在倒空间关联Γ点横光学(TO)声子支的高曲率；并与同构的NaCl对比。

Result: 低温下PbTe的空间极性关联范围超过50 Å且随温度升高而减小；晶格非谐性反而抑制极性关联；需延伸至13 Å的长程谐波相互作用才能再现局域偶极子的空间相干性；在倒空间这些扩展谐波相互作用对应Γ点TO支的高曲率；与NaCl对比将其归因于以库仑力为主的局域欠屏蔽（local underscreening）。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限程极性关联提供了关键的微观描述，并建立了理解中心对称材料中局域对称性破缺的框架。

Abstract: We use machine-learned interatomic potentials combined with higher-order interatomic force constant models to show that finite-range polar correlations in PbTe are driven by long-range harmonic interactions rather than lattice anharmonicity. Our molecular dynamics simulations reveal a spatial polar correlation range exceeding 50 Å at low temperatures and decreasing with increasing temperature. Selective interatomic force constant mapping demonstrates that crystal anharmonicity usually associated with the formation of polar nanoregions suppresses polar correlations. Instead, long-range harmonic interactions extending to 13 Å are necessary to reproduce the spatial coherence of local dipoles. In reciprocal space, these extended harmonic interactions correspond to high curvature of the transverse optical (TO) phonon branch at the $Γ$ point. Comparison with isostructural NaCl connects these extended interactions to local underscreening of predominantly Coulombic forces. Our results provide a key microscopic description of finite-range polar correlations and a framework for understanding local symmetry breaking in centrosymmetric materials.

</details>


### [143] [GibbScope: interactive calculation and visualization of phase diagrams in the browser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12616)
*Ramya Ramachandran,Zhuocheng Huang,Jiayang Wang,Marat I. Latypov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: GibbScope 是一个基于 Web 的框架，可在浏览器中即时计算和交互式可视化二元相图，核心 CALPHAD 引擎由 Rust 编写并编译为 WebAssembly，无需安装即可快速运算，并提供参数调整、实验数据拟合对比等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相图仍以静态图形方式传播，而 Gibbs 能量模型在持续的实验与计算数据推动下不断被拟合、修订和争议；作者希望将相图从静态图像转变为可按需交互、实时计算的活的工作对象。

Method: 开发 GibbScope 网络框架，采用 Rust 编写的全新 CALPHAD 引擎并编译为 WebAssembly，实现浏览器端零安装的快速相图重算；通过交互式参数调整、不同评估的叠加显示、与实验数据逐点偏差展示，并结合 SGTE 集合中 2745 个热力学数据库（其中 1200 余个数据库的全部相均被当前实现完整建模）来实现互动。

Result: 与成熟 CALPHAD 软件验证对比，不变温度一致性在 0.01 K 以内，多数数据库的相平衡连接线成分偏差在 1e-6 以内；GibbScope 可在零点几秒内在浏览器中重算完整相图，并支持对 SGTE 数据库的便捷访问与实时调整。

Conclusion: GibbScope 将相图与底层热力学评估转化为可交互、可实时计算的动态对象，为相图研究、验证与教学提供了新的在线工具，使相图成为持续更新的工作对象而非常规静态图表。

Abstract: Phase diagrams are a constant work in progress as Gibbs-energy models are fitted, revised, and disputed in the light of an endless stream of experimental and computational data. Yet, phase diagrams are still largely communicated as static figures, in much the same way as a century ago. We present GibbScope, a web-based framework for interactive calculation and visualization of binary phase diagrams. Written in Rust and compiled to WebAssembly, the new CALPHAD engine recomputes a complete phase diagram in the browser in a fraction of a second with zero installation on the user or server side. The high speed allows new ways of interacting with phase diagrams and underlying assessments. Validation against well-established CALPHAD software shows agreement to within 0.01 K for invariant temperatures and within 1e-6 for tie-line compositions for most databases. GibbScope offers interactive CALPHAD parameter adjustments, overlays of different assessments and point-by-point misfit to experimental data, which makes the diagram a live, working object. In addition, GibbScope provides convenient access to 2745 thermodynamic databases from the SGTE collection (Hallstedt, 2025) and their live adjustment. For over 1200 of these databases, all phases are modeled in full with the current implementation.

</details>


### [144] [Out-of-plane interference effects in the angle-resolved photoemission of epitaxial graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12733)
*E. Favorite,P. Krüger,Ph. Rosenzweig,R. Joshi,S. Williams,B. Matta,K. Küster,M. Hashimoto,U. Starke,H. Nakamura*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用光子能量依赖的同步辐射角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）研究了6H-SiC(0001)上通过Si升华外延生长的单层石墨烯/ZLG/SiC异质结构，揭示了衬底与缓冲层的三维结构如何通过光电子末态干涉影响二维石墨烯的光电子能谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层石墨烯与SiC之间的重构碳缓冲层（ZLG，零层石墨烯）及衬底的相互作用对光电子能谱测量的影响此前理解有限，除已知会产生狄拉克锥复制带外，缺乏系统认识。

Method: 采用同步辐射光源进行光子能量依赖的角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）测量，并结合多重散射模型，研究光电子在石墨烯/ZLG/SiC异质结构中的末态相互作用。

Result: 1) 在狄拉克锥光电子发射强度中识别出多个随光子能量变化的共振，归因于层间干涉，并由多重散射模型证实；2) 首次证明由(6√3×6√3)R30°重构诱导的复制带相对强度随电子动能变化；3) 分辨出四个源自ZLG的非色散电子态。

Conclusion: SiC与ZLG的三维结构会在二维外延石墨烯的光电子能谱中显现出来，本文揭示了三维衬底结构如何主导二维层的光电子发射，为利用ARPES研究复杂异质结构的电子结构建立了关键框架。

Abstract: The epitaxial growth of monolayer graphene by Si sublimation of 6H-SiC(0001) is one of the most effective ways of producing large-scale monocrystalline graphene samples of high quality. However, graphene is left interacting with a reconstructed carbon buffer layer known as zerolayer graphene (ZLG) and the SiC substrate underneath. Beyond the mere creation of Dirac cone replicas, the effect of this interaction on photoemission measurements is poorly understood. We utilized synchrotron radiation for photon-energy-dependent angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) to investigate the complex final-state interactions of photoelectrons within the heterostructure of monolayer graphene, ZLG, and SiC. We identify multiple resonances in the photoemission intensity of the Dirac cone as a function of photon energy that originate from interlayer interference, confirmed with a multiple scattering model. We also demonstrate for the first time that the relative intensity of the replica bands induced by the $(6\sqrt{3} \times 6\sqrt{3})\text{R}30^\circ$ reconstruction scales with the electron kinetic energy. Finally, we resolved four nondispersing electronic states, which originate from the ZLG. Our findings show that the three-dimensional (3D) structure of SiC and ZLG manifests itself in the photoemission from two-dimensional (2D) epitaxial graphene. By revealing how 3D substrate architecture dictates the photoemission of a 2D layer, this work establishes a crucial framework for investigating the electronic structure of complex heterostructures via ARPES.

</details>


### [145] [High Pressure Material Phase Identification with Gaussian Kernel Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12840)
*Cameron Ryan,Yasantha Pasindu Hetti Kankanamalage,Shuai Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经网络正切核(NTK)理论的高效材料物相识别方法，通过在原点周围布置"原子探测器"并对以各原子为中心的高斯函数求和作为神经网络输入，在取得与现有方法相当或更优准确率的同时实现大幅计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物相识别是依据原子位置对材料结构进行分类的计算任务，常用方法计算开销较大。作者希望借助神经机制网络理论（特别是NTK）设计一种更高效的新分类方法。

Method: 定义一组位于原点周围笛卡尔坐标处的"原子探测器"(atom detectors)，在每个探测器处计算以各原子为中心的高斯函数之和，将该求和结果作为神经网络的输入进行分类，并给出理论与实验证据支持。

Result: 实验表明该方法在准确率上达到或优于常用方法，同时在计算速度上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 基于NTK理论设计的"原子探测器+高斯求和"特征表示结合神经网络，能高效准确地完成模拟材料样本的物相识别任务。

Abstract: Phase identification is a computational task involving the classification of material structure using the positions of atoms. Using data from Molecular Dynamics (MD) simulations, our task is to identify the material phase of a set of atoms, based on how the simulations predict their positioning relative to each other. By leveraging existing research on mechanistic neural network theory, particularly Neural Tangent Kernels (NTK), we develop a new and efficient method for classifying simulated material samples.
