Our method involves defining a set of "atom detectors" that are given positions around the origin in cartesian coordinates. The sum of Gaussians centered at each atom is calculated at each atom detector, and is used as the input to a neural network. We present theoretical and experimental evidence to show that this method is highly effective and efficient at the task. Our method obtains better or similar accuracy scores to commonly used methods while having a major computational speedup.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

### [146] [Memory flows: geometry and dynamics of sequential retrieval in input-driven Hopfield networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09995)
*Simone Betteti,Matteo Martin,Morten Gram Pedersen,Sandro Zampieri,Giacomo Baggio*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双时间尺度、输入驱动的 Hopfield 网络，通过一个沿预设记忆转移图演化的慢反馈变量调制快速联想检索，从而实现自发的图约束记忆序列转换，并利用奇异摄动理论给出了记忆固定、逃逸与转换的解析判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典联想记忆模型将检索刻画为向存储模式的收敛，但顺序检索（sequential retrieval）理论仍相对匮乏；作者希望建立一个既能产生自主图约束转换、又保持解析可处理性的框架。

Method: 构建双时间尺度的输入驱动 Hopfield 网络：快速联想检索由慢反馈变量调制，该慢变量沿给定的记忆转移图演化。运用慢-快分解与几何奇异摄动理论，通过"记忆流形"刻画记忆固定，识别导致稳定性丧失与从已检索记忆逃逸的几何区域；进一步推导约化的慢动力学，并给出转换起始、自维持检索与塌缩的显式判据（以逃逸时间、增益阈值和不动点表达）。

Result: 得到了记忆固定与逃逸的几何刻画、约化慢动力学，以及转换起始、自维持检索和塌缩的显式条件，包括逃逸时间、增益阈值与不动点等形式。

Conclusion: 该工作为分析 Hopfield 网络中反馈驱动的顺序检索提供了一个可解析处理的框架，弥合了经典联想记忆与顺序记忆转换理论之间的空白。

Abstract: Associative memory models classically describe retrieval as convergence to stored patterns, but the theory of sequential retrieval remains comparatively limited. We develop a two-timescale input-driven Hopfield network in which fast associative retrieval is modulated by a slow feedback variable evolving over a prescribed memory-transition graph. This yields autonomous, graph-constrained transitions while preserving analytical tractability. Using slow-fast and geometric singular perturbation theory, we characterize memory fixation via memory manifolds and identify the geometric regions that govern stability loss and escape from retrieved memories. We further derive reduced slow dynamics and explicit criteria for transition onset, self-sustained retrieval, and collapse, expressed through escape times, gain thresholds, and fixed points. Together, these results provide a tractable framework for analyzing feedback-driven sequential retrieval in Hopfield networks.

</details>


### [147] [How Path Integral Diffusion Decides a Finite Spin Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11826)
*Michael Chertkov*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究桥扩散型 AI 生成器（PID 型）从点源采样受挫自旋玻璃时，何时以及如何做出宏观选择。核心结论是：任何各向同性 PID 都是时间变换后的高斯随机局部化，反之任意信噪比时间表都可由某个 PID 协议实现，因此生成器决定什么并不依赖协议，协议只决定何时、以何种代价决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解扩散型 AI 生成器在采样受挫自旋玻璃等复杂目标分布时，其宏观决策（如镜像选择、玻璃态类的归属）发生的时机与顺序，并厘清算法协议在多大程度上影响这些决策。

Method: 将各向同性桥扩散（PID）模型等价表述为时间变换后的高斯随机局部化；用“部分承诺”（partial commitments）——即生成器在每一时刻对目标所固定的嵌套配置类分配的概率——刻画生成器状态，证明这些部分承诺是仅沿类间对比度运动的鞅，其总体平均以平方磁化对比度决定的精确速率泄露各级信息；每个分叉近似于信噪比为 1.04/λ_P 的二元高斯信道（λ_P 为父相关矩阵的主特征值）。理论应用于 Bethe 晶格与最多 64 自旋的 Sherrington–Kirkpatrick (SK) 玻璃。

Result: 普遍观察到：轨迹首先把权重转移到零场测度两个镜像半部之一，再转移到其内部的玻璃态类；这些类在玻璃相变附近出现并在降温过程中保持身份。嵌套玻璃层级具有相近的 λ_P，因而同时被决定，轨迹在一次或两次不同事件中完成承诺——即镜像选择和一次复合玻璃态决策——二者随耦合强度和系统尺寸进一步分离，与谱预测一致。对有限步生成器，协议主要通过后期局部阶段产生影响，而非宏观决策。

Conclusion: 各向同性 PID 生成器的宏观决策内容与协议无关，协议仅决定决策时机与代价；生成器的状态可用部分承诺（鞅）完整描述，各级信息泄露速率由平方磁化对比度精确刻画；分叉行为可近似为信噪比 1.04/λ_P 的二元高斯信道。在自旋玻璃上，宏观决策表现为先镜像选择、后化合物的玻璃态决策，且决策间隔随耦合与尺寸增大。

Abstract: When an AI diffusion-type generator samples a frustrated spin glass, which macroscopic alternatives does it decide first, and when? We study this for bridge-diffusion models of the Path Integral Diffusion (PID) type started from a point source. We show that every isotropic PID is a time-changed Gaussian stochastic localization, and conversely every signal-to-noise schedule is realized by a PID protocol. What a continuous-time generator decides therefore does not depend on the protocol, which only sets when and at what cost. We describe the generator's state by its partial commitments: the probabilities it assigns, at each moment, to nested classes of configurations fixed by the target. The partial commitments are martingales that move only along class contrasts. Their ensemble average gives the information about each level at an exact rate set by squared magnetization contrasts. Each split is decided, approximately, like a binary Gaussian channel at signal-to-noise ratio 1.04/λ_P, with λ_P the leading eigenvalue of the parent's correlation matrix. The theory is applied to Bethe-lattice and Sherrington--Kirkpatrick (SK) glasses with up to 64 spins. It is observed generically that a trajectory first shifts its weight to one of the two mirror halves of the zero-field measure and then to the glassy classes inside it. These classes appear near the glass transition and keep their identity on cooling. Nested glassy levels have similar λ_P and are decided together, so a trajectory commits in one or two distinct events---the mirror choice and a compound glassy decision---which separate further with coupling and size, as the spectra predict. For a generator that takes finitely many steps, the protocol matters through the late, local stage rather than the macroscopic decisions.

</details>


### [148] [Spatial effects of Kuramoto modeling on the power-grid frequencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12132)
*Kristóf Benedek,Michelle T. Cirunay,Sangita Dutta,Attila Balogh,Bálint Hartmann,Géza Ódor*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文利用仓本模型（含/不含噪声）研究电力系统频率波动，并与匈牙利输电网实测数据对比，发现空间耦合的非线性动力学与外部驱动共同塑造观测到的频率波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网频率动力学受空间耦合与空间异质性影响，呈现复杂行为。作者旨在厘清空间结构、内在非线性动力学与外部驱动三者对频率波动的相对贡献，并检验仓本模型能否同时再现实测频谱与增量统计特征。

Method: 采集匈牙利16个输电站点一个月、100毫秒分辨率的频率数据；在387节点输电网和二维格网上模拟仓本模型（含与不含非相关噪声）。分析功率谱的标度指数、一秒频率增量的自相关与局部方差倒数的时间相关性、超统计高斯混合模型、非平稳长时间自相关指数，并与模拟结果对照。

Result: 实测功率谱呈两段标度律，指数约1.63和2.23；一秒增量去相关快，但其局部方差倒数的相关性衰减极慢，初始呈幂律 τ^{-0.26(2)} 持续至15分钟。基于局部方差的超统计高斯混合能捕捉大部分非高斯增量分布，但无法再现极端事件。低维网络上的仓本模拟给出代数衰减自相关与幂律谱，与实测及稳态维纳-辛钦关系一致；而异质输电网上的部分同步与频率锁定产生持续空间模式与多峰频率分布。然而，确定性仓本动力学或独立高斯驱动均无法同时复现实测频谱与增量统计；实测频率波动跨站点高度共享，而模拟波动主要反映节点间相对运动。

Conclusion: 实测与模拟的差异表明，空间耦合的非线性动力学（叠加外部驱动，包括15分钟电力调度）共同贡献于观测到的频率波动；单一机制不足以解释全部观测特征。

Abstract: Spatial coupling and heterogeneity can generate complex power-grid frequency dynamics. We investigate the contributions of spatial structure, intrinsic nonlinear dynamics, and external driving using the Kuramoto model (KM), with and without uncorrelated noise, and compare its predictions with real-grid measurements.
  We analyse one month of 100 ms frequency measurements from 16 Hungarian transmission-grid sites and simulate the KM on a 387-node transmission network and two-dimensional lattices. The measured power spectra exhibit two scaling regimes, with exponents of approximately 1.63 and 2.23. One-second frequency increments decorrelate rapidly, while correlations of their local inverse variance decay slowly, initially as a power law, $τ^{-0.26(2)}$, up to 15 minutes. A superstatistical Gaussian mixture based on measured local variances captures much of the non-Gaussian increment distribution, but not the rare extreme events.
  We also estimate the non-stationary long-time frequency autocorrelation exponent. KM simulations on low-dimensional networks exhibit algebraically decaying autocorrelations and power spectra, consistent with the measurements and the Wiener-Khinchin relation in the steady state. On the heterogeneous transmission network, partial synchronization and frequency locking produce persistent spatial patterns and multi-peaked frequency distributions. However, neither deterministic KM dynamics nor independent Gaussian driving reproduces the measured spectra and increment statistics simultaneously. Moreover, empirical frequency fluctuations are largely shared across substations, whereas simulated fluctuations mainly reflect relative node motion. These findings suggest that spatially coupled nonlinear dynamics contribute to observed frequency fluctuations alongside external driving, including 15-minute power dispatches.

</details>

### [149] [Markov-Shielded Typicality for Thermal Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10593)
*Atsushi Iwaki,Chisa Hotta*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性变分框架，用马尔可夫屏蔽典型态（MST）作为热平衡的纯态表示，其局域约化密度矩阵从基态到无限温极限都逼近吉布斯态，并用局域马尔可夫熵替代非局域冯·诺依曼熵，证明了MST自由能严格位于热自由能附近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热平衡下量子多体系统的典型纯态虽能再现吉布斯态的局域观测期望值，但传统典型态本质上是随机的（由高温态生成）、存在统计涨落，且无法与基态连续衔接。因此需要一个确定性的、能覆盖从基态到无限温全程的热纯态代表性构造。

Method: 提出确定性变分框架，通过将自由能最小化中的非局域冯·诺依曼熵替换为局域、可计算的马尔可夫熵，构造一个唯一的马尔可夫屏蔽典型态（MST），使其局域约化密度矩阵逼近吉布斯态，并建立MST自由能的上界与下界。

Result: MST态从基态连续延伸至无限温极限，其局域约化密度矩阵与吉布斯态高度近似；所建立的严格上下界证明MST自由能紧邻热自由能，从而把典型性概念推广到确定性的纯态热平衡表示。

Conclusion: 该框架将热平衡的典型性推广为确定性纯态表示，提供了一种可扩展的波函数方法，在经典计算与量子计算中均有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Pure states of quantum many-body systems at thermal equilibrium are known to be typical, in a sense that a randomly chosen pure state from an appropriate energy shell reproduces the Gibbs-state expectation values of most observables acting on a small subsystem. However, such typical states are intrinsically stochastic, conventionally generated from the random high-temperature state, suffer from statistical fluctuations, and do not provide a continuous connection to the ground state. Here, we introduce a deterministic variational framework for constructing a Markov-Shielded Typical (MST) state, a unique thermal pure-state representative whose local reduced density matrices closely approximate those of the Gibbs state, from the ground state up to infinite-temperature limit. Our approach reformulates free-energy minimization by replacing the nonlocal von Neumann entropy with a local, computationally tractable Markov entropy, where we establish rigorous upper and lower bounds of MST free energy to lie in the very vicinity of the thermal free energy. This framework extends the concept of typicality to deterministic pure-state representations of thermal equilibrium, offering a scalable wave-function approach with potential applications to both classical and quantum computing.

</details>


### [150] [Dynamic Scaling and Aging in Hydrodynamic Coarsening of Vapor Bubbles in a Lennard-Jones Fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10621)
*Sutapa Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文利用三维单组分Lennard-Jones流体的大规模分子动力学模拟，研究液-气相分离过程中蒸汽气泡的粗化与老化动力学，发现气泡生长遵循惯性流体动力学粗化的幂律（指数约2/3），并观察到老化现象及动力学标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究液-气相分离中蒸汽气泡这一不连通形貌的粗化与老化动力学规律，并将其与液滴生长进行对比，以揭示不同不连通形貌之间动力学机制的差异。

Method: 采用三维单组分Lennard-Jones流体的大规模分子动力学模拟；从均匀高温态温度淬火至两相共存区，使蒸汽气泡在液相背景中成核生长；测量特征气泡尺寸的幂律生长，并通过双时自相关函数C_ag(t,t_w)研究老化与动力学标度行为。

Result: 特征气泡尺寸遵循幂律增长 ℓ(t)~t^α，生长指数 α≈2/3，符合惯性流体动力学粗化特征；双时自相关函数表现出等待时间依赖的弛豫（老化）；不同等待时间的数据以长度比 ℓ(t)/ℓ(t_w) 作图时坍塌到同一标度函数，验证动力学标度；该标度函数呈代数衰减，指数 λ≈3。气泡与液滴尽管均为不连通形貌，却表现出截然不同的畴生长指数与老化指数。

Conclusion: 蒸汽气泡的粗化遵循惯性流体动力学标度（α≈2/3）并呈现老化与动力学标度（λ≈3）；不连通形貌的畴生长和老化指数并非普适，气泡与液滴之间存在本质差异。

Abstract: We investigate the coarsening and aging dynamics of vapor bubbles during liquid-vapor phase separation in a three-dimensional single-component Lennard-Jones fluid using large-scale molecular dynamics simulations. Following a temperature quench from a homogeneous high-temperature state into the two-phase coexistence region, vapor bubbles nucleate and grow in the liquid background. The characteristic bubble size follows a power-law growth, $\ell(t)\sim t^α$, with a growth exponent $α\simeq 2/3$, characteristic of inertial hydrodynamic coarsening. We also investigate the two-time autocorrelation function $C_{ag}(t,t_w)$ which follows waiting-time dependent relaxation, revealing aging. Furthermore, data at different waiting times collapse onto a scaling function when plotted as a function of the length ratio $\ell(t)/\ell(t_w)$, demonstrating dynamical scaling during aging. The corresponding scaling function exhibits an algebraic decay with an exponent $λ\simeq 3$. Interestingly, bubble and droplet growth exhibit distinctly different domain-growth and aging exponents despite both being disconnected morphologies.

</details>


### [151] [Ising model on Calandre lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10834)
*F. A. Kassan-Ogly,A. V. Zarubin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出将尚未被研究的新型平面晶格——Calandre晶格与Ising模型相结合，运用精确的Kramers–Wannier转移矩阵方法与扩展的组合式Vdovichenko–Feynman方法，严格求解了Helmholtz自由能、转移矩阵主特征值以及自发磁化强度的精确表达式，并据此系统研究了该晶格及其多种衍生晶格的热力学与磁学性质，揭示了磁阻挫、多重相变和磁重入等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Calandre晶格是一种此前未被开发利用的新型平面晶格，对其施加Ising模型的研究尚属空白。作者旨在利用精确解析方法填补这一空白，深入探索该晶格及其衍生晶格的热力学与磁学行为。

Method: 采用精确解析的Kramers–Wannier转移矩阵方法，并结合组合式Vdovichenko–Feynman方法的推广形式，严格求解了Helmholtz自由能和转移矩阵的主特征值，同时推导出自发磁化强度的精确表达式。

Result: 得到了Helmholtz自由能、Kramers–Wannier转移矩阵主特征值以及自发磁化强度的精确解析表达式；利用这些表达式对Calandre晶格及其众多衍生晶格进行了全面研究，发现了Calandre晶格与已知及若干新型晶格之间多样的关联，并揭示出磁阻挫、多重相变和磁重入等效应。

Conclusion: Calandre晶格及其衍生晶格体系展现出丰富的热力学与磁学现象，包括磁阻挫、多重相变和磁重入。所发展的精确解析方法为研究此类新型晶格提供了有力工具，并为后续探索更多关联晶格奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we proposed to consideration a noval (not yet exploited) planar lattice --- Calandre lattice with the Ising model applied to it. In the framework of an exact analytical Kramers--Wannier transfer-matrix approach and using an extension of the combinatorial Vdovichenko--Feynman method, we have obtained exact solution for Helmholtz free energy and consequent expression for the principal eigenvalue of the Kramers--Wannier transfer-matrix. In addition, we derived the exact expression for spontaneous magnetization. The usage of both expressions made it possible to perform the comprehensive studies of thermodynamic and magnetic properties of the Calandre lattice itself and a wide variety of its affiliated lattices. Among them, diversified interrelations between Calandre lattice and already known and even some novel lattices. These lattices have also revealed such effects as magnetic frustrations, multiple phase transitions and magnetic reentrancy.

</details>


### [152] [Ergotropy as distance from equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11343)
*Michele Campisi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明热孤立系统的ergotropy（可用能）等于系统能量分布与其关联被动态能量分布之间的1-Wasserstein距离，该结论对经典与量子系统均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ergotropy是衡量系统远离平衡程度的重要物理量，但其与概率分布间距离的精确数学关系此前缺乏严格形式化。作者旨在为“ergotropy量化偏离平衡的距离”这一直观认识提供严格的数学基础。

Method: 将系统状态的能量概率密度函数与其对应被动态（passive state）的能量概率密度函数进行比较，证明二者之间的1-Wasserstein距离恰好等于系统的ergotropy。证明覆盖经典系统和量子系统两种情形，并辅以具体例子加以说明。

Result: 严格证明了对热孤立系统，ergotropy（即可用能）等于系统能量分布与关联被动态能量分布之间的1-Wasserstein距离，并在经典与量子情形下均成立。

Conclusion: 该结果将ergotropy解释为能量分布间的1-Wasserstein距离，形式化地确立了“ergotropy是偏离平衡的距离度量”这一物理直觉，为理解可用能与非平衡态之间的关系提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: We show that the ergotropy (a.k.a available energy) of a thermally isolated system is the $1$-Wasserstein distance between two energy probability density functions. Specifically, between the energy profile of the system state, and the energy profile of its associated passive state. We prove this for both classical and quantum systems, and illustrate it with examples. Our result formalises the intuition that ergotropy is a quantifier of distance from equilibrium

</details>


### [153] [Computing Classical Partition Functions via Cycle-Space Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11850)
*Yoav Feigenbaum,Lev Barash,Itay Hen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过把配分函数改写为相互作用图偶子图之和来计算经典自旋系统热力学性质的方法，并基于该表示设计蒙特卡洛方案，一次采样即可获得任意温度下的零场性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典自旋系统的配分函数计算通常依赖特定温度下的采样，需多次运行才能得到温度依赖的热力学量；作者希望利用高温展开结构，把展开系数与图论对象（偶子图/零空间）联系起来，从而实现对温度无关权重的单次估计，并推广到多自旋相互作用系统。

Method: 作者从高温展开出发，将配分函数转化为相互作用图上偶子图（even subgraph）的求和，证明展开系数由二元关联矩阵在 $\mathbb{F}_2$ 上零空间的权重分布决定；对多自旋相互作用，将图替换为超图后结论逐字成立。进一步提出基于 $1/t$ Wang-Landau 算法的蒙特卡洛方案，直接在零空间中做随机游走，一次性估计该温度无关的权重分布，从而由其温度导数得到任意温度下的零场配分函数与热力学量。

Result: 在二维三角晶格 Ising 模型上与精确有限尺寸结果验证一致；成功应用于无精确解的三维、四维超立方晶格 Ising 模型，以及二、三维 Baxter-Wu 模型（三自旋相互作用超出 Pfaffian 方法适用范围）。对二体模型与能量空间中的传统 Wang-Landau 采样对比，对 Baxter-Wu 模型则与各维度最小尺寸的精确枚举结果对照验证。

Conclusion: 该方法通过图/超图零空间权重分布的单次估计，即可得到任意温度下的零场热力学性质，能有效处理含多自旋相互作用的经典自旋系统，扩展了可精确/高效计算热力学量的模型范围。

Abstract: We present a method for computing thermodynamic properties of classical spin systems by reformulating the partition function as a sum over even subgraphs of the interaction graph. Starting from the high-temperature expansion, we show that the expansion coefficients are determined by the weight distribution of the null space of the binary incidence matrix over $\mathbb{F}_2$, and that this statement holds verbatim for multi-spin interactions once the graph is replaced by a hypergraph. We introduce a Monte Carlo scheme based on the $1/t$ Wang-Landau algorithm that performs a random walk directly in this null space. Because the weight distribution is temperature independent, a single estimate yields the zero-field partition function, and the thermodynamic quantities obtained from its temperature derivatives, at arbitrary temperature. We validate the approach against exact finite-size results on the two-dimensional triangular-lattice Ising model, apply it to the three- and four-dimensional hypercubic Ising models where no exact solution is available, and to the two- and three-dimensional Baxter-Wu models, whose three-spin interactions place them outside the reach of Pfaffian methods. For the two-body models we compare against conventional Wang-Landau sampling in energy space; for the Baxter-Wu models we validate against exact enumeration at the smallest size in each dimension.

</details>


### [154] [The role of preparation protocols in the Mpemba effect under time-delayed Newton cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12328)
*Andrés Santos*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用时间延迟牛顿冷却定律研究姆潘巴效应，发现同步双储层协议在至多两次淬火且等待时间相同的协议类中最大化效应，且该最优性在异步淬火或额外淬火扩展下依然稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姆潘巴效应同时依赖于弛豫动力学和初始态的制备协议，但此前对协议选择如何影响效应发生与强度缺乏精确刻画，因而需要系统比较不同淬火协议。

Method: 采用时间延迟牛顿冷却定律建模，比较两个样品在置于共同最终热浴前经历分段恒定温度（淬火）的协议；对至多两次淬火且等待时间相同的协议导出精确解析条件，并通过数值优化研究异步淬火或额外淬火的扩展情形。

Result: 解析条件排除了该模型下的Pontus协议，并证明同步双储层协议在考察类内最大化效应；数值优化显示该最优性在异步淬火或额外淬火情形下仍然成立，同步双储层协议为最大化极限。

Conclusion: 同步双储层协议在所研究的协议类中最优，并据此推测其对更一般热浴历史仍保持最优，但这一更广泛结论尚未被证明。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect depends on both the relaxation dynamics and the protocol used to prepare the initial states. Here, this dependence is investigated using the time-delayed Newton cooling law, comparing protocols in which two samples are exposed to piecewise-constant bath temperatures before being placed in a common final bath. For protocols with at most two quenches and a common waiting time, exact conditions for the occurrence of the effect are derived. They rule out the Pontus protocol within this model and show that the synchronous two-reservoir protocol maximizes the effect magnitude within the class considered. The robustness of this optimum is then examined by allowing asynchronous quenches or an additional quench for one sample. Numerical optimization of these extensions identifies the synchronous two-reservoir protocol as the maximizing limit in the cases studied. This evidence motivates a conjecture that the same protocol remains optimal for more general bath histories, although that broader statement is not proved.

</details>


### [155] [Discrete time crystals stabilized by bounded measurement noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12400)
*Pallabi Chatterjee,Tanay Nag,Ranjan Modak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究连续弱测量能否稳定清洁相互作用系统中的离散时间晶体序。作者发现，仅靠测量诱导的面积律纠缠不足以保证序的存在；真正关键的是限制测量涨落（有界噪声），从而淘汰大偏差轨迹，使最概然轨迹展现稳健的周期倍增。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动的清洁相互作用系统通常会加热，从而破坏离散时间晶体序。作者希望探究连续弱测量是否能作为一种稳定机制，维持这种非平衡序。

Method: 研究一条长程相互作用自旋链，其局域占据被连续监测。作者比较了三类情况：典型量子轨迹、抑制测量涨落后的最概然轨迹，以及一个"有界噪声协议"（将测量涨落限制在有限窗口内）。通过调节窗口宽度，观察周期倍增（时间晶体序）的出现、共存与消失。

Result: 测量通常诱导从体积律到面积律纠缠的转变，但典型轨迹仍达到无次谐波响应的无特征态。最概然轨迹表现出稳健的周期倍增。有界噪声协议下：窄窗口保留周期倍增；中间窗口产生时间晶体与非时间晶体轨迹共存，且轨迹逐个离开时间晶体群体，序寿命有限但长久；宽窗口则破坏序。由此证明测量诱导的面积律相是序的必要而非充分条件。

Conclusion: 有界测量噪声（淘汰大偏差轨迹）为在清洁系统中实现时间晶体序提供了一条可行途径；同时表明测量诱导的面积律相是必要的，但并不足以保证该序的存在。

Abstract: Periodically driven clean interacting systems generically heat up, which destroys discrete time-crystalline order. Here we ask whether continuous weak measurement can stabilize such order. We study a long-range interacting spin chain whose local occupation is continuously monitored. Measurements generally induce a transition from volume-law to area-law entanglement. Yet low entanglement alone is not enough: along typical quantum trajectories, the system still reaches a featureless state with no subharmonic response. In contrast, the most-probable trajectory, obtained by suppressing measurement fluctuations, shows robust period doubling. To connect these limits, we introduce a bounded-noise protocol that restricts measurement fluctuations to a finite window. Narrow windows preserve period doubling. Intermediate windows produce coexisting time-crystalline and non-time-crystalline trajectories. Over time, trajectories leave the time-crystalline group one by one, so the order is long lived but finite. Wide windows destroy the order. Our results establish bounded measurement noise, discarding the large-deviation trajectories, as a route to time-crystalline order in clean systems. They also show that the measurement-induced area-law phase is necessary, but not sufficient, for this order.

</details>


### [156] [Deriving Tsallis Entropy from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12441)
*J. A. S. Lima,M. H. Benetti*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过“自下而上”的动力学方法推导q分布，进而首次从第一性原理确立了Tsallis熵公式 S_q(W)=k_B ln_q(W) 的有效性；所得q熵具有可加性，热力学第零定律自动满足，且q→1时自然回到标准结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsallis熵公式 S_q(W)=k_B ln_q(W) 此前缺乏基于第一性原理的严格推导，其在不同框架下的可加性、热力学第零定律兼容性以及统计平均的选取方式存在争议；作者希望从动力学（bottom-up）出发重新构建q熵，以弥合“自下而上”与“自上而下”两种方法的鸿沟，并澄清q分布中平均量应如何计算。

Method: 将描述q分布的动力学位形（kinetic approach）通过“自下而上”（bottom-up）的程序推广到对应的熵表达式，从而直接导出 S_q(W)=k_B ln_q(W)；在此基础上采用标准线性平均（而非q期望值）计算熵、能量等宏观量，并同时处理离散与连续情形，最后比较bottom-up与top-down两种推导所得速度分布。

Result: 由第一性原理确立了 Tsallis 公式 S_q(W)=k_B ln_q(W)；所得q熵具有可加性；由于传统关系 beta=1/(k_B T) 依然成立，热力学第零定律自动满足；“自下而上”与“自上而下”两种方法给出相同的速度q分布；离散与连续情形下的熵、能量等相关量均可由标准线性平均求得。

Conclusion: 通过“自下而上”的动力学推导可严格建立起可加、满足第零定律的q熵形式，并确认 Tsallis 公式的正确性；标准线性平均足以处理所有相关热力学量，两种推导路径自洽一致，且在 q→1 极限下自然恢复经典统计力学结果。

Abstract: The kinetic approach for q-distribution is extended through a "bottom-up" procedure to the corresponding entropic formula. In this way, the validity of Tsallis' formula, S_q(W) = k_B ln_q(W), is established from first principles. The resulting q-entropy is additive and the zeroth law of thermodynamics is automatically satisfied because the conventional identification beta = 1/(k_B T) remains valid. Entropy, energy, and all relevant quantities for both discrete and continuous cases are evaluated using standard (linear) averages. The "bottom-up" and "top-down" approaches yield the same q-distribution for velocities. The standard results are naturally recovered in the limit q -> 1.

</details>


### [157] [Giant Diffusion at Flocking Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12465)
*Sohini Chatterjee,Wasim Akram,Subir K. Das*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文通过有限尺寸标度方法模拟二维取向活性物质模型，发现在速度场有序-无序转变的临界点处，单粒子扩散系数Ds出现强烈增强，并在有序相内表现出显著的有限尺寸效应。这一非平衡簇拥转变的动力学行为与平衡系统临界性截然不同，作者将增强扩散解释为产生有效长程相互作用，从而放宽了Mermin-Wagner定理对二维连续对称性系统发生有序-无序转变的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维取向活性物质模型中速度场的有序-无序转变，旨在揭示非平衡簇拥转变临界点处粒子输运行为的异常，并阐明其与平衡系统临界现象的本质区别，从而为建立非平衡输运的定量理论提供依据。

Method: 对二维取向活性物质模型进行动力学模拟，研究其速度场的有序-无序转变；采用有限尺寸标度方法量化单粒子扩散系数Ds在临界点处的增强行为及其在有序相内的有限尺寸效应；并借助簇拥（flocking）理论框架对输运异常进行解释。

Result: 单粒子扩散系数Ds在临界点处呈现强烈增强，且在有序相内表现出显著的有限尺寸效应。这些行为与平衡系统临界点处扩散系数消失或保持非临界、以及有序相内不存在有限尺寸效应的特征根本不同。增强扩散被认为产生有效长程相互作用，从而绕过Mermin-Wagner定理的限制，使二维连续对称系统能够发生有序-无序转变。

Conclusion: 非平衡簇拥转变的动力学特性与平衡系统存在根本差异，增强扩散诱导的有效长程相互作用放宽了Mermin-Wagner定理约束。这些结果具有重要实验相关性，并对建立非平衡输运的定量理论具有深远意义。

Abstract: We simulate dynamics in two-dimensional aligning active-matter models exhibiting order-disorder transitions in the velocity field. Analysis reveals that the single-particle diffusivity, $D_s$, exhibits strong enhancements at criticality which we quantify via a finite-size scaling method. This dynamical behavior at nonequilibrium flocking transitions is fundamentally different from that at the criticality of equilibrium systems, where diffusivities either vanish or remain non-critical. Furthermore, $D_s$ exhibits pronounced finite-size effects throughout the ordered phase, which also are absent in equilibrium settings. We interpret these counterintuitive transport anomalies within the framework of a flocking theory. Our results suggest that enhanced diffusion generates effective long-range interactions. This relaxes the constraints of the Mermin-Wagner theorem that otherwise prevents order-disorder transitions in two dimensions for systems possessing continuous symmetry. These results are of much experimental relevance and have important implications in formulating quantitative theories for nonequilibrium transports.

</details>


### [158] [Holonomy, Spectral Topology, and Detailed Balance in Classical and Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12514)
*Alvan Hunt*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文综述了能带拓扑、非厄米谱理论与详细平衡之间的交叉联系，以三种和乐量（Berry相位、位错Burgers矢量、马尔可夫网络的循环亲和力）为框架，系统回答"线性生成元的谱数据能对详细平衡揭示什么"这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明线性算子的谱拓扑（复特征值、奇异点、点隙绕数）在何种程度上能够约束或排除详细平衡/热力学平衡，并厘清谱数据对耗散幅度（熵产生）的决定能力边界。

Method: 以"和乐"为主线组织教程式综合：将Berry相位、位错缺陷几何的积分挠率（Burgers矢量）、以及马尔可夫跃迁率连接ln(W_ij/W_ji)的和乐（循环亲和力）三类和乐统一起来；并通过压缩奇黏性流体的Chern数、Paulose-Chen-Vitelli位错零模计数等实例，结合Hatano-Nelson环与广义详细平衡（含时间反演宇称）进行谱分析与熵产生评估。

Result: 重新导出可压缩奇黏性流体的Chern数C_m=m(sgn Ω+sgn ν_o)，并将位错零模计数重述为积分挠率与拓扑极化的配对。证明谱拓扑可阻碍详细平衡但不决定耗散大小：非实特征值、奇异点或非零马尔可夫生成元点隙绕数均排除详细平衡并强制σ>0（且σ≥k_B j²/D）；反之实对角化谱不足以证明详细平衡。Hatano-Nelson环（正规但复谱）在固定绕数下σ可扫过(0,∞)。奇异点需结合时间反演奇偶动力学单独评估：广义详细平衡下EP仅当含时间反演奇动力学（如Langevin振子临界阻尼）时才与平衡相容；仅有偶变量系统（如奇弹性介质）的EP则证明非平衡稳态；两种情形下σ在EP处均解析。

Conclusion: 谱拓扑能够排除详细平衡并强制正熵产生，但无法定量确定耗散幅度；对奇异点须结合变量时间反演宇称做动力学评估。作者为每条结论提出了实验台上可验证的测试方案。

Abstract: Band topology and non-Hermitian spectral theory are now applied to mechanical metamaterials, chiral active fluids, stochastic networks, and open quantum systems. We give a tutorial synthesis organized by holonomy and by one question: what can the spectral data of a linear generator reveal about detailed balance? Three holonomies recur: the Berry phase of a band, the Burgers vector of a dislocation (the integrated torsion of the defect geometry), and the cycle affinity of a Markov network (the holonomy of the connection $\ln(W_{ij}/W_{ji})$). We rederive the Chern numbers $C_m=m(\mathrm{sgn}\,Ω+\mathrm{sgn}\,ν_o)$ of a compressible odd-viscous fluid and restate the dislocation zero-mode count of Paulose, Chen, and Vitelli as a pairing of integrated torsion with topological polarization. Our central message is that spectral topology can obstruct detailed balance but does not determine the magnitude of dissipation. Detailed balance is self-adjointness in the inner product fixed by the stationary state, so a non-real eigenvalue, an exceptional point (EP), or a nonzero point-gap winding number of a Markov generator rules it out and forces entropy production $σ>0$, with $σ\ge k_B j^2/D$ for any current, whereas a real, diagonalizable spectrum does not in general certify it. In the Hatano-Nelson ring, which is normal yet has complex spectrum, $σ$ sweeps $(0,\infty)$ at fixed winding number. EPs need a separate assessment of the dynamics: with generalized detailed balance including time-reversal parity, an EP is compatible with equilibrium only through time-reversal-odd dynamics, as at critical damping of a Langevin oscillator, whereas an EP of a system with only even variables, such as an odd elastic medium, certifies a nonequilibrium steady state; in both cases $σ$ is analytic across the EP. We propose benchtop tests for each statement.

</details>


### [159] [Memory in biological Lévy walks from the dynamic-entropy formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12547)
*Adriano Barra,Andrea Ladiana*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于动态熵（因果熵最大化）的形式化方法，用于检验 Lévy 步程中的“更新假设”（即每一步飞行长度独立重新抽取），并将其推广到群体运动的记忆、延迟互动与共同驱动源的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教科书上的 Lévy 步程模型依赖更新假设（每条飞行长度独立重抽），但这一假设很少被真正检验。作者希望用一个数据可判定“记忆深度”的框架，区分更新行走者、马尔可夫行走者和更长记忆行走者，并进一步厘清群体中个体记忆、延迟交互与共同驱动源的贡献，以纠正仅用群体平均带来的混淆。

Method: 采用动态熵形式化：在固定滞后相关（lagged correlations）的约束下最大化对数飞行长度序列的因果熵，得到一个定义在紧区间上的连续自旋链，其“深度”由数据选择，从而区分不同记忆阶。单滞后情形可精确求解。利用交换长短飞行的对偶性，定位飞行在上截断处的一阶凝聚相变（对数飞行范围为“系统尺寸”）；正记忆使直方图比生成规则更重尾。群体层面，将同一构造扩展以分离个体记忆、延迟交互与全体单元的公共驱动源，并揭示集体速度的临界点。

Result: 在开放数据上：果蝇幼虫的 10 个实验条件中有 7 个拒绝更新假设；所有白蚁个体均被拒绝更新，其记忆回溯数步，并使直方图指数相对单步规则下降超过 0.5 单位。野生狒狒的转向与其最近邻（尤其是前方个体）10–30 秒前的朝向对齐；归巢鸽在小群体内存在耦合。斑马鱼 T 细胞作为负对照只记住自身速度，细胞间无延迟耦合通过对照检验。

Conclusion: 该动态熵框架能可靠判定 Lévy 行为背后的记忆深度并拒绝更新假设，且可推广到多体系统，有效区分个体记忆、延迟交互与共同驱动力，为行为数据（从单一个体到群体）提供统一的因果/记忆分析工具。

Abstract: Lévy walks are recognized by the histogram of their flight lengths, while the renewal hypothesis on which the textbook model rests, that every flight is drawn afresh, is seldom tested. We test it with the dynamic-entropy formalism. Maximizing the causal entropy of a sequence of logarithmic flight lengths at fixed lagged correlations yields a chain of continuous spins on a compact interval, whose depth, selected by the data, tells a renewal walker from a Markovian one and from one with a longer memory. With one lag the chain is exactly solvable. A duality that exchanges long and short flights locates a first-order condensation of the flights at the upper cutoff, with the logarithmic range of the flights in the role of system size, and a positive memory makes the histogram heavier-tailed than the rule that generates each flight. For groups, the same construction separates the memory of each individual from delayed interactions and from drives common to all units, which an analysis of the group average confounds, and it gives the collective speed a critical point. On open data, renewal is rejected in seven of ten conditions of Drosophila larvae and in every termite, whose memory reaches a few flights back and lowers the exponent of the histogram by more than half a unit below that of the one-step rule. Wild baboons align with where their nearest neighbors, those ahead above all, were heading 10 to 30 s earlier, and homing pigeons are coupled across their small flocks. Zebrafish T cells, a negative control, remember their own speed, but no delayed coupling between the speeds of different cells survives the controls.

</details>


### [160] [Resolving the backbone of multiscale interactions through mutual information rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12554)
*Alberto Raiola,Giorgio Nicoletti,Daniel Maria Busiello*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用多时间尺度耦合重建多尺度交互树（网络结构）的框架，通过重标定的互信息率及其条件形式，结合时间尺度排序，能区分直接相连过程并辨别快-慢与慢-快耦合方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然系统常由多时间尺度过程通过复杂交互网络耦合而成，但当时间尺度差异悬殊时，解析最快过程的同时精确采样最慢过程通常代价极高，导致网络推断极具挑战。作者希望将多时间尺度的存在由推断障碍转化为结构可辨识性的来源。

Method: 提出重建多尺度交互树的框架，方法包括：对互信息率进行重标定（rescaled version），引入其条件对应（conditional mutual information rate），并结合时间尺度排序进行推断。针对平稳高斯过程导出精确理论，证明这些测度能识别直接相连过程；其中渐近互信息率可区分快到慢与慢到快耦合。重建可基于谱矩阵实现。

Result: 理论证明在平稳高斯过程下，所提测度能准确识别直接连接的过程，并通过渐近互信息率区分快-慢耦合与慢-快耦合方向。数值实验表明该方法对有限轨迹长度、中等程度时间尺度分离以及弱非线性均具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用真实系统中存在的多时间尺度作为结构辨识的来源（而非网络推断的障碍）铺平了道路，为多尺度耦合网络的推断提供了新理论框架与可行方法。

Abstract: Natural systems are often composed of processes evolving on multiple timescales and coupled through complex interaction networks. Inferring such networks is especially challenging when timescales are widely separated, since resolving the fastest processes while accurately sampling the slowest ones is generally prohibitive. In this work, we propose a framework to reconstruct a multiscale interaction tree by combining a rescaled version of the mutual information rate, its conditional counterpart, and the ordering of timescales. We derive an exact theory for stationary Gaussian processes, finding that these measures identify directly connected processes, with the asymptotic mutual information rate distinguishing fast-to-slow from slow-to-fast couplings. We show that this reconstruction can be achieved from the spectral matrix and illustrate its robustness to finite trajectory lengths, moderate timescale separation, and weak nonlinearities. Our work paves the way for exploiting the presence of multiple timescales in real-world systems as a source of structural identifiability rather than an obstacle to network inference.

</details>


### [161] [A Hierarchy of Fast and Strong Signatures for Vertex Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12653)
*Joe Germany,Mohamad Al Sayed,Rana Dbouk,Amer E. Mouawad,Sara Najem*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一系列基于图拉普拉斯算子和Dirac算子谱性质的、自同构不变且表达能力逐步增强的签名，并引入“探测”（临时结构扰动）技术，以揭示图顶点之间看似相似但不可互换的细微结构差异，从而逼近精确的顶点轨道划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图的对称性体现为自同构，不变量体现为顶点轨道。关键难题在于：两个顶点在结构上看似不可区分，却未必在任何图自同构下可互换。作者的目标是区分“表面相似性”与“真实对称性”，解决顶点轨道划分问题。

Method: 作者构建了一个表达能力逐级增强的、自同构不变的签名层次体系，利用图拉普拉斯算子和Dirac算子的谱特征；在一系列反例的引导下不断强化签名，最终引入“探测”技术（临时性结构扰动），通过扰动后的信息暴露原始未扰动图中隐藏的结构差异；并在多种基准图族上评估，与低维Weisfeiler–Leman细化进行对照。

Result: 该方法在多个基准图族上恢复了精确的轨道划分，包括一些能抵抗低维Weisfeiler–Leman细化的困难实例；同时作者也考察了该方法在计算与数值上的局限，区分了经验上的成功与理论上的完备性。

Conclusion: 研究彰显了基于谱方法和探测技术的方法在揭示定义顶点轨道的结构差异方面的潜力，尽管这些构造尚不具备理论完备性，但在困难实例中表现出色，为顶点轨道划分问题提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: The defining features of a physical system are its symmetries and its invariants. For a network, the symmetries are the automorphisms, and the invariants are the vertex orbits they define. Two vertices of a graph may appear structurally indistinguishable without being interchangeable under any graph automorphism. Distinguishing apparent similarity from genuine symmetry lies at the heart of the vertex-orbit partition problem. In this work, we develop a hierarchy of increasingly expressive, automorphism-invariant signatures that draw on the spectral properties of the graph Laplacian and the Dirac operator to reveal subtle structural differences. Guided by a sequence of counterexamples, we progressively strengthen these signatures, ultimately incorporating probing techniques, which are temporary structural perturbations, that expose the hidden distinctions in the original unperturbed graph. We evaluate our approach across diverse benchmark families, recovering exact orbit partitions in challenging instances, including graphs that resist low-dimensional Weisfeiler--Leman refinement. We also examine the computational and numerical limitations of our constructions, distinguishing empirical success from theoretical completeness. Our findings highlight the potential of spectral and probing-based methods for uncovering the structural distinctions that define vertex orbit.

</details>


### [162] [Renewal Bias in Parallel Simulations of Absorbing Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12692)
*Sandro M. Reia,José F. Fontanari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 异步并行模拟含吸收态的随机过程时，若轨迹时长与最终吸收结果相关，会产生系统性采样偏差；本文用更新理论精确刻画该偏差，并提出"首轨迹过滤"策略以指数速度压制偏差，指出提高吞吐量未必提升统计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行工作者在固定计算预算下不断重启吸收轨迹，会优先记录快速终止的轨迹，扭曲观测到的系综统计；作者旨在揭示这一采样偏差的机制、量化其规模，并提出改进的采样策略。

Method: 采用含两个吸收态、状态依赖指数时长的最小更新模型，用更新理论推导完成轨迹期望数量与组成的精确表达式；比较连续采样与"首轨迹过滤"(First-Trajectory Filtering)两种策略，并通过均方误差分析确定预算依赖的交叉工作者分配；进一步研究严重删失下未知吸收概率与特征时长的统计推断局限，并在 Axelrod 模型的非平衡相变附近做仿真验证。

Result: 连续采样偏差随时长呈代数式下降，但在固定预算下随工作者数增加仍持续存在；首轨迹过滤虽因有限截止时间引入残余删失偏差，但该偏差随计算预算呈指数式衰减；存在预算依赖的交叉工作者数，超过该数后首轨迹过滤虽采样轨迹更少却更准确；Axelrod 模型仿真证实了快速吸收的碎片化构型被优先采样。

Conclusion: 提高计算吞吐量并不必然提升统计精度；在含吸收过程的并行模拟中必须考虑轨迹时长的异质性，首轨迹过滤等方法有助于以更少轨迹获得更准确的统计结果。

Abstract: Asynchronous parallel simulations of stochastic processes with absorbing states can exhibit systematic sampling bias when trajectory durations depend on their eventual absorbing outcomes. Workers that continuously restart upon absorption preferentially record rapidly terminating trajectories within a fixed computational budget, distorting the observed ensemble statistics. We investigate this effect using a minimal renewal model with two absorbing states and state-dependent exponential durations. Renewal theory yields exact expressions for the expected number and composition of completed trajectories, showing that the resulting bias decreases algebraically with execution time but persists as the number of parallel workers increases at fixed budget. We compare continuous sampling with First-Trajectory Filtering, which retains only the initial trajectory attempted by each worker. Although finite execution deadlines introduce residual censoring bias, this bias decreases exponentially with the computational budget. An analytical mean-squared error calculation reveals budget-dependent crossover worker allocations beyond which First-Trajectory Filtering becomes more accurate despite collecting fewer trajectories. We also examine the limitations of statistical inference under severe censoring when the absorbing-state probabilities and characteristic durations are unknown. Finally, simulations of the Axelrod model near its nonequilibrium transition demonstrate the preferential sampling of rapidly absorbing fragmented configurations, illustrating the relevance of the renewal mechanism in a spatially interacting system. Our results show that increasing computational throughput does not necessarily improve statistical accuracy and highlight the importance of accounting for trajectory-duration heterogeneity in parallel simulations of absorbing processes.

</details>
